CREATE FUNCTION DESCRICAO_TIPO 
(@Tipo VARCHAR(10))  
RETURNS VARCHAR(30) 
AS  
BEGIN 
	DECLARE @Descricao VARCHAR(30)
	SELECT @Descricao = 
		CASE UPPER(SUBSTRING(@Tipo,1,3))
			WHEN 'ISS' THEN 'Issues'
			WHEN 'ACT' THEN 'Actions'
			WHEN 'RSK' THEN 'Risks'
			WHEN 'CHR' THEN 'Chang_Requests'
			WHEN 'TSK' THEN 'Tasks'
		END
	RETURN(@Descricao)
 
END




GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS OFF 
GO

CREATE FUNCTION RetornaBCWS
(
@Equipe VARCHAR(50)
)  

RETURNS DECIMAL(25,6)
AS 
BEGIN 

DECLARE @RetBCWS  DECIMAL(25,6)

SELECT @RetBCWS = Sum(M.TASK_BCWS)
FROM MSP_TASKS M 
	left outer join DADOSEQUIPE D on 
	D.TEXT_REF_UID = M.TASK_UID AND D.PROJ_ID = M.PROJ_ID
WHERE M.TASK_IS_SUMMARY = 0 
	AND M.PROJ_ID = 1
	AND D.TEXT_VALUE =  @Equipe
	AND M.TASK_BCWS<>M.TASK_ACT_COST
	AND M.TASK_BCWS <> 0
	AND M.TASK_BCWS>M.TASK_BCWP

RETURN(@RetBCWS)
END








GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS OFF 
GO

CREATE FUNCTION RetornaBCWP
(
@Equipe VARCHAR(50)
)  

RETURNS DECIMAL(25,6)
AS 
BEGIN 

DECLARE @RetBCWP DECIMAL(25,6)

SELECT @RetBCWP = Sum(M.TASK_BCWP)
FROM MSP_TASKS M 
	left outer join DADOSEQUIPE D on 
	D.TEXT_REF_UID = M.TASK_UID AND D.PROJ_ID = M.PROJ_ID
WHERE M.TASK_IS_SUMMARY = 0 
	AND M.PROJ_ID = 1
	AND D.TEXT_VALUE =  @Equipe
	AND M.TASK_BCWS<>M.TASK_ACT_COST
	AND M.TASK_BCWS <> 0
	AND M.TASK_BCWS>M.TASK_BCWP

RETURN(@RetBCWP)
END











GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS OFF 
GO

CREATE FUNCTION RetornaPetrobras
()
RETURNS VARCHAR(50)
AS 
BEGIN 

DECLARE @RetPetro VARCHAR(50)

SELECT @RetPetro =  M.TASK_OUTLINE_NUM
FROM MSP_TASKS M 
WHERE M.TASK_IS_SUMMARY =1
	AND M.TASK_NAME = 'Saneamento para atender Go-Live Petrobras'
	AND M.PROJ_ID=2
Set @RetPetro = @RetPetro+ '.%'
RETURN(@RetPetro)
END





GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS OFF 
GO


CREATE FUNCTION RetornaUsuario 
(
@Workflow VARCHAR(50),
@Usuario VARCHAR(50),
@Identificador VARCHAR(50),
@Designador VARCHAR(50),
@Responsavel VARCHAR(50),
@Revisor VARCHAR(50),
@Aprovador VARCHAR(50)
)  
RETURNS VARCHAR(50)
AS  
BEGIN 
	DECLARE @RetUsuario VARCHAR(50)


IF Upper(RTrim(LTrim(@Usuario))) = 'MULTIPLE USERS'
BEGIN

	Set @RetUsuario = CASE Upper(RTrim(LTrim(@Workflow)))
	         WHEN 'IDENTIFICADOR' THEN  Upper(RTrim(LTrim(SUBSTRING(@Identificador,1,PATINDEX ( '%,%' , @Identificador) - 1))))
	         WHEN 'DESIGNADOR' THEN  Upper(RTrim(LTrim(SUBSTRING(@Designador,1,PATINDEX ( '%,%' , @Designador) - 1))))
	         WHEN 'RESPONSÁVEL' THEN  Upper(RTrim(LTrim(SUBSTRING(@Responsavel,1,PATINDEX ( '%,%' , @Responsavel) - 1))))
	         WHEN 'REVISOR' THEN  Upper(RTrim(LTrim(SUBSTRING(@Revisor,1,PATINDEX ( '%,%' , @Revisor) - 1))))
	         WHEN 'APROVADOR' THEN  Upper(RTrim(LTrim(SUBSTRING(@Aprovador,1,PATINDEX ( '%,%' , @Aprovador) - 1))))
	      END
END
ELSE
BEGIN
	IF Upper(RTrim(LTrim(@Usuario))) = ''
	BEGIN
	Set @RetUsuario = CASE Upper(RTrim(LTrim(@Workflow)))
	         WHEN 'IDENTIFICADOR' THEN  Upper(RTrim(LTrim(@Identificador)))
	         WHEN 'DESIGNADOR' THEN  Upper(RTrim(LTrim(@Designador)))
	         WHEN 'RESPONSÁVEL' THEN  Upper(RTrim(LTrim(@Responsavel)))
	         WHEN 'REVISOR' THEN  Upper(RTrim(LTrim(@Revisor)))
	         WHEN 'APROVADOR' THEN  Upper(RTrim(LTrim(@Aprovador)))
	      END
	END
	ELSE
	BEGIN
		Set @RetUsuario = @Usuario
	END
END

RETURN(@RetUsuario)

 
END






GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS OFF 
GO





CREATE FUNCTION ScoreCardAndamento 
(
@Cod_Equipe NUMERIC(9),
@Criticidade VARCHAR(20),
@Data DATETIME
)  
RETURNS INT
AS  
BEGIN 
	DECLARE @Andamento INT


IF RTrim(LTrim(@Criticidade)) = '--Nao Definido--'
BEGIN

	Select @Andamento = Count(*) From Issues
	Where Equipe = @Cod_Equipe AND
		(UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(Criticidade_Impacto))) = '--Nao Definido--' OR UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(Criticidade_Impacto))) = '--Não Definido--') AND
		Data_Limite >= CONVERT(VARCHAR(8),@Data,112) AND
		(UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(Overall_Status))) = 'OPEN' OR 
		UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(Overall_Status))) = 'PAST DUE')
	
END
ELSE
BEGIN

	Select @Andamento = Count(*) From Issues
	Where  Equipe = @Cod_Equipe AND
		UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(Criticidade_Impacto))) = UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(@Criticidade))) AND
		Data_Limite >= CONVERT(VARCHAR(8),@Data,112) AND
		(UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(Overall_Status))) = 'OPEN' OR 
		UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(Overall_Status))) = 'PAST DUE')

END

	RETURN(@Andamento)

 
END












GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO


CREATE FUNCTION ScoreCardAndamentoRisks 
(
@Cod_Equipe NUMERIC(9),
@Criticidade VARCHAR(20),
@Data DATETIME
)  
RETURNS INT
AS  
BEGIN 
	DECLARE @Andamento INT


IF RTrim(LTrim(@Criticidade)) = '--Nao Definido--'
BEGIN

	Select @Andamento = Count(*) From Risks
	Where Equipe = @Cod_Equipe AND
		(UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(Impacto))) = '--Nao Definido--' OR UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(Impacto))) = '--Não Definido--') AND
		Data_Limite >= CONVERT(VARCHAR(8),@Data,112) AND
		(UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(Overall_Status))) = 'OPEN' OR 
		UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(Overall_Status))) = 'PAST DUE')
	
END
ELSE
BEGIN

	Select @Andamento = Count(*) From Risks
	Where  Equipe = @Cod_Equipe AND
		UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(Impacto))) = UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(@Criticidade))) AND
		Data_Limite >= CONVERT(VARCHAR(8),@Data,112) AND
		(UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(Overall_Status))) = 'OPEN' OR 
		UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(Overall_Status))) = 'PAST DUE')

END

	RETURN(@Andamento)

 
END



GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS OFF 
GO

)  
RETURNS INT
AS

	DECLARE @Ult_Data DATETIME

	Select @Ult_Data = max(Data_Score)
	from Scorecard

	BEGIN
		SET @Andamento = 1
	END
	ELSE

		SET @Andamento = 0
	END

	RETURN(@Andamento)
 
END

CREATE FUNCTION ScoreCardAtrasadas10 
(
@Cod_Equipe NUMERIC(9),
@Criticidade VARCHAR(20),
@Data DATETIME
)  
RETURNS INT
AS  
BEGIN 
	DECLARE @Andamento INT

IF RTrim(LTrim(@Criticidade)) = '--Nao Definido--'
BEGIN

	Select @Andamento = Count(*) From Issues
	Where  Equipe = @Cod_Equipe 
		AND (UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(Criticidade_Impacto))) = '--Nao Definido--' OR UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(Criticidade_Impacto))) = '--Não Definido--')
		AND Data_Limite >= CONVERT(VARCHAR(8),(DATEADD(d, -10, @Data)),112) 
		AND Data_Limite < CONVERT(VARCHAR(8),@Data,112)
		AND (UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(Overall_Status))) = 'OPEN' OR 
		UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(Overall_Status))) = 'PAST DUE')


END
ELSE
BEGIN

	Select @Andamento = Count(*) From Issues
	Where  Equipe = @Cod_Equipe 
		AND UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(Criticidade_Impacto))) = UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(@Criticidade)))
		AND Data_Limite >= CONVERT(VARCHAR(8),(DATEADD(d, -10, @Data)),112) 
		AND Data_Limite < CONVERT(VARCHAR(8),@Data,112)
		AND (UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(Overall_Status))) = 'OPEN' OR 
		UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(Overall_Status))) = 'PAST DUE')


END
	
	RETURN(@Andamento)
 
END












GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO


CREATE FUNCTION ScoreCardAtrasadas10Risks
(
@Cod_Equipe NUMERIC(9),
@Criticidade VARCHAR(20),
@Data DATETIME
)  
RETURNS INT
AS  
BEGIN 
	DECLARE @Andamento INT

IF RTrim(LTrim(@Criticidade)) = '--Nao Definido--'
BEGIN

	Select @Andamento = Count(*) From Risks
	Where  Equipe = @Cod_Equipe 
		AND (UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(Impacto))) = '--Nao Definido--' OR UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(Impacto))) = '--Não Definido--')
		AND Data_Limite >= CONVERT(VARCHAR(8),(DATEADD(d, -10, @Data)),112) 
		AND Data_Limite < CONVERT(VARCHAR(8),@Data,112)
		AND (UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(Overall_Status))) = 'OPEN' OR 
		UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(Overall_Status))) = 'PAST DUE')


END
ELSE
BEGIN

	Select @Andamento = Count(*) From Risks
	Where  Equipe = @Cod_Equipe 
		AND UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(Impacto))) = UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(@Criticidade)))
		AND Data_Limite >= CONVERT(VARCHAR(8),(DATEADD(d, -10, @Data)),112) 
		AND Data_Limite < CONVERT(VARCHAR(8),@Data,112)
		AND (UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(Overall_Status))) = 'OPEN' OR 
		UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(Overall_Status))) = 'PAST DUE')


END
	
	RETURN(@Andamento)

	[Id_Origem] [varchar] (10) COLLATE SQL_Latin1_General_CP1_CI_AS NOT NULL ,
	[Cod_Proj_Destino] [varchar] (25) COLLATE SQL_Latin1_General_CP1_CI_AS NOT NULL ,
	[Id_Destino] [varchar] (10) COLLATE SQL_Latin1_General_CP1_CI_AS NOT NULL 
) ON [PRIMARY]
GO

CREATE TABLE [dbo].[Unidades] (
	[Cod_Unidade] [numeric](18, 0) IDENTITY (1, 1) NOT NULL ,
	[Desc_Unidade] [varchar] (50) COLLATE SQL_Latin1_General_CP1_CI_AS NULL 
) ON [PRIMARY]
GO

CREATE TABLE [dbo].[Usuarios] (
	[Login] [varchar] (10) COLLATE SQL_Latin1_General_CP1_CI_AS NOT NULL ,
	[Nome_Usuario] [varchar] (100) COLLATE SQL_Latin1_General_CP1_CI_AS NULL ,
	[Senha] [varchar] (10) COLLATE SQL_Latin1_General_CP1_CI_AS NULL ,
	[Cod_Unidade] [numeric](18, 0) NULL ,
	[Integrador] [varchar] (50) COLLATE Latin1_General_CI_AS NULL ,
	[Flag_CLI] [bit] NULL 
) ON [PRIMARY]
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

CREATE VIEW dbo.Scorecard_MAT1
AS
SELECT     Processo = 'MES', a.Aberto, b.Andamento AS 'Em andamento', c.Fechado, d.Total
	FROM         (SELECT     COUNT(R .Name) AS Aberto
		      FROM          Cursos_Replan R
                      WHERE (R .PercComp = '0') AND R .CodCurso LIKE 'MES%') a,

	     (SELECT     COUNT(R .Name) AS Andamento
		      FROM          Cursos_Replan R
                      WHERE (R .PercComp > '0') AND (R .PercComp < '100') AND (R .CodCurso LIKE 'MES%')) b,

	       (SELECT     COUNT(R .Name) AS Fechado
		      FROM          Cursos_Replan R
                      WHERE (R .PercComp = '100') AND (R .CodCurso LIKE 'MES%')) c,
		(SELECT     COUNT(R .Name) AS Total
		      FROM          Cursos_Replan R
                      WHERE (R .CodCurso LIKE 'MES%')) d

UNION ALL        
SELECT     Processo = 'FI', a.Aberto, b.Andamento AS 'Em andamento', c.Fechado,d.Total
	FROM         (SELECT     COUNT(R .Name) AS Aberto
		      FROM          Cursos_Replan R
                      WHERE (R .PercComp = '0') AND R .CodCurso LIKE 'FIN%') a,

	     (SELECT     COUNT(R .Name) AS Andamento
		      FROM          Cursos_Replan R
                      WHERE (R .PercComp > '0') AND (R .PercComp < '100') AND (R .CodCurso LIKE 'FIN%')) b,

	       (SELECT     COUNT(R .Name) AS Fechado
		      FROM          Cursos_Replan R
                      WHERE (R .PercComp = '100') AND (R .CodCurso LIKE 'FIN%')) c,
		(SELECT     COUNT(R .Name) AS Total
		      FROM          Cursos_Replan R
                      WHERE (R .CodCurso LIKE 'FIN%')) d

UNION ALL       

SELECT     Processo = 'PC', a.Aberto, b.Andamento AS 'Em andamento', c.Fechado, d.Total
	FROM         (SELECT     COUNT(R .Name) AS Aberto
		      FROM          Cursos_Replan R
                      WHERE (R .PercComp = '0') AND R .CodCurso LIKE 'PLC%') a,

	     (SELECT     COUNT(R .Name) AS Andamento
		      FROM          Cursos_Replan R
                      WHERE (R .PercComp > '0') AND (R .PercComp < '100') AND (R .CodCurso LIKE 'PLC%')) b,

	       (SELECT     COUNT(R .Name) AS Fechado
		      FROM          Cursos_Replan R
                      WHERE (R .PercComp = '100') AND (R .CodCurso LIKE 'PLC%')) c,
		(SELECT     COUNT(R .Name) AS Total
		      FROM          Cursos_Replan R
                      WHERE (R .CodCurso LIKE 'PLC%')) d

UNION ALL   
SELECT     Processo = 'IS', a.Aberto, b.Andamento AS 'Em andamento', c.Fechado, d.Total
	FROM         (SELECT     COUNT(R .Name) AS Aberto
		      FROM          Cursos_Replan R
                      WHERE (R .PercComp = '0') AND R .CodCurso LIKE 'SUP%') a,

	     (SELECT     COUNT(R .Name) AS Andamento
		      FROM          Cursos_Replan R
                      WHERE (R .PercComp > '0') AND (R .PercComp < '100') AND (R .CodCurso LIKE 'SUP%')) b,

	       (SELECT     COUNT(R .Name) AS Fechado
		      FROM          Cursos_Replan R
                      WHERE (R .PercComp = '100') AND (R .CodCurso LIKE 'SUP%')) c,
           (SELECT     COUNT(R .Name) AS Total
		      FROM          Cursos_Replan R
                      WHERE (R .CodCurso LIKE 'SUP%')) d

UNION ALL
                                          
SELECT     Processo = 'SD', a.Aberto, b.Andamento AS 'Em andamento', c.Fechado, d. Total 
	FROM         (SELECT     COUNT(R .Name) AS Aberto
		      FROM          Cursos_Replan R
                      WHERE (R .PercComp = '0') AND R .CodCurso LIKE 'COM%') a,

	     (SELECT     COUNT(R .Name) AS Andamento
		      FROM          Cursos_Replan R
                      WHERE (R .PercComp > '0') AND (R .PercComp < '100') AND (R .CodCurso LIKE 'COM%')) b,

	       (SELECT     COUNT(R .Name) AS Fechado
		      FROM          Cursos_Replan R
                      WHERE (R .PercComp = '100') AND (R .CodCurso LIKE 'COM%')) c,
		
               (SELECT     COUNT(R .Name) AS Total
		      FROM          Cursos_Replan R
                      WHERE (R .CodCurso LIKE 'COM%')) d

UNION ALL  
SELECT     Processo = 'PS', a.Aberto, b.Andamento AS 'Em andamento', c.Fechado, d.Total 
	FROM         (SELECT     COUNT(R .Name) AS Aberto
		      FROM          Cursos_Replan R
                      WHERE (R .PercComp = '0') AND R .CodCurso LIKE 'EMP%') a,

	     (SELECT     COUNT(R .Name) AS Andamento
		      FROM          Cursos_Replan R
                      WHERE (R .PercComp > '0') AND (R .PercComp < '100') AND (R .CodCurso LIKE 'EMP%')) b,

	       (SELECT     COUNT(R .Name) AS Fechado
		      FROM          Cursos_Replan R
                      WHERE (R .PercComp = '100') AND (R .CodCurso LIKE 'EMP%')) c,
		(SELECT     COUNT(R .Name) AS Total
		      FROM          Cursos_Replan R
                      WHERE (R .CodCurso LIKE 'EMP%')) d

UNION ALL                                                                                                           
                                                  
SELECT     Processo = 'LT', a.Aberto, b.Andamento AS 'Em andamento', c.Fechado, d.Total
	FROM         (SELECT     COUNT(R .Name) AS Aberto
		      FROM          Cursos_Replan R
                      WHERE (R .PercComp = '0') AND R .CodCurso LIKE 'LTE%') a,

	     (SELECT     COUNT(R .Name) AS Andamento
		      FROM          Cursos_Replan R
                      WHERE (R .PercComp > '0') AND (R .PercComp < '100') AND (R .CodCurso LIKE 'LTE%')) b,

	       (SELECT     COUNT(R .Name) AS Fechado
		      FROM          Cursos_Replan R
                      WHERE (R .PercComp = '100') AND (R .CodCurso LIKE 'LTE%')) c,
		(SELECT     COUNT(R .Name) AS Total
		      FROM          Cursos_Replan R
                      WHERE (R .CodCurso LIKE 'LTE%')) d

UNION ALL                                                              
SELECT     Processo = 'PM', a.Aberto, b.Andamento AS 'Em andamento', c.Fechado,d.Total
	FROM         (SELECT     COUNT(R .Name) AS Aberto
		      FROM          Cursos_Replan R
                      WHERE (R .PercComp = '0') AND R .CodCurso LIKE 'MAN%') a,

	     (SELECT     COUNT(R .Name) AS Andamento
		      FROM          Cursos_Replan R
                      WHERE (R .PercComp > '0') AND (R .PercComp < '100') AND (R .CodCurso LIKE 'MAN%')) b,

	       (SELECT     COUNT(R .Name) AS Fechado
		      FROM          Cursos_Replan R
                      WHERE (R .PercComp = '100') AND (R .CodCurso LIKE 'MAN%')) c,
		(SELECT     COUNT(R .Name) AS Total
		      FROM          Cursos_Replan R
                      WHERE (R .CodCurso LIKE 'MAN%')) d

UNION ALL                                                              
                 
                                                           
SELECT     Processo = 'PO', a.Aberto, b.Andamento AS 'Em andamento', c.Fechado, d.Total
	FROM         (SELECT     COUNT(R .Name) AS Aberto
		      FROM          Cursos_Replan R
                      WHERE (R .PercComp = '0') AND R .CodCurso LIKE 'POS%') a,

	     (SELECT     COUNT(R .Name) AS Andamento
		      FROM          Cursos_Replan R
                      WHERE (R .PercComp > '0') AND (R .PercComp < '100') AND (R .CodCurso LIKE 'POS%')) b,

	       (SELECT     COUNT(R .Name) AS Fechado
		      FROM          Cursos_Replan R
                      WHERE (R .PercComp = '100') AND (R .CodCurso LIKE 'POS%')) c,
                (SELECT     COUNT(R .Name) AS Total
		      FROM          Cursos_Replan R
                      WHERE (R .CodCurso LIKE 'POS%')) d

UNION ALL                                                              
SELECT     Processo = 'PP', a.Aberto, b.Andamento AS 'Em andamento', c.Fechado, d.Total
	FROM         (SELECT     COUNT(R .Name) AS Aberto
		      FROM          Cursos_Replan R
                      WHERE (R .PercComp = '0') AND R .CodCurso LIKE 'PRD%') a,

	     (SELECT     COUNT(R .Name) AS Andamento
		      FROM          Cursos_Replan R
                      WHERE (R .PercComp > '0') AND (R .PercComp < '100') AND (R .CodCurso LIKE 'PRD%')) b,

	       (SELECT     COUNT(R .Name) AS Fechado
		      FROM          Cursos_Replan R
                      WHERE (R .PercComp = '100') AND (R .CodCurso LIKE 'PRD%')) c,
                (SELECT     COUNT(R .Name) AS Total
		      FROM          Cursos_Replan R
                      WHERE (R .CodCurso LIKE 'PRD%')) d



GO

CREATE VIEW dbo.Scorecard_MES
AS


SELECT     Processo = 'QM', a.Aberto, b.Andamento AS 'Em andamento', c.Fechado, d.Total
	FROM         (SELECT     COUNT(R .Name) AS Aberto
		      FROM          Cursos_Replan R
                      WHERE (R .PercComp = '0') AND R .CodCurso LIKE 'QUA%') a,

	     (SELECT     COUNT(R .Name) AS Andamento
		      FROM          Cursos_Replan R
                      WHERE (R .PercComp > '0') AND (R .PercComp < '100') AND (R .CodCurso LIKE 'QUA%')) b,

	       (SELECT     COUNT(R .Name) AS Fechado
		      FROM          Cursos_Replan R
                      WHERE (R .PercComp = '100') AND (R .CodCurso LIKE 'QUA%')) c,
             (SELECT     COUNT(R .Name) AS Total
		      FROM          Cursos_Replan R
                      WHERE (R .CodCurso LIKE 'QUA%')) d


UNION ALL                                                              
SELECT     Processo = 'RH', a.Aberto, b.Andamento AS 'Em andamento', c.Fechado, d.Total
	FROM         (SELECT     COUNT(R .Name) AS Aberto
		      FROM          Cursos_Replan R
                      WHERE (R .PercComp = '0') AND R .CodCurso LIKE 'RHU%') a,

	     (SELECT     COUNT(R .Name) AS Andamento
		      FROM          Cursos_Replan R
                      WHERE (R .PercComp > '0') AND (R .PercComp < '100') AND (R .CodCurso LIKE 'RHU%')) b,

	       (SELECT     COUNT(R .Name) AS Fechado
		      FROM          Cursos_Replan R
                      WHERE (R .PercComp = '100') AND (R .CodCurso LIKE 'RHU%')) c,
           (SELECT     COUNT(R .Name) AS Total
		      FROM          Cursos_Replan R
                      WHERE (R .CodCurso LIKE 'RHU%')) d

UNION ALL                                                              

SELECT     Processo = 'Total', a.Aberto, b.Andamento AS 'Em andamento', c.Fechado, d.Total
	FROM         (SELECT     COUNT(R .Name) AS Aberto
		      FROM          Cursos_Replan R
                      WHERE (R .PercComp = '0') AND R .CodCurso NOT LIKE 'BW%') a,

	     (SELECT     COUNT(R .Name) AS Andamento
		      FROM          Cursos_Replan R
                      WHERE (R .PercComp > '0') AND (R .PercComp < '100') AND (R .CodCurso NOT LIKE 'BW%')) b,

	       (SELECT     COUNT(R .Name) AS Fechado
		      FROM          Cursos_Replan R
                      WHERE (R .PercComp = '100') AND (R .CodCurso NOT LIKE 'BW%')) c,
           (SELECT     COUNT(R .Name) AS Total
		      FROM          Cursos_Replan R
                      WHERE (R .CodCurso NOT LIKE 'BW%%')) d
UNION ALL
SELECT     Processo = 'BW', a.Aberto, b.Andamento AS 'Em andamento', c.Fechado, d.Total 
	FROM         (SELECT     COUNT(R .Name) AS Aberto
		      FROM          Cursos_Replan R
                      WHERE (R .PercComp = '0') AND R .CodCurso LIKE 'BW%') a,

	     (SELECT     COUNT(R .Name) AS Andamento
		      FROM          Cursos_Replan R
                      WHERE (R .PercComp > '0') AND (R .PercComp < '100') AND (R .CodCurso LIKE 'BW%')) b,

	       (SELECT     COUNT(R .Name) AS Fechado
		      FROM          Cursos_Replan R
                      WHERE (R .PercComp = '100') AND (R .CodCurso LIKE 'BW%')) c,
		(SELECT     COUNT(R .Name) AS Total
		      FROM          Cursos_Replan R     
		WHERE (R .CodCurso  LIKE 'BW%%')) d                                          




GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

CREATE VIEW dbo.Ar
AS
SELECT     dbo.MSP_NUMBER_FIELDS.PROJ_ID, dbo.MSP_NUMBER_FIELDS.NUM_REF_UID, dbo.MSP_NUMBER_FIELDS.NUM_FIELD_ID, 
                      dbo.MSP_CONVERSIONS.CONV_STRING, dbo.MSP_NUMBER_FIELDS.NUM_VALUE
FROM         dbo.MSP_CONVERSIONS INNER JOIN
                      dbo.MSP_NUMBER_FIELDS ON dbo.MSP_CONVERSIONS.CONV_VALUE = dbo.MSP_NUMBER_FIELDS.NUM_FIELD_ID
WHERE     (dbo.MSP_CONVERSIONS.CONV_STRING = 'Task Number12')

GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

CREATE VIEW dbo.CodigoCurso
AS
SELECT    dbo.MSP_TEXT_FIELDS.Proj_ID, dbo.MSP_TEXT_FIELDS.TEXT_REF_UID, dbo.MSP_TEXT_FIELDS.TEXT_VALUE
FROM         dbo.MSP_CONVERSIONS INNER JOIN
                      dbo.MSP_TEXT_FIELDS ON dbo.MSP_CONVERSIONS.CONV_VALUE = dbo.MSP_TEXT_FIELDS.TEXT_FIELD_ID
WHERE     (dbo.MSP_CONVERSIONS.CONV_STRING = 'TASK Text24') AND (dbo.MSP_TEXT_FIELDS.PROJ_ID = '1' or MSP_TEXT_FIELDS.PROJ_ID = '4' ) AND 
                      (dbo.MSP_TEXT_FIELDS.TEXT_VALUE <> 'Null')



GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

CREATE VIEW dbo.ComparaSaneamentoSinergia
AS
SELECT A.Cod_UID_Orig AS UID_Saneamento, A.Cod_UID_Dest AS UID_Sinergia, M.TASK_PCT_COMP AS PERCENTSaneamento, 
               T.TASK_PCT_COMP AS PERCENTSinergia, M.TASK_START_DATE AS STARTSaneamento, T.TASK_START_DATE AS STARTSinergia, 
               M.TASK_FINISH_DATE AS FINISHSaneamento, T.TASK_FINISH_DATE AS FINISHSinergia
FROM  dbo.MSP_TASKS M INNER JOIN
               dbo.Atualiza_Perc_San_Sin A ON A.Cod_UID_Orig = M.TASK_UID AND A.Cod_Proj_Orig = 8 AND M.PROJ_ID = 8 INNER JOIN
               dbo.MSP_TASKS T ON A.Cod_UID_Dest = T.TASK_UID AND A.Cod_Proj_Dest = 1 AND T.PROJ_ID = 1

GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

CREATE VIEW dbo.Compara_EP_SAN
AS
SELECT     A.Cod_UID_Orig AS UID_PMO, A.Cod_UID_Dest AS UID_EP, M.TASK_PCT_COMP AS PERCENTPMO, M.TASK_FINISH_DATE AS FINISHPMO, 
                      S.TASK_PCT_COMP AS PERCENTEP, S.TASK_FINISH_DATE AS FINISHEP
FROM         dbo.MSP_TASKS M INNER JOIN
                      dbo.Atualiza_Perc_EP_SMR A ON A.Cod_UID_Orig = M.TASK_UID AND A.Cod_Proj_Orig = '18' AND M.PROJ_ID = '8' INNER JOIN
                      EP.dbo.MSP_TASKS S ON A.Cod_UID_Dest = S.TASK_UID AND S.PROJ_ID = '18'



GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

CREATE VIEW DadosConsultaIssues AS 
SELECT ISS.ID, 
	ISS.Name, 
	ISS.Descricao, 
	ISS.Aberto_em, 
	ISS.Data_Fechamento, 
	ISS.Overall_Status, 
	ISS.Comments, 
	PRJ.Desc_Projeto, 
	PRJ.Prioridade_Ordenacao 
FROM Issues ISS, Projetos_PMO PRJ 
WHERE ISS.Project_Code = PRJ.Project_Code AND 
	ISS.Project_Code IN ('kcin001763PB_FI', 'kcin001777PB_FT', 'kcin001885PB_CO', 'kcin001772PB_SD-OIL', 'kcin001767PB_PC', 'kcin001766PB_MES') 





GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO




CREATE VIEW DadosConsultaRisks AS 
SELECT RKS.ID, 
	RKS.Name, 
	RKS.Descricao, 
	RKS.Aberto_em, 
	RKS.Data_Fechamento, 
	RKS.Overall_Status, 
	RKS.Comments, 
	PRJ.Desc_Projeto, 
	PRJ.Prioridade_Ordenacao 
FROM Risks RKS, Projetos_PMO PRJ 
WHERE RKS.Project_Code = PRJ.Project_Code AND 
	RKS.Project_Code IN ('kcin001763PB_FI', 'kcin001777PB_FT', 'kcin001885PB_CO', 'kcin001772PB_SD-OIL', 'kcin001767PB_PC', 'kcin001766PB_MES')

CREATE VIEW DadosDivLogIssues AS 
SELECT PRP.Desc_Projeto,
	ISS.ID, 
	ISS.Name, 
	ISS.Data_Limite, 
	ISS.Descricao,
	FNC.Nome_Funcionario,
	'NOV' as Tipo
FROM Issues ISS, Projetos_PMO PRP, Funcionarios FNC 
WHERE ISS.Project_Code = PRP.Project_Code AND 
	ISS.Usuario = FNC.Cod_Funcionario AND 
	ISS.Modulo = 'Sim' AND 
NOT EXISTS 
(SELECT * FROM Log_PMO_Issues LPO WHERE LPO.Project_Code = ISS.Project_Code AND LPO.Sr_No = ISS.Sr_No AND LPO.ID = ISS.ID)
UNION ALL
SELECT PRP.Desc_Projeto, 
	ISS.ID, ISS.Name, 
	ISS.Data_Limite, 
	ISS.Descricao,
	FNC.Nome_Funcionario,
	'ALT' as Tipo
FROM Issues ISS, Projetos_PMO PRP, Funcionarios FNC, Log_PMO_Issues LPO
WHERE     ISS.Project_Code = PRP.Project_Code AND 
	ISS.Usuario = FNC.Cod_Funcionario AND 
	ISS.Modulo = 'Sim' AND 
             LPO.Project_Code = ISS.Project_Code AND 
	LPO.Sr_No = ISS.Sr_No AND 
	LPO.ID = ISS.ID AND 
	LPO.Modulo <> 'Sim'






GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO




CREATE VIEW DadosDivLogRisks AS 
SELECT PRP.Desc_Projeto, 
	RKS.ID, 
	RKS.Name, 
	RKS.Data_Limite, 
	RKS.Descricao, 
	FNC.Nome_Funcionario,
	'NOV' as Tipo
FROM Risks RKS, Projetos_PMO PRP, Funcionarios FNC 
WHERE RKS.Project_Code = PRP.Project_Code AND 
	RKS.Usuario = FNC.Cod_Funcionario AND 
	RKS.Modulo = 'Sim' AND 
	NOT EXISTS (SELECT * FROM Log_PMO_Risks LPO WHERE LPO.Project_Code = RKS.Project_Code AND LPO.Sr_No = RKS.Sr_No AND LPO.ID = RKS.ID) 
UNION ALL 
SELECT PRP.Desc_Projeto, 
	RKS.ID, 
	RKS.Name, 
	RKS.Data_Limite, 
	RKS.Descricao, 
	FNC.Nome_Funcionario, 
	'ALT' as Tipo
FROM Risks RKS, Projetos_PMO PRP, Funcionarios FNC, Log_PMO_Risks LPO 
WHERE RKS.Project_Code = PRP.Project_Code AND 
	RKS.Usuario = FNC.Cod_Funcionario AND 
	RKS.Modulo = 'Sim' AND 
	LPO.Project_Code = RKS.Project_Code AND 
	LPO.Sr_No = RKS.Sr_No AND 
	LPO.ID = RKS.ID AND
	 LPO.Modulo <> 'Sim' 




GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF

SET ANSI_NULLS ON

CREATE VIEW dbo.Issues_Rel
AS
SELECT    TOP 100 PERCENT   I.ID AS Codigo, I.Name AS Nome, C.Desc_Comite AS Comite, E.Desc_Eqp AS Equipe,Riscos=0
FROM         dbo.Issues I INNER JOIN
                      dbo.Comites C ON C.Cod_Comite = I.Comite INNER JOIN
                      dbo.Equipes E ON E.Cod_Eqp = I.Equipe
WHERE     (I.Project_Code = 'kcin001774PB_RH' OR
                      I.Project_Code = 'kcin001773PB_BW' OR
                      I.Project_Code = 'kcin001876PB_Master') AND (I.Overall_Status = 'open' OR
                      I.Overall_Status = 'past due')and (E.Desc_Eqp not like '--Não Definido--')
ORDER BY C.DESC_COMITE





GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

CREATE VIEW dbo.Risk_Teste
AS
SELECT     R.ID, R.Name, R.Area_Impacto, R.Impacto, R.Probabilidade, C.Desc_Comite, E.Desc_Eqp
FROM         dbo.Risks R INNER JOIN
                      dbo.Comites C ON C.Cod_Comite = R.Comite INNER JOIN
                      dbo.Equipes E ON E.Cod_Eqp = R.Equipe
where R.Overall_status='Open' or R.Overall_status='Past Due'

GO

CREATE VIEW dbo.Risks_Rel
AS
SELECT TOP 100 PERCENT R.ID AS Codigo, R.Name as Nome, C.Desc_Comite as Comite, E.Desc_EQP as Equipe,Riscos=1
FROM         dbo.Risks R 
INNER JOIN  dbo.Comites C ON C.Cod_Comite = R.Comite
INNER JOIN dbo.Equipes E ON E.COD_EQP = R.Equipe
WHERE     (R.Project_Code = 'kcin001774PB_RH' or R.Project_Code='kcin001773PB_BW' or R.Project_Code='kcin001876PB_Master')
AND ( R.Overall_Status = 'open' OR R.Overall_Status = 'past due')and (E.Desc_EQP not like '--Não Definido--')
ORDER BY C.Desc_Comite





GO

CREATE VIEW dbo.Cursos_Replan
AS
SELECT     T.TASK_UID AS UID, C.TEXT_VALUE AS CodCurso, T.TASK_NAME AS Name, T.TASK_START_DATE AS Start, T.TASK_FINISH_DATE AS Finish, 
                      T.TASK_PCT_COMP AS PercComp, F.DATE_VALUE AS Prev_Fim
FROM         dbo.MSP_TASKS T
 INNER JOIN
                      dbo.CodigoCurso C ON C.TEXT_REF_UID = T.TASK_UID AND C.Proj_ID = T.PROJ_ID LEFT OUTER JOIN
                      dbo.PrevisaoDeFim F ON F.DATE_REF_UID = T.TASK_UID AND F.PROJ_ID = T.PROJ_ID
WHERE     (T.TASK_IS_SUMMARY = '0') AND (T.PROJ_ID = 1 or T.PROJ_ID = 4 )





GO

CREATE VIEW DadosEntradas AS 

SELECT TK.PROJ_ID AS Projeto, 
	TK.TASK_ID AS ID, 
	TK.TASK_UID AS UID, 
	DE.FLAG_VALUE AS Flag2, 
	TK.TASK_NAME, 
	TK.TASK_BCWS AS BCWS, 
	TK.TASK_BCWP AS BCWP, 
	TK.TASK_ACWP AS ACWP, 
	TK.TASK_BASE_COST AS BAC, 
	TK.TASK_FINISH_DATE AS Finish, 
	TK.TASK_PCT_COMP AS Complete 
FROM MSP_TASKS TK LEFT OUTER JOIN DadosFlagEntradas DE 
	ON TK.PROJ_ID = DE.PROJ_ID AND 
	TK.TASK_UID = DE.FLAG_REF_UID WHERE DE.FLAG_VALUE = 1




GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

CREATE VIEW DadosFlagCurvaS_Final AS
Select Pj.PROJ_NAME,TK.TASK_ID, TK.TASK_UID,TK.TASK_NAME,TK.TASK_START_DATE,TK.TASK_FINISH_DATE,TK.TASK_PCT_COMP, DC.FLAG_VALUE
FROM MSP_PROJECTS PJ, MSP_TASKS TK left outer join DadosFlagCurvaS DC on 
	TK.PROJ_ID = DC.PROJ_ID and TK.TASK_UID = DC.FLAG_REF_UID 
Where PJ.PROJ_ID = TK.PROJ_ID
AND DC.FLAG_VALUE = 1

CREATE VIEW DadosLog AS 
SELECT  DadosArea.TEXT_VALUE as Area,
	DadosUnidade.TEXT_VALUE as Unidade,
	Log_Tarefas.Cod_Usuario, 
	Usuarios.Nome_Usuario AS Nome, 
	Usuarios.Flag_CLI,
	Log_Tarefas.Data_Atualizacao, 
	MSP_PROJECTS.PROJ_NAME, 
	Log_Tarefas.TASK_UID,
	MSP_TASKS.TASK_NAME,
	Log_Tarefas.TASK_PCT_COMP
FROM Log_Tarefas INNER JOIN Usuarios 
	ON Log_Tarefas.Cod_Usuario = Usuarios.Login COLLATE Latin1_General_CI_AS 
	INNER JOIN MSP_PROJECTS ON Log_Tarefas.PROJ_ID = MSP_PROJECTS.PROJ_ID 
	INNER JOIN MSP_TASKS ON Log_Tarefas.TASK_UID = MSP_TASKS.TASK_UID AND Log_Tarefas.PROJ_ID = MSP_TASKS.PROJ_ID 
	left outer join DadosArea ON DadosArea.PROJ_ID = Log_Tarefas.PROJ_ID AND DadosArea.TEXT_REF_UID = Log_Tarefas.TASK_UID
	left outer join DadosUnidade ON DadosUnidade.PROJ_ID = Log_Tarefas.PROJ_ID AND DadosUnidade.TEXT_REF_UID = Log_Tarefas.TASK_UID







GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

CREATE VIEW DadosTarefasAtrasadas AS 
SELECT TK.TASK_OUTLINE_NUM, 
	TK.TASK_NAME, 
	TK.TASK_OUTLINE_LEVEL, 
	TK.TASK_START_DATE, 
	TK.TASK_FINISH_DATE, 
	TK.TASK_PCT_COMP, 
	TK.TASK_IS_SUMMARY, 
	TK.TASK_IS_EXTERNAL, 
	TK.TASK_UID, 
	TK.TASK_ID, 
	TK.TASK_DUR, 
	UPPER(DR.TEXT_VALUE) as Responsavel, 
	UPPER(DE.TEXT_VALUE) as Equipe 
FROM MSP_TASKS TK left outer join DadosCLI DR on 
	TK.PROJ_ID = DR.PROJ_ID and TK.TASK_UID = DR.TEXT_REF_UID 
	left outer join DadosEquipe DE on 
	TK.PROJ_ID = DE.PROJ_ID and TK.TASK_UID = DE.TEXT_REF_UID 
WHERE TK.PROJ_ID = 1
 And (TK.TASK_ID IS NOT NULL) 
 And (TK.TASK_ID <> 0) 





GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

CREATE VIEW dbo.IDArea
AS
SELECT     TOP 100 PERCENT M.TASK_ID, D.TEXT_VALUE,m.task_is_summary
FROM         dbo.MSP_TASKS M LEFT OUTER JOIN
                      dbo.DadosArea D ON M.TASK_UID = D.TEXT_REF_UID AND M.PROJ_ID = D.PROJ_ID
WHERE     (M.PROJ_ID = 2) AND (M.TASK_UID > 0) AND (M.TASK_OUTLINE_NUM LIKE dbo.RetornaPetrobras())
ORDER BY M.TASK_ID


GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO


CREATE VIEW dbo.IDEquipe
AS
SELECT     m.TASK_ID, D.TEXT_VALUE,F.FLAG_VALUE AS Flag,m.task_is_summary
FROM         dbo.MSP_TASKS M LEFT OUTER JOIN
                      dbo.DadosEquipe D ON m.TASK_UID = D.TEXT_REF_UID AND m.PROJ_ID = D.PROJ_ID
		LEFT OUTER JOIN
                      dbo.FlagCurva F ON m.TASK_UID = F.FLAG_REF_UID AND m.PROJ_ID = F.PROJ_ID
WHERE     (m.PROJ_ID = 1) AND M.TASK_UID>0









GO

GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON

GO

CREATE VIEW dbo.IDUnidade
AS
SELECT     TOP 100 PERCENT M.TASK_ID, D.TEXT_VALUE
FROM         dbo.MSP_TASKS M LEFT OUTER JOIN
                      dbo.DadosUnidade D ON M.TASK_UID = D.TEXT_REF_UID AND M.PROJ_ID = D.PROJ_ID
WHERE     (M.PROJ_ID = 2) AND (M.TASK_UID > 0) AND M.TASK_OUTLINE_NUM like dbo.retornapetrobras()
ORDER BY M.TASK_ID

CREATE VIEW dbo.PainelSinopticoRefap
AS
SELECT     TOP 100 PERCENT M.TASK_UID AS UniqueID, M.TASK_NAME AS Nome, M.TASK_PCT_COMP AS Complete, M.TASK_START_DATE AS Inicio, 
                      M.TASK_FINISH_DATE AS Fim, R.NUM_VALUE AS Realizado, I.NUM_VALUE AS Impacto,  C.TEXT_VALUE AS Criticidade,M.TASK_OUTLINE_LEVEL AS Identacao,
	         M.TASK_IS_SUMMARY AS Sumaria	 
FROM         dbo.MSP_TASKS M INNER JOIN
                      dbo.Realizado R ON R.NUM_REF_UID = M.TASK_UID AND R.PROJ_ID = M.PROJ_ID INNER JOIN
                      dbo.ImpactoRefap I ON I.NUM_REF_UID = M.TASK_UID AND I.PROJ_ID = M.PROJ_ID INNER JOIN
                      dbo.Criticidade C ON C.TEXT_REF_UID = M.TASK_UID AND C.PROJ_ID = M.PROJ_ID INNER JOIN
                      dbo.FlagRefap F ON F.FLAG_REF_UID = M.TASK_UID AND F.PROJ_ID = M.PROJ_ID
WHERE     (M.PROJ_ID = 1) AND (F.FLAG_VALUE = 1)  and M.TASK_PCT_COMP<>100
ORDER BY M.TASK_ID





GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO


CREATE VIEW dbo.RelEquipes
AS
SELECT     Processo = 'GM-AO', SUM(M.TASK_BCWS) AS BCWSTOT, SUM(M.TASK_BCWP) AS BCWPTOT, SUM(M.TASK_ACT_COST) AS ACTOT
FROM         MSP_TASKS M INNER JOIN
                      DADOSEQUIPE D ON D .TEXT_REF_UID = M.TASK_UID AND D .PROJ_ID = M.PROJ_ID
WHERE     M.TASK_IS_SUMMARY = '0' AND M.PROJ_ID = 1 AND D .TEXT_VALUE = 'GM-AO'
UNION ALL
SELECT     Processo = 'GM-CO', SUM(M.TASK_BCWS) AS BCWSTOT, SUM(M.TASK_BCWP) AS BCWPTOT, SUM(M.TASK_ACT_COST) AS ACTOT
FROM         MSP_TASKS M INNER JOIN
                      DADOSEQUIPE D ON D .TEXT_REF_UID = M.TASK_UID AND D .PROJ_ID = M.PROJ_ID
WHERE    M.TASK_IS_SUMMARY = '0' AND M.PROJ_ID =1 AND D .TEXT_VALUE = 'GM-CO'
UNION ALL
SELECT     Processo = 'GM-MO', SUM(M.TASK_BCWS) AS BCWSTOT, SUM(M.TASK_BCWP) AS BCWPTOT, SUM(M.TASK_ACT_COST) AS ACTOT
FROM         MSP_TASKS M INNER JOIN
                      DADOSEQUIPE D ON D .TEXT_REF_UID = M.TASK_UID AND D .PROJ_ID = M.PROJ_ID
WHERE     M.TASK_IS_SUMMARY = '0' AND M.PROJ_ID = 1 AND D .TEXT_VALUE = 'GM-MO'
UNION ALL
SELECT     Processo = 'GM-TR', SUM(M.TASK_BCWS) AS BCWSTOT, SUM(M.TASK_BCWP) AS BCWPTOT, SUM(M.TASK_ACT_COST) AS ACTOT
FROM         MSP_TASKS M INNER JOIN
                      DADOSEQUIPE D ON D .TEXT_REF_UID = M.TASK_UID AND D .PROJ_ID = M.PROJ_ID
WHERE     M.TASK_IS_SUMMARY = '0' AND M.PROJ_ID = 1 AND D .TEXT_VALUE = 'GM-TR'
UNION ALL
SELECT     Processo = 'GM-SUP', SUM(M.TASK_BCWS) AS BCWSTOT, SUM(M.TASK_BCWP) AS BCWPTOT, SUM(M.TASK_ACT_COST) AS ACTOT
FROM         MSP_TASKS M INNER JOIN
                      DADOSEQUIPE D ON D .TEXT_REF_UID = M.TASK_UID AND D .PROJ_ID = M.PROJ_ID
WHERE     M.TASK_IS_SUMMARY = '0' AND M.PROJ_ID = 1 AND D .TEXT_VALUE = 'GM-SUP'
UNION ALL
SELECT     Processo = 'INT', SUM(M.TASK_BCWS) AS BCWSTOT, SUM(M.TASK_BCWP) AS BCWPTOT, SUM(M.TASK_ACT_COST) AS ACTOT
FROM         MSP_TASKS M INNER JOIN
                      DADOSEQUIPE D ON D .TEXT_REF_UID = M.TASK_UID AND D .PROJ_ID = M.PROJ_ID
WHERE     M.TASK_IS_SUMMARY = '0' AND M.PROJ_ID = 1 AND D .TEXT_VALUE = 'INT'
UNION ALL
SELECT     Processo = 'INT-ECCS', SUM(M.TASK_BCWS) AS BCWSTOT, SUM(M.TASK_BCWP) AS BCWPTOT, SUM(M.TASK_ACT_COST) AS ACTOT
FROM         MSP_TASKS M INNER JOIN
                      DADOSEQUIPE D ON D .TEXT_REF_UID = M.TASK_UID AND D .PROJ_ID = M.PROJ_ID
WHERE     M.TASK_IS_SUMMARY = '0' AND M.PROJ_ID = 1 AND D .TEXT_VALUE = 'INT-ECCS'
UNION ALL
SELECT     Processo = 'INT-FI', SUM(M.TASK_BCWS) AS BCWSTOT, SUM(M.TASK_BCWP) AS BCWPTOT, SUM(M.TASK_ACT_COST) AS ACTOT
FROM         MSP_TASKS M INNER JOIN
                      DADOSEQUIPE D ON D .TEXT_REF_UID = M.TASK_UID AND D .PROJ_ID = M.PROJ_ID
WHERE    M.TASK_IS_SUMMARY = '0' AND M.PROJ_ID =1 AND D .TEXT_VALUE = 'INT-FI'
UNION ALL
SELECT     Processo = 'INT-FT', SUM(M.TASK_BCWS) AS BCWSTOT, SUM(M.TASK_BCWP) AS BCWPTOT, SUM(M.TASK_ACT_COST) AS ACTOT
FROM         MSP_TASKS M INNER JOIN
                      DADOSEQUIPE D ON D .TEXT_REF_UID = M.TASK_UID AND D .PROJ_ID = M.PROJ_ID
WHERE    M.TASK_IS_SUMMARY = '0' AND M.PROJ_ID = 1 AND D .TEXT_VALUE = 'INT-FT'
UNION ALL
SELECT     Processo = 'INT-LT', SUM(M.TASK_BCWS) AS BCWSTOT, SUM(M.TASK_BCWP) AS BCWPTOT, SUM(M.TASK_ACT_COST) AS ACTOT
FROM         MSP_TASKS M INNER JOIN
                      DADOSEQUIPE D ON D .TEXT_REF_UID = M.TASK_UID AND D .PROJ_ID = M.PROJ_ID
WHERE     M.TASK_IS_SUMMARY = '0' AND M.PROJ_ID = 1 AND D .TEXT_VALUE = 'INT-LT'
UNION ALL
SELECT     Processo = 'INT-MES', SUM(M.TASK_BCWS) AS BCWSTOT, SUM(M.TASK_BCWP) AS BCWPTOT, SUM(M.TASK_ACT_COST) AS ACTOT
FROM         MSP_TASKS M INNER JOIN
                      DADOSEQUIPE D ON D .TEXT_REF_UID = M.TASK_UID AND D .PROJ_ID = M.PROJ_ID
WHERE    M.TASK_IS_SUMMARY = '0' AND M.PROJ_ID =1 AND D .TEXT_VALUE = 'INT-MES'
UNION ALL
SELECT     Processo = 'INT-PC', SUM(M.TASK_BCWS) AS BCWSTOT, SUM(M.TASK_BCWP) AS BCWPTOT, SUM(M.TASK_ACT_COST) AS ACTOT
FROM         MSP_TASKS M INNER JOIN
                      DADOSEQUIPE D ON D .TEXT_REF_UID = M.TASK_UID AND D .PROJ_ID = M.PROJ_ID
WHERE     M.TASK_IS_SUMMARY = '0' AND M.PROJ_ID = 1 AND D .TEXT_VALUE = 'INT-PC'
UNION ALL
SELECT     Processo = 'INT-PM', SUM(M.TASK_BCWS) AS BCWSTOT, SUM(M.TASK_BCWP) AS BCWPTOT, SUM(M.TASK_ACT_COST) AS ACTOT
FROM         MSP_TASKS M INNER JOIN
                      DADOSEQUIPE D ON D .TEXT_REF_UID = M.TASK_UID AND D .PROJ_ID = M.PROJ_ID
WHERE     M.TASK_IS_SUMMARY = '0' AND M.PROJ_ID = 1 AND D .TEXT_VALUE = 'INT-PM'
UNION ALL

FROM         MSP_TASKS M INNER JOIN
                      DADOSEQUIPE D ON D .TEXT_REF_UID = M.TASK_UID AND D .PROJ_ID = M.PROJ_ID
WHERE     M.TASK_IS_SUMMARY = '0' AND M.PROJ_ID = 1 AND D .TEXT_VALUE = 'INT-PO'
UNION ALL
SELECT     Processo = 'INT-PP', SUM(M.TASK_BCWS) AS BCWSTOT, SUM(M.TASK_BCWP) AS BCWPTOT, SUM(M.TASK_ACT_COST) AS ACTOT
FROM         MSP_TASKS M INNER JOIN
                      DADOSEQUIPE D ON D .TEXT_REF_UID = M.TASK_UID AND D .PROJ_ID = M.PROJ_ID
WHERE     M.TASK_IS_SUMMARY = '0' AND M.PROJ_ID = 1 AND D .TEXT_VALUE = 'INT-PP'
UNION ALL
SELECT     Processo = 'INT-PS', SUM(M.TASK_BCWS) AS BCWSTOT, SUM(M.TASK_BCWP) AS BCWPTOT, SUM(M.TASK_ACT_COST) AS ACTOT
FROM         MSP_TASKS M INNER JOIN
                      DADOSEQUIPE D ON D .TEXT_REF_UID = M.TASK_UID AND D .PROJ_ID = M.PROJ_ID
WHERE    M.TASK_IS_SUMMARY = '0' AND M.PROJ_ID = 1 AND D .TEXT_VALUE = 'INT-PS'
UNION ALL
SELECT     Processo = 'INT-QM', SUM(M.TASK_BCWS) AS BCWSTOT, SUM(M.TASK_BCWP) AS BCWPTOT, SUM(M.TASK_ACT_COST) AS ACTOT
FROM         MSP_TASKS M INNER JOIN
                      DADOSEQUIPE D ON D .TEXT_REF_UID = M.TASK_UID AND D .PROJ_ID = M.PROJ_ID
WHERE    M.TASK_IS_SUMMARY = '0'  AND M.PROJ_ID = 1 AND D .TEXT_VALUE = 'INT-QM'
UNION ALL
SELECT     Processo = 'INT-SD', SUM(M.TASK_BCWS) AS BCWSTOT, SUM(M.TASK_BCWP) AS BCWPTOT, SUM(M.TASK_ACT_COST) AS ACTOT
FROM         MSP_TASKS M INNER JOIN
                      DADOSEQUIPE D ON D .TEXT_REF_UID = M.TASK_UID AND D .PROJ_ID = M.PROJ_ID
WHERE    M.TASK_IS_SUMMARY = '0' AND M.PROJ_ID =1 AND D .TEXT_VALUE = 'INT-SD'
UNION ALL
SELECT     Processo = 'TI', SUM(M.TASK_BCWS) AS BCWSTOT, SUM(M.TASK_BCWP) AS BCWPTOT, SUM(M.TASK_ACT_COST) AS ACTOT
FROM         MSP_TASKS M INNER JOIN
                      DADOSEQUIPE D ON D .TEXT_REF_UID = M.TASK_UID AND D .PROJ_ID = M.PROJ_ID
WHERE    M.TASK_IS_SUMMARY = '0'  AND M.PROJ_ID = 1 AND D .TEXT_VALUE = 'TI'

CREATE VIEW dbo.RelRefap2
AS
SELECT     E.TEXT_VALUE AS Equipe, M.TASK_UID AS UID, M.TASK_NAME AS NOME, 
                      M.TASK_BCWP/100 AS BCWP,  M.TASK_BCWS/100 AS BCWS, CAST(((M.TASK_BCWS - M.TASK_BCWP) ) /
                        (dbo.RetornaBCWS(E.TEXT_VALUE) - dbo.RetornaBCWP(E.TEXT_VALUE)) AS DECIMAL(25,6)) AS ATRASO,
 M.TASK_BCWP/M.TASK_BCWS AS SPI, (M.TASK_BCWP - M.TASK_BCWS) 
                      / M.TASK_BCWS AS 'SV%', (M.TASK_BASE_DUR / 4800) * (M.TASK_BCWP - M.TASK_BCWS) / M.TASK_BASE_COST AS SV_DIAS, 
                      R.TEXT_VALUE AS Responsavel, M.TASK_START_DATE AS START, M.TASK_FINISH_DATE AS FINISH
FROM         dbo.MSP_TASKS M LEFT OUTER JOIN
                      dbo.DadosEquipe E ON M.PROJ_ID = E.PROJ_ID AND M.TASK_UID = E.TEXT_REF_UID LEFT OUTER JOIN
                      dbo.DadosResponsavel R ON M.PROJ_ID = R.PROJ_ID AND M.TASK_UID = R.TEXT_REF_UID
WHERE     (M.TASK_BCWS <> 0) AND M.TASK_BCWS<>M.TASK_ACT_COST AND (M.PROJ_ID = 1) AND (E.TEXT_VALUE<>'' AND  E.TEXT_VALUE<>'GM' AND M.TASK_IS_SUMMARY=0)
and M.TASK_BCWS>M.TASK_BCWP


































GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO


CREATE PROCEDURE SP_LISTAR_SCORECARD_USUARIO_RISKS
AS

Select dbo.RetornaUsuario(R.Workflow, F.Nome_Funcionario, R.Identificador, R.Designador, R.Responsavel, R.Revisor, R.Aprovador) as Ret_Usuario,
	sum(dbo.ScoreCardAndamentoUsuarioRisks(R.Data_Limite)) As NoPrazo,
	sum(dbo.ScoreCardAtrasadas10UsuarioRisks(R.Data_Limite)) As Atras10,
	sum(dbo.ScoreCardAtrasadasMais10UsuarioRisks(R.Data_Limite)) As AtrasM10
From Risks R, Funcionarios F
Where R.Usuario = F.Cod_Funcionario And
	R.Overall_Status <> 'Closed'
Group by dbo.RetornaUsuario(R.Workflow, F.Nome_Funcionario, R.Identificador, R.Designador, R.Responsavel, R.Revisor, R.Aprovador)
Order by AtrasM10 desc




GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS OFF 
GO

CREATE PROCEDURE SP_ALTERAR_CONFIG_FECHAMENTO
(
@SIGLA_PROJ varchar(10),
@FLAG_SEG NUMERIC(9),
@HR_SEG_INICIO VARCHAR(8),
@HR_SEG_FIM VARCHAR(8),
@FLAG_TER NUMERIC(9),
@HR_TER_INICIO VARCHAR(8),
@HR_TER_FIM VARCHAR(8),
@FLAG_QUA NUMERIC(9),
@HR_QUA_INICIO VARCHAR(8),
@HR_QUA_FIM VARCHAR(8),
@FLAG_QUI NUMERIC(9),
@HR_QUI_INICIO VARCHAR(8),
@HR_QUI_FIM VARCHAR(8),
@FLAG_SEX NUMERIC(9),
@HR_SEX_INICIO VARCHAR(8),
@HR_SEX_FIM VARCHAR(8),
@FLAG_SAB NUMERIC(9),
@HR_SAB_INICIO VARCHAR(8),
@HR_SAB_FIM VARCHAR(8),
@FLAG_DOM NUMERIC(9),
@HR_DOM_INICIO VARCHAR(8),
@HR_DOM_FIM VARCHAR(8)
)
AS
UPDATE 
	CONFIG_FECHAMENTO
SET
	FLAG_SEG = @FLAG_SEG,
	HR_SEG_INICIO = @HR_SEG_INICIO,
	HR_SEG_FIM = @HR_SEG_FIM,
	FLAG_TER = @FLAG_TER,
	HR_TER_INICIO = @HR_TER_INICIO,
	HR_TER_FIM = @HR_TER_FIM,
	FLAG_QUA = @FLAG_QUA,
	HR_QUA_INICIO = @HR_QUA_INICIO,
	HR_QUA_FIM = @HR_QUA_FIM,
	FLAG_QUI = @FLAG_QUI,
	HR_QUI_INICIO = @HR_QUI_INICIO,
	HR_QUI_FIM = @HR_QUI_FIM,
	FLAG_SEX = @FLAG_SEX,
	HR_SEX_INICIO = @HR_SEX_INICIO,
	HR_SEX_FIM = @HR_SEX_FIM,
	FLAG_SAB = @FLAG_SAB,
	HR_SAB_INICIO = @HR_SAB_INICIO,
	HR_SAB_FIM = @HR_SAB_FIM,
	FLAG_DOM = @FLAG_DOM,
	HR_DOM_INICIO = @HR_DOM_INICIO,
	HR_DOM_FIM = @HR_DOM_FIM
WHERE
	SIGLA_PROJ = @SIGLA_PROJ
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS OFF 
GO

CREATE PROCEDURE SP_ALTERAR_FRENTE
(
@Cod_Frente		NUMERIC(9),
@Descricao		VARCHAR(50)
)
AS
UPDATE 
	Frentes
SET
	Desc_Frente = @Descricao
WHERE
	Cod_Frente = @Cod_Frente




GO

CREATE PROCEDURE SP_ALTERAR_PROJETO
(
@CodigoProjeto		VARCHAR(25),
@NomeProjeto		VARCHAR(50),
@Ordenacao		SMALLINT
)

AS

UPDATE 
	Projetos_PMO
SET
	Desc_Projeto = @NomeProjeto,
	Prioridade_Ordenacao = @Ordenacao
WHERE
	Project_Code = @CodigoProjeto





GO

CREATE PROCEDURE SP_ALTERAR_USUARIO 
(
@Login			VARCHAR(10),
@NomeUsuario		VARCHAR(50),
@Senha		VARCHAR(10),
@Integrador		VARCHAR(50),
@Flag_CLI		BIT,
@Unidade                       NUMERIC(9)
)
AS
UPDATE 
	Usuarios
SET
	Nome_Usuario = @NomeUsuario,
	Senha = @Senha,
	Cod_Unidade = @Unidade,
	Integrador = @Integrador,
	Flag_CLI = @Flag_CLI
WHERE
	Login = @Login
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS OFF 
GO


CREATE PROCEDURE SP_ATUALIZAR_CASES_TESTEINT_SIN 
(
@Projeto int,
@UID int,
@Data datetime
)

AS
	declare @Cont int

	Select @Cont = count(*) from MSP_DATE_FIELDS Where PROJ_ID = @Projeto and DATE_REF_UID = @UID and DATE_FIELD_ID = 188743965
		
	If @Cont <> 0
	Begin
		UPDATE  MSP_DATE_FIELDS Set DATE_VALUE = @Data
			Where PROJ_ID = @Projeto and DATE_REF_UID = @UID  and DATE_FIELD_ID = 188743965
	End
	Else
	Begin
		Insert into MSP_DATE_FIELDS (PROJ_ID, DATE_CATEGORY, DATE_REF_UID, DATE_FIELD_ID, DATE_VALUE) 
		Values (@Projeto, 0, @UID, 188743965, @Data)
	End


	Select @Cont = count(*) from MSP_FLAG_FIELDS Where PROJ_ID = @Projeto and FLAG_REF_UID = @UID and FLAG_FIELD_ID = 188743761
		
	If @Cont <> 0
	Begin
		UPDATE  MSP_FLAG_FIELDS Set FLAG_VALUE = 1
			Where PROJ_ID = @Projeto and FLAG_REF_UID = @UID  and FLAG_FIELD_ID = 188743761
	End
	Else
	Begin
		Insert into MSP_FLAG_FIELDS (PROJ_ID, FLAG_CATEGORY, FLAG_REF_UID, FLAG_FIELD_ID, FLAG_VALUE) 
		Values (@Projeto, 0, @UID, 188743761, 1)
	End

GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

CREATE PROCEDURE SP_ATUALIZAR_DADOS_SCORECARD
AS
	
Delete from Scorecard
Where CONVERT(VARCHAR(8),Data_Score,112) = CONVERT(VARCHAR(8),GETDATE(),112)

Insert into Scorecard
select GetDate() as Data,
	Cod_Eqp, 
	'Baixo' as Criticidade,
	dbo.ScoreCardAndamento(Cod_Eqp, 'Baixo', getdate()) as Andamento, 
	dbo.ScoreCardAtrasadas10(Cod_Eqp, 'Baixo', getdate()) as Atrasadas10,
	dbo.ScoreCardAtrasadasMais10(Cod_Eqp, 'Baixo', getdate()) as AtrasadasMais10
from Equipes
Where (dbo.ScoreCardAndamento(Cod_Eqp, 'Baixo', getdate()) <> 0 
	OR dbo.ScoreCardAtrasadas10(Cod_Eqp, 'Baixo', getdate()) <> 0 
	OR dbo.ScoreCardAtrasadasMais10(Cod_Eqp, 'Baixo', getdate()) <> 0)
Union All
select GetDate() as Data,
	Cod_Eqp, 
	'Moderado' as Criticidade,
	dbo.ScoreCardAndamento(Cod_Eqp, 'Moderado', getdate()) as Andamento, 
	dbo.ScoreCardAtrasadas10(Cod_Eqp, 'Moderado', getdate()) as Atrasadas10,
	dbo.ScoreCardAtrasadasMais10(Cod_Eqp, 'Moderado', getdate()) as AtrasadasMais10
from Equipes
Where (dbo.ScoreCardAndamento(Cod_Eqp, 'Moderado', getdate()) <> 0 
	OR dbo.ScoreCardAtrasadas10(Cod_Eqp, 'Moderado', getdate()) <> 0 
	OR dbo.ScoreCardAtrasadasMais10(Cod_Eqp, 'Moderado', getdate()) <> 0)
Union All
select GetDate() as Data,
	Cod_Eqp, 
	'Alto' as Criticidade,
	dbo.ScoreCardAndamento(Cod_Eqp, 'Alto', getdate()) as Andamento, 
	dbo.ScoreCardAtrasadas10(Cod_Eqp, 'Alto', getdate()) as Atrasadas10,
	dbo.ScoreCardAtrasadasMais10(Cod_Eqp, 'Alto', getdate()) as AtrasadasMais10
from Equipes
Where (dbo.ScoreCardAndamento(Cod_Eqp, 'Alto', getdate()) <> 0 
	OR dbo.ScoreCardAtrasadas10(Cod_Eqp, 'Alto', getdate()) <> 0 
	OR dbo.ScoreCardAtrasadasMais10(Cod_Eqp, 'Alto', getdate()) <> 0)
Union All
select GetDate() as Data,
	Cod_Eqp, 
	'Critico' as Criticidade,
	dbo.ScoreCardAndamento(Cod_Eqp, 'Critico', getdate()) as Andamento, 
	dbo.ScoreCardAtrasadas10(Cod_Eqp, 'Critico', getdate()) as Atrasadas10,
	dbo.ScoreCardAtrasadasMais10(Cod_Eqp, 'Critico', getdate()) as AtrasadasMais10
from Equipes
Where (dbo.ScoreCardAndamento(Cod_Eqp, 'Critico', getdate()) <> 0 
	OR dbo.ScoreCardAtrasadas10(Cod_Eqp, 'Critico', getdate()) <> 0 
	OR dbo.ScoreCardAtrasadasMais10(Cod_Eqp, 'Critico', getdate()) <> 0)
Union All
select GetDate() as Data,
	Cod_Eqp, 
	'--Nao Definido--' as Criticidade,
	dbo.ScoreCardAndamento(Cod_Eqp, '--Nao Definido--', getdate()) as Andamento, 
	dbo.ScoreCardAtrasadas10(Cod_Eqp, '--Nao Definido--', getdate()) as Atrasadas10,
	dbo.ScoreCardAtrasadasMais10(Cod_Eqp, '--Nao Definido--', getdate()) as AtrasadasMais10
from Equipes
Where (dbo.ScoreCardAndamento(Cod_Eqp, '--Nao Definido--', getdate()) <> 0 
	OR dbo.ScoreCardAtrasadas10(Cod_Eqp, '--Nao Definido--', getdate()) <> 0 
	OR dbo.ScoreCardAtrasadasMais10(Cod_Eqp, '--Nao Definido--', getdate()) <> 0)

GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO


CREATE PROCEDURE SP_ATUALIZAR_DADOS_SCORECARD_RISKS
AS
	
Delete from Scorecard_Risks
Where CONVERT(VARCHAR(8),Data_Score,112) = CONVERT(VARCHAR(8),GETDATE(),112)

Insert into Scorecard_Risks

	Cod_Eqp, 
	'Baixo' as Criticidade,

	dbo.ScoreCardAtrasadas10Risks(Cod_Eqp, 'Baixo', getdate()) as Atrasadas10,
	dbo.ScoreCardAtrasadasMais10Risks(Cod_Eqp, 'Baixo', getdate()) as AtrasadasMais10

	OR dbo.ScoreCardAtrasadas10Risks(Cod_Eqp, 'Baixo', getdate()) <> 0 
	OR dbo.ScoreCardAtrasadasMais10Risks(Cod_Eqp, 'Baixo', getdate()) <> 0)

	OR dbo.ScoreCardAtrasadasMais10Risks(Cod_Eqp, 'Medio', getdate()) <> 0)

CREATE PROCEDURE SP_ATUALIZAR_DESENVOLVIMENTO 
(
@Projeto int,
@UID int,
@DtAtrazo datetime = Null,
@Complete int,
@Causa char(50) = Null,
@Descricao char(255),
@Tipo char(1) = Null,
@TesteInt int = Null
)

AS
	declare @Cont int
	
	UPDATE  MSP_TASKS Set TASK_PCT_COMP = @Complete, TASK_NAME = @Descricao
	Where PROJ_ID = @Projeto AND 
		TASK_UID = @UID
	
	/* Atualizacao da Data Atrazo*/
	IF @DtAtrazo IS NOT NULL
	BEGIN

		Select @Cont = count(*) from MSP_DATE_FIELDS Where PROJ_ID = @Projeto and DATE_REF_UID = @UID and DATE_FIELD_ID = 188743733
		
		If @Cont <> 0
		Begin
		UPDATE  MSP_DATE_FIELDS Set DATE_VALUE = @DtAtrazo
			Where PROJ_ID = @Projeto and DATE_REF_UID = @UID  and DATE_FIELD_ID = 188743733
		End
		Else
		Begin
			Insert into MSP_DATE_FIELDS (PROJ_ID, DATE_CATEGORY, DATE_REF_UID, DATE_FIELD_ID, DATE_VALUE) 
			Values (@Projeto, 0, @UID, 188743733, @DtAtrazo)
		End

	END	


	/* Atualizacao da Causa*/

	IF @Causa IS NOT NULL
	BEGIN

		Select @Cont = count(*) from MSP_TEXT_FIELDS Where PROJ_ID = @Projeto and TEXT_REF_UID = @UID and TEXT_FIELD_ID = 188744001
		
		If @Cont <> 0
		Begin
			UPDATE  MSP_TEXT_FIELDS Set TEXT_VALUE = @Causa
				Where PROJ_ID = @Projeto and TEXT_REF_UID = @UID  and TEXT_FIELD_ID = 188744001
		End
		Else
		Begin
			Insert into MSP_TEXT_FIELDS (PROJ_ID, TEXT_CATEGORY, TEXT_REF_UID, TEXT_FIELD_ID, TEXT_VALUE) 
			Values (@Projeto, 0, @UID, 188744001, @Causa)
		End
	END

	/* Atualizacao da Ferramenta H*/

	IF @Tipo IS NOT NULL
	BEGIN

		Select @Cont = count(*) from MSP_TEXT_FIELDS Where PROJ_ID = @Projeto and TEXT_REF_UID = @UID and TEXT_FIELD_ID = 188744002
		
		If @Cont <> 0
		Begin
			UPDATE  MSP_TEXT_FIELDS Set TEXT_VALUE = @Tipo
				Where PROJ_ID = @Projeto and TEXT_REF_UID = @UID  and TEXT_FIELD_ID = 188744002
		End
		Else
		Begin
			Insert into MSP_TEXT_FIELDS (PROJ_ID, TEXT_CATEGORY, TEXT_REF_UID, TEXT_FIELD_ID, TEXT_VALUE) 
			Values (@Projeto, 0, @UID, 188744001, @Tipo)
		End
	END

	/* Atualizacao do Teste Integrado*/
	IF @TesteInt IS NOT NULL
	BEGIN
		Select @Cont = count(*) from MSP_NUMBER_FIELDS Where PROJ_ID = @Projeto and NUM_REF_UID = @UID and NUM_FIELD_ID = 188743995
		
		If @Cont <> 0
		Begin
			UPDATE  MSP_NUMBER_FIELDS Set NUM_VALUE = @TesteInt
				Where PROJ_ID = @Projeto and NUM_REF_UID = @UID  and NUM_FIELD_ID = 188743995
		End
		Else
		Begin
			Insert into MSP_NUMBER_FIELDS (PROJ_ID, NUM_CATEGORY, NUM_REF_UID, NUM_FIELD_ID, NUM_VALUE) 
			Values (@Projeto, 0, @UID, 188743995, @TesteInt)
		End
	END
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS OFF 
GO



CREATE PROCEDURE SP_ATUALIZAR_ISSUES_GVI
(
@Cod_Issue int,
@Coment_Issue Text,
@Fechamento int,
@Login_Fechamento varchar(10) = Null
)
AS
IF @Fechamento  IS NOT NULL
BEGIN
	UPDATE  ISSUES_GVI Set Coment_Issue = @Coment_Issue,
		Fechamento = @Fechamento,
		Data_Fechamento = Getdate(),
		Login_Fechamento = @Login_Fechamento
		Where Cod_Issue = @Cod_Issue
End
Else
Begin
	UPDATE  ISSUES_GVI Set Coment_Issue = @Coment_Issue
		Where Cod_Issue = @Cod_Issue
End


GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS OFF 
GO

CREATE PROCEDURE SP_ATUALIZAR_PERCDT_TESTEINT_SIN 
(
@Projeto int,
@UID int,
@Data datetime,
@Complete int = Null
)

AS
	declare @Cont int
	
	IF @Complete IS NOT NULL
	BEGIN

		UPDATE  MSP_TASKS Set TASK_PCT_COMP = @Complete
		Where PROJ_ID = @Projeto AND 
			TASK_UID = @UID

	END

	Select @Cont = count(*) from MSP_DATE_FIELDS Where PROJ_ID = @Projeto and DATE_REF_UID = @UID and DATE_FIELD_ID = 188743969
		
	If @Cont <> 0
	Begin
		UPDATE  MSP_DATE_FIELDS Set DATE_VALUE = @Data
			Where PROJ_ID = @Projeto and DATE_REF_UID = @UID  and DATE_FIELD_ID = 188743969
	End
	Else
	Begin
		Insert into MSP_DATE_FIELDS (PROJ_ID, DATE_CATEGORY, DATE_REF_UID, DATE_FIELD_ID, DATE_VALUE) 
		Values (@Projeto, 0, @UID, 188743969, @Data)
	End

GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS OFF 
GO

DELETE TarefasExcluidas_GVI WHERE proj = @proj and id = @id and uid = @uid
GO

CREATE PROCEDURE SP_EXCLUIR_ATUALIZA_PERC_EP_SMR
(
@Codigo_Projeto_Org numeric(9),
@UID_Tarefa_Org numeric(9),
@Codigo_Projeto_Des numeric(9),
@UID_Tarefa_Des numeric(9)
)

AS

Delete from Atualiza_Perc_EP_SMR
Where Cod_Proj_Orig = @Codigo_Projeto_Org AND
	Cod_UID_Orig = @UID_Tarefa_Org AND
	Cod_Proj_Dest = @Codigo_Projeto_Des AND
	Cod_UID_Dest = @UID_Tarefa_Des



GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

CREATE PROCEDURE SP_EXCLUIR_DADOS_PMO AS
	
	Delete from Actions
	Delete from Chang_Requests
	Delete from Issues
	Delete from Risks
	Delete from Tasks
	Delete from Meetings
	Delete from Traceability
	Delete from Status_Report

CREATE PROCEDURE SP_EXCLUIR_FLAG_10_DATAS_FIM_7

AS


	UPDATE  MSP_DATE_FIELDS Set DATE_VALUE = Null
		Where DATE_FIELD_ID = 188743965


	UPDATE  MSP_FLAG_FIELDS Set FLAG_VALUE = Null
		Where FLAG_FIELD_ID = 188743761


SET NOCOUNT ON

CREATE PROCEDURE SP_INCLUIR_ACTIONS
@Project_Code		varchar(25),
@Sr_No		numeric(9),
@ID			varchar(	10),
@Name			text,
@Access		varchar(	20),
@Aberto_em		datetime,
@Data_Limite		datetime,
@Data_Fechamento	datetime,
@Descricao		text,
@Estimativa_Horas	numeric(9),
@Impacto		varchar(	20),
@Motivo		varchar(	50),
@Modulo		varchar(	50),
@Overall_Status	varchar(20),
@Percent_Complete	float,
@Priority		varchar(	20),
@Equipe		varchar(50),
@Usuario		varchar(50),
@Workflow		varchar(	50),
@Identificador		varchar(	50),
@Responsavel		varchar(	50),
@Revisor		varchar(	50),
@Comments		text,
@Traceability		text,
@Activity_Log		text,
@Comite		varchar(	20)

AS

declare @Nu_Access int
declare @Nu_Equipe int
declare @Nu_Comite int
declare @Nu_Usuario int


/* Access*/

select @Nu_Access = Cod_Access from Access where LTrim(RTrim(Desc_Access)) = LTrim(RTrim(@Access))

if @Nu_Access = 0 or @Nu_Access is Null  
Begin
	Insert into Access (Desc_Access) Values (LTrim(RTrim(@Access)))
	set @Nu_Access = @@IDENTITY
End

/* Equipes*/
select @Nu_Equipe = Cod_Eqp from Equipes where LTrim(RTrim(Desc_Eqp)) = LTrim(RTrim(@Equipe))

if @Nu_Equipe = 0  or @Nu_Equipe is Null  
Begin
	Insert into Equipes (Desc_Eqp) Values (LTrim(RTrim(@Equipe)))
	set @Nu_Equipe = @@IDENTITY
End

/* Comites*/
select @Nu_Comite = Cod_Comite from Comites where LTrim(RTrim(Desc_Comite)) = LTrim(RTrim(@Comite))

if @Nu_Comite = 0  or @Nu_Comite is Null  
Begin
	Insert into Comites (Desc_Comite) Values (LTrim(RTrim(@Comite)))
	set @Nu_Comite = @@IDENTITY
End


/* Usuario*/
select @Nu_Usuario = Cod_Funcionario from Funcionarios where Upper(LTrim(RTrim(Nome_Funcionario))) = Upper(LTrim(RTrim(@Usuario)))

if @Nu_Usuario = 0  or @Nu_Usuario is Null  
Begin
	Insert into Funcionarios (Nome_Funcionario) Values (Upper(LTrim(RTrim(@Usuario))))
	set @Nu_Usuario = @@IDENTITY
End

Insert into ACTIONS (Project_Code, Sr_No, ID, Name, Access, Aberto_em, Data_Limite, Data_Fechamento, Descricao, Estimativa_Horas, Impacto, Motivo, Modulo, Overall_Status, Percent_Complete, Priority, Equipe, Usuario, Workflow, Identificador, Responsavel, Revisor, Comments, Traceability, Activity_Log, Comite)
Values (@Project_Code, @Sr_No, @ID, @Name, @Nu_Access, @Aberto_em, @Data_Limite, @Data_Fechamento, @Descricao, @Estimativa_Horas, @Impacto, @Motivo, @Modulo, @Overall_Status, @Percent_Complete, @Priority, @Nu_Equipe, @Nu_Usuario, @Workflow, @Identificador, @Responsavel, @Revisor, @Comments, @Traceability, @Activity_Log, @Nu_Comite)



GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS OFF 
GO

CREATE PROCEDURE SP_INCLUIR_CHANG_REQUESTS 

@Project_Code		varchar(25),
@Sr_No		numeric(9),
@ID			varchar(10),
@Name			text,
@Aberto_em		datetime,
@Access		varchar(20),
@Alteracao		varchar(50),
@Check_If_Resolved	varchar(1),
@Comite		varchar(50),
@Data_Limite		datetime,
@Data_Fechamento	datetime,
@Descricao		text,
@Equipe		varchar(50),
@Estimativa_Horas	numeric(9),
@Limite			varchar(50),
@Modulo		varchar(20),
@Overall_Status	varchar(20),
@Percent_Complete	float,
@Priority		varchar(20),
@Release		varchar(50),
@Resolution		text,
@Revision_No		numeric(9),
@Site			varchar(50),
@Usuario		varchar(50),
@Workflow		varchar(50),
@Identificador		varchar(50),
@Requisitante		varchar(50),
@PMO			varchar(50),
@Analista		varchar(50),
@Revisor		varchar(50),
@Aprovador		varchar(50),
@Comments		text,
@Activity_Log		text,
@Traceability		text
AS

declare @Nu_Access int
declare @Nu_Equipe int
declare @Nu_Comite int
declare @Nu_Usuario int

/* Access*/

select @Nu_Access = Cod_Access from Access where LTrim(RTrim(Desc_Access)) = LTrim(RTrim(@Access))

if @Nu_Access = 0 or @Nu_Access is Null  
Begin
	Insert into Access (Desc_Access) Values (LTrim(RTrim(@Access)))
	set @Nu_Access = @@IDENTITY
End

/* Equipes*/
select @Nu_Equipe = Cod_Eqp from Equipes where LTrim(RTrim(Desc_Eqp)) = LTrim(RTrim(@Equipe))

if @Nu_Equipe = 0  or @Nu_Equipe is Null  
Begin
	Insert into Equipes (Desc_Eqp) Values (LTrim(RTrim(@Equipe)))
	set @Nu_Equipe = @@IDENTITY
End

/* Comites*/
select @Nu_Comite = Cod_Comite from Comites where LTrim(RTrim(Desc_Comite)) = LTrim(RTrim(@Comite))

if @Nu_Comite = 0  or @Nu_Comite is Null  
Begin
	Insert into Comites (Desc_Comite) Values (LTrim(RTrim(@Comite)))
	set @Nu_Comite = @@IDENTITY
End


/* Usuario*/
select @Nu_Usuario = Cod_Funcionario from Funcionarios where Upper(LTrim(RTrim(Nome_Funcionario))) = Upper(LTrim(RTrim(@Usuario)))

if @Nu_Usuario = 0  or @Nu_Usuario is Null  
Begin
	Insert into Funcionarios (Nome_Funcionario) Values (Upper(LTrim(RTrim(@Usuario))))
	set @Nu_Usuario = @@IDENTITY
End


Insert into CHANG_REQUESTS (Project_Code, Sr_No, ID, Name, Aberto_em, Access, Alteracao, Check_If_Resolved, Comite, Data_Limite, Data_Fechamento, Descricao, Equipe, Estimativa_Horas, Limite, Modulo, Overall_Status, Percent_Complete,	Priority, Release, Resolution, Revision_No, Site, Usuario, Workflow, Identificador, Requisitante, PMO, Analista, Revisor, Aprovador, Comments, Activity_Log, Traceability)
Values (@Project_Code, @Sr_No, @ID, @Name, @Aberto_em, @Nu_Access, @Alteracao, @Check_If_Resolved, @Nu_Comite, @Data_Limite, @Data_Fechamento, @Descricao, @Nu_Equipe, @Estimativa_Horas, @Limite, @Modulo, @Overall_Status, @Percent_Complete, @Priority, @Release, @Resolution, @Revision_No, @Site, @Nu_Usuario, @Workflow, @Identificador, @Requisitante, @PMO, @Analista, @Revisor, @Aprovador, @Comments, @Activity_Log, @Traceability)



GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF

CREATE PROCEDURE SP_INCLUIR_CHANG_REQUEST_DETALHE_TB_TEMP
(
@Usuario varchar(10)
)
AS
DECLARE @strSQL VARCHAR(2000)

SET NOCOUNT ON

delete from TabelaTemp Where Upper(RTrim(LTrim(Usuario))) = Upper(RTrim(LTrim(@Usuario))) AND Upper(RTrim(LTrim(Tipo))) = 'R'

SET @strSQL = 'Insert into TabelaTemp SELECT TK.TASK_OUTLINE_NUM, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.TASK_NAME, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.TASK_OUTLINE_LEVEL, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.TASK_BASE_START, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.TASK_BASE_FINISH, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.TASK_START_DATE, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.TASK_FINISH_DATE, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.TASK_PCT_COMP, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.TASK_IS_SUMMARY, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.TASK_IS_EXTERNAL, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.PROJ_ID, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'PJ.PROJ_NAME, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.TASK_UID, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.TASK_ID, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.TASK_DUR, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'Null as CLI, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'Null as Frente, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'Null as Equipe, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'Null as Governanca, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'Null as RespInt, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'Null as Area, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'Null as Unidade, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'UPPER(LTrim(RTrim(DL.TEXT_VALUE))) as Login, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'Null as Arquivo, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '''' +  @Usuario + ''' as Usuario, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '''R'' as Tipo '

SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'FROM MSP_PROJECTS PJ, MSP_TASKS TK left outer join DadosLogin DL on '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.PROJ_ID = DL.PROJ_ID and TK.TASK_UID = DL.TEXT_REF_UID '

SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'WHERE PJ.PROJ_ID = TK.PROJ_ID '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' And (TK.PROJ_ID = 1) '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' And (TK.TASK_ID IS NOT NULL)  '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' And (TK.TASK_ID <> 0) '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' And (TK.TASK_IS_SUMMARY <> 1) '

SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' And UPPER(LTrim(RTrim(DL.TEXT_VALUE))) LIKE ''%' +  UPPER(LTrim(RTrim(@Usuario)))+ '%'''

SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' Order By TK.PROJ_ID, TK.TASK_ID '

EXECUTE (@strSQL)

GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS OFF 
GO

CREATE PROCEDURE SP_INCLUIR_ISSUES 

@Project_Code	varchar(25),
@Sr_No numeric(9),
@ID	varchar(10),
@Name		text,
@Access varchar(20),
@Check_If_Resolved	char(1),
@Aberto_em	datetime,
@Data_Limite	datetime,
@Data_Fechamento	datetime,
@Descricao	text,
@Estimativa_Horas	numeric(9),
@Criticidade_Impacto	varchar(	20),
@Tipo_Impacto	varchar(	20),
@Modulo varchar(20),
@Overall_Status	varchar(	20),
@Percent_Complete	float,
@Priority	varchar(	20),
@Resolution	varchar(	50),
@Equipe varchar(50),
@Usuario varchar(50),
@Workflow	varchar(	50),
@Identificador	varchar(	50),
@Designador	varchar(	50),
@Responsavel	varchar(	50),
@Revisor	varchar(	50),
@Aprovador	varchar(	50),
@Comments	text,
@Traceability	text,
@Activity_Log	text,
@Causa	varchar(	50),
@Comite	varchar(	50),
@Descricao_Impacto	text

AS

declare @Nu_Access int
declare @Nu_Equipe int
declare @Nu_Comite int
declare @Nu_Usuario int

/* Access*/

select @Nu_Access = Cod_Access from Access where LTrim(RTrim(Desc_Access)) = LTrim(RTrim(@Access))

if @Nu_Access = 0 or @Nu_Access is Null  
Begin
	Insert into Access (Desc_Access) Values (LTrim(RTrim(@Access)))
	set @Nu_Access = @@IDENTITY
End

/* Equipes*/
select @Nu_Equipe = Cod_Eqp from Equipes where LTrim(RTrim(Desc_Eqp)) = LTrim(RTrim(@Equipe))

if @Nu_Equipe = 0  or @Nu_Equipe is Null  
Begin
	Insert into Equipes (Desc_Eqp) Values (LTrim(RTrim(@Equipe)))
	set @Nu_Equipe = @@IDENTITY
End


/* Comites*/
select @Nu_Comite = Cod_Comite from Comites where LTrim(RTrim(Desc_Comite)) = LTrim(RTrim(@Comite))

if @Nu_Comite = 0  or @Nu_Comite is Null  
Begin
	Insert into Comites (Desc_Comite) Values (LTrim(RTrim(@Comite)))
	set @Nu_Comite = @@IDENTITY
End


/* Usuario*/
select @Nu_Usuario = Cod_Funcionario from Funcionarios where Upper(LTrim(RTrim(Nome_Funcionario))) = Upper(LTrim(RTrim(@Usuario)))

if @Nu_Usuario = 0  or @Nu_Usuario is Null  
Begin
	Insert into Funcionarios (Nome_Funcionario) Values (Upper(LTrim(RTrim(@Usuario))))
	set @Nu_Usuario = @@IDENTITY
End

Insert into ISSUES (Project_Code, Sr_No, ID, Name, Access, Check_If_Resolved, Aberto_em, Data_Limite, Data_Fechamento	, Descricao, Estimativa_Horas, Criticidade_Impacto,
		Tipo_Impacto, Modulo, Overall_Status, Percent_Complete, Priority, Resolution, Equipe, Usuario, Workflow, Identificador, Designador, Responsavel, Revisor,
 		Aprovador, Comments, Traceability, Activity_Log, Causa, Comite, Descricao_Impacto)
Values (@Project_Code, @Sr_No, @ID, @Name, @Nu_Access, @Check_If_Resolved, @Aberto_em, @Data_Limite, @Data_Fechamento, @Descricao, @Estimativa_Horas, @Criticidade_Impacto, 
	@Tipo_Impacto, @Modulo, @Overall_Status, @Percent_Complete, @Priority, @Resolution, @Nu_Equipe, @Nu_Usuario, @Workflow, @Identificador, @Designador, @Responsavel, @Revisor,
	@Aprovador, @Comments, @Traceability, @Activity_Log, @Causa, @Nu_Comite, @Descricao_Impacto)



GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

CREATE PROCEDURE SP_INCLUIR_MEDICAO_DETALHE_TB_TEMP
(
@Usuario varchar(10),
@Frente varchar(255) = Null,
@Equipe varchar(255) = Null
)
AS
DECLARE @strSQL VARCHAR(2000)
DECLARE @dtAtual as datetime
SET @dtAtual = GETDATE()
WHILE  DATEPART(dw, @dtAtual) <> 3 
BEGIN
	SET @dtAtual = DATEADD(d,1,@dtAtual)
END

SET NOCOUNT ON

delete from TabelaTemp Where Upper(RTrim(LTrim(Usuario))) = Upper(RTrim(LTrim(@Usuario))) AND Upper(RTrim(LTrim(Tipo))) = 'M'

SET @strSQL = 'Insert into TabelaTemp SELECT TK.TASK_OUTLINE_NUM, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.TASK_NAME, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.TASK_OUTLINE_LEVEL, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.TASK_BASE_START, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.TASK_BASE_FINISH, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.TASK_START_DATE, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.TASK_FINISH_DATE, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.TASK_PCT_COMP, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.TASK_IS_SUMMARY, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.TASK_IS_EXTERNAL, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.PROJ_ID, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'PJ.PROJ_NAME, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.TASK_UID, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.TASK_ID, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.TASK_DUR, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'UPPER(DC.TEXT_VALUE) as CLI, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'UPPER(DF.TEXT_VALUE) as Frente, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'UPPER(DE.TEXT_VALUE) as Equipe, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'UPPER(DG.TEXT_VALUE) as Governanca, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'Null as RespInt, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'Null as Area, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'Null as Unidade, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'UPPER(LTrim(RTrim(DL.TEXT_VALUE))) as Login, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'UPPER(LTrim(RTrim(DA.TEXT_VALUE))) as Arquivo, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '''' +  @Usuario + ''' as Usuario, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '''M'' as Tipo '

SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'FROM MSP_PROJECTS PJ, MSP_TASKS TK left outer join DadosCLI DC on '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.PROJ_ID = DC.PROJ_ID and TK.TASK_UID = DC.TEXT_REF_UID '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'left outer join DadosFrete DF on '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.PROJ_ID = DF.PROJ_ID and TK.TASK_UID = DF.TEXT_REF_UID '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'left outer join DadosEquipe DE on '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.PROJ_ID = DE.PROJ_ID and TK.TASK_UID = DE.TEXT_REF_UID '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'left outer join DadosGovernanca DG on '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.PROJ_ID = DG.PROJ_ID and TK.TASK_UID = DG.TEXT_REF_UID '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'left outer join DadosUnidade DU on '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.PROJ_ID = DU.PROJ_ID and TK.TASK_UID = DU.TEXT_REF_UID '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'left outer join DadosLogin DL on '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.PROJ_ID = DL.PROJ_ID and TK.TASK_UID = DL.TEXT_REF_UID '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'left outer join DadosArquivo DA on '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.PROJ_ID = DA.PROJ_ID and TK.TASK_UID = DA.TEXT_REF_UID '

SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'WHERE PJ.PROJ_ID = TK.PROJ_ID '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' And (TK.TASK_ID IS NOT NULL)  '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' And (TK.TASK_ID <> 0) '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' And TK.TASK_START_DATE <= '''  + CONVERT(VARCHAR(8),@dtAtual,112) + ' 23:59:59'''
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' And (TK.TASK_PCT_COMP < 100) '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' And (TK.TASK_IS_SUMMARY <> 1) '


SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' And UPPER(LTrim(RTrim(DL.TEXT_VALUE))) LIKE ''%' +  UPPER(LTrim(RTrim(@Usuario)))+ '%'''


SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' Order By TK.PROJ_ID, TK.TASK_ID '

EXECUTE (@strSQL)

GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS OFF 
GO

SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'WHERE PJ.PROJ_ID = TK.PROJ_ID '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' And (TK.TASK_ID IS NOT NULL)  '

SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' And TK.TASK_START_DATE <= '''  + CONVERT(VARCHAR(8),@dtAtual,112) + ' 23:59:59'''
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' And (TK.TASK_PCT_COMP < 100) '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' And (TK.TASK_IS_SUMMARY <> 1) '


SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' And UPPER(LTrim(RTrim(DL.TEXT_VALUE))) LIKE ''%' +  UPPER(LTrim(RTrim(@Usuario)))+ '%'''

CREATE PROCEDURE SP_INCLUIR_MEETING
@Project_Code			varchar(25),
@Sr_No			numeric(9),
@ID				varchar(10),
@Title				varchar(50),
@Access			varchar(50),
@Agenda_Reuniao		varchar(50),
@Author			varchar(50),
@Comite			varchar(50),
@Data_Reuniao		datetime,
@Description			text,
@Equipe			varchar(50),
@Hora_Reuniao		datetime,
@Local				varchar(50),
@Modulo			varchar(50),
@Open_Items			numeric(9),
@Priority			varchar(20),
@Revision_No			numeric(9),
@Total_Items			numeric(9),
@Type				varchar(50),
@Autor				varchar(50),
@Aprovador			varchar(50),
@Comments			text,
@Activity_Log			text,
@Traceability			text
AS

declare @Nu_Access int
declare @Nu_Equipe int
declare @Nu_Comite int

/* Access*/

select @Nu_Access = Cod_Access from Access where LTrim(RTrim(Desc_Access)) = LTrim(RTrim(@Access))

if @Nu_Access = 0 or @Nu_Access is Null  
Begin
	Insert into Access (Desc_Access) Values (LTrim(RTrim(@Access)))
	set @Nu_Access = @@IDENTITY
End

/* Equipes*/
select @Nu_Equipe = Cod_Eqp from Equipes where LTrim(RTrim(Desc_Eqp)) = LTrim(RTrim(@Equipe))

if @Nu_Equipe = 0  or @Nu_Equipe is Null  
Begin
	Insert into Equipes (Desc_Eqp) Values (LTrim(RTrim(@Equipe)))
	set @Nu_Equipe = @@IDENTITY
End

/* Comites*/
select @Nu_Comite = Cod_Comite from Comites where LTrim(RTrim(Desc_Comite)) = LTrim(RTrim(@Comite))

if @Nu_Comite = 0  or @Nu_Comite is Null  
Begin
	Insert into Comites (Desc_Comite) Values (LTrim(RTrim(@Comite)))
	set @Nu_Comite = @@IDENTITY
End


Insert into Meetings (Project_Code, Sr_No,ID,Title,Access,Agenda_Reuniao,Author,Comite,Data_Reuniao,Description,Equipe,Hora_Reuniao,[Local],Modulo,Open_Items,Priority,Revision_No,Total_Items,Type,Autor,Aprovador,Comments,Activity_Log, Traceability)
Values (@Project_Code, @Sr_No,@ID,@Title,@Nu_Access,@Agenda_Reuniao,@Author,@Nu_Comite,@Data_Reuniao,@Description,@Nu_Equipe,@Hora_Reuniao,@Local,@Modulo,@Open_Items,@Priority,@Revision_No,@Total_Items,@Type,@Autor,@Aprovador,@Comments,@Activity_Log, @Traceability)


GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS OFF 
GO

CREATE PROCEDURE SP_INCLUIR_REL_MEDICAO_DETALHE_TB_TEMP
(
@Usuario varchar(10),
@NomeCLI varchar(255) = Null,
@Frente varchar(255) = Null,
@Equipe varchar(255) = Null,
@Governanca varchar(255) = Null,
@Unidade varchar(255) = Null,
@Area varchar(255) = Null,
@Completo varchar(1) = Null,
@DtInicio varchar(1) = Null
)
AS
DECLARE @strSQL VARCHAR(2000)
DECLARE @dtAtual as datetime
SET @dtAtual = GETDATE()

delete from TabelaTemp Where Upper(RTrim(LTrim(Usuario))) = Upper(RTrim(LTrim(@Usuario))) AND Upper(RTrim(LTrim(Tipo))) = 'C'

SET @strSQL = 'Insert into TabelaTemp SELECT TK.TASK_OUTLINE_NUM, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.TASK_NAME, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.TASK_OUTLINE_LEVEL, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.TASK_BASE_START, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.TASK_BASE_FINISH, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.TASK_START_DATE, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.TASK_FINISH_DATE, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.TASK_PCT_COMP, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.TASK_IS_SUMMARY, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.TASK_IS_EXTERNAL, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.PROJ_ID, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'PJ.PROJ_NAME, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.TASK_UID, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.TASK_ID, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.TASK_DUR, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'UPPER(DC.TEXT_VALUE) as CLI, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'UPPER(DF.TEXT_VALUE) as Frente, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'UPPER(DE.TEXT_VALUE) as Equipe, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'UPPER(DG.TEXT_VALUE) as Governanca, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'UPPER(RI.TEXT_VALUE) as RespInt, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'UPPER(DA.TEXT_VALUE) as  Area,'
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'UPPER(DU.TEXT_VALUE) as  Unidade, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'UPPER(DL.TEXT_VALUE) as  Login, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'UPPER(DV.TEXT_VALUE) as  Arquivo, '

SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '''' +  @Usuario + ''' as Usuario, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '''C'' as Tipo '

SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'FROM MSP_PROJECTS PJ, MSP_TASKS TK left outer join DadosCLI DC on '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.PROJ_ID = DC.PROJ_ID and TK.TASK_UID = DC.TEXT_REF_UID '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'left outer join DadosRespInt RI on '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.PROJ_ID = RI.PROJ_ID and TK.TASK_UID = RI.TEXT_REF_UID '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'left outer join DadosFrete DF on '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.PROJ_ID = DF.PROJ_ID and TK.TASK_UID = DF.TEXT_REF_UID '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'left outer join DadosEquipe DE on '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.PROJ_ID = DE.PROJ_ID and TK.TASK_UID = DE.TEXT_REF_UID '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'left outer join DadosGovernanca DG on '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.PROJ_ID = DG.PROJ_ID and TK.TASK_UID = DG.TEXT_REF_UID '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'left outer join DadosArea DA on '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.PROJ_ID = DA.PROJ_ID and TK.TASK_UID = DA.TEXT_REF_UID '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'left outer join DadosUnidade DU on '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.PROJ_ID = DU.PROJ_ID and TK.TASK_UID = DU.TEXT_REF_UID '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'left outer join DadosLogin DL on '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.PROJ_ID = DL.PROJ_ID and TK.TASK_UID = DL.TEXT_REF_UID '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'left outer join DadosArquivo DV on '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.PROJ_ID = DV.PROJ_ID and TK.TASK_UID = DV.TEXT_REF_UID '

SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'WHERE PJ.PROJ_ID = TK.PROJ_ID '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' And (TK.TASK_ID IS NOT NULL)  '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' And (TK.TASK_ID <> 0) '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' And (TK.TASK_IS_SUMMARY <> 1) '


IF @NomeCLI IS NOT NULL
BEGIN
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' And UPPER(LTrim(RTrim(DL.TEXT_VALUE))) LIKE ''%' +  UPPER(LTrim(RTrim(@NomeCLI)))+ '%'''
END

IF @Frente IS NOT NULL
BEGIN
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '  And UPPER(LTrim(RTrim(DF.TEXT_VALUE))) = ''' +  UPPER(LTrim(RTrim(@Frente)))+ ''''
END

IF @Equipe IS NOT NULL
BEGIN
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '  And UPPER(LTrim(RTrim(DE.TEXT_VALUE))) = ''' +  UPPER(LTrim(RTrim(@Equipe)))+ ''''
END

IF @Governanca IS NOT NULL
BEGIN
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '  And UPPER(LTrim(RTrim(DG.TEXT_VALUE))) = ''' +  UPPER(LTrim(RTrim(@Governanca)))+ ''''
END

IF @Unidade IS NOT NULL
BEGIN
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '  And UPPER(LTrim(RTrim(DU.TEXT_VALUE))) = ''' +  UPPER(LTrim(RTrim(@Unidade)))+ ''''
END

IF @Area IS NOT NULL
BEGIN
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '  And UPPER(LTrim(RTrim(DA.TEXT_VALUE))) = ''' +  UPPER(LTrim(RTrim(@Area)))+ ''''
END

IF @Completo IS NOT NULL
BEGIN
	IF  UPPER(LTrim(RTrim(@Completo))) = 'S'
	BEGIN
		SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '  And TK.TASK_PCT_COMP = 100'
	END
	ELSE
	BEGIN
		SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '  And TK.TASK_PCT_COMP <> 100'
	END
END

IF @DtInicio IS NOT NULL
BEGIN
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' And TK.TASK_START_DATE <= '''  + CONVERT(VARCHAR(8),@dtAtual,112) + ' 23:59:59'''
END


SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' Order By TK.PROJ_ID, TK.TASK_ID '


EXECUTE (@strSQL)
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS OFF 
GO

SET @dtAtual = GETDATE()

delete from TabelaTemp Where Upper(RTrim(LTrim(Usuario))) = Upper(RTrim(LTrim(@Usuario))) AND Upper(RTrim(LTrim(Tipo))) = 'C'
