SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '''C'' as Tipo '

SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'FROM MSP_PROJECTS PJ, MSP_TASKS TK left outer join DadosCLI DC on '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.PROJ_ID = DC.PROJ_ID and TK.TASK_UID = DC.TEXT_REF_UID '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'left outer join DadosRespInt RI on '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.PROJ_ID = RI.PROJ_ID and TK.TASK_UID = RI.TEXT_REF_UID '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'left outer join DadosFrete DF on '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.PROJ_ID = DF.PROJ_ID and TK.TASK_UID = DF.TEXT_REF_UID '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'left outer join DadosEquipe DE on '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.PROJ_ID = DE.PROJ_ID and TK.TASK_UID = DE.TEXT_REF_UID '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'left outer join DadosGovernanca DG on '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.PROJ_ID = DG.PROJ_ID and TK.TASK_UID = DG.TEXT_REF_UID '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'left outer join DadosArea DA on '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.PROJ_ID = DA.PROJ_ID and TK.TASK_UID = DA.TEXT_REF_UID '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'left outer join DadosUnidade DU on '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.PROJ_ID = DU.PROJ_ID and TK.TASK_UID = DU.TEXT_REF_UID '

SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'WHERE PJ.PROJ_ID = TK.PROJ_ID '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' And (TK.TASK_ID IS NOT NULL)  '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' And (TK.TASK_ID <> 0) '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' And (TK.TASK_IS_SUMMARY <> 1) '


IF @NomeCLI IS NOT NULL
BEGIN
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' And UPPER(LTrim(RTrim(DC.TEXT_VALUE))) = ''' +  UPPER(LTrim(RTrim(@NomeCLI)))+ ''''
END

IF @Frente IS NOT NULL
BEGIN
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '  And UPPER(LTrim(RTrim(DF.TEXT_VALUE))) = ''' +  UPPER(LTrim(RTrim(@Frente)))+ ''''
END

IF @Equipe IS NOT NULL
BEGIN
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '  And UPPER(LTrim(RTrim(DE.TEXT_VALUE))) = ''' +  UPPER(LTrim(RTrim(@Equipe)))+ ''''
END

IF @Governanca IS NOT NULL
BEGIN
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '  And UPPER(LTrim(RTrim(DG.TEXT_VALUE))) = ''' +  UPPER(LTrim(RTrim(@Governanca)))+ ''''
END

IF @Unidade IS NOT NULL
BEGIN
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '  And UPPER(LTrim(RTrim(DU.TEXT_VALUE))) = ''' +  UPPER(LTrim(RTrim(@Unidade)))+ ''''
END

IF @Area IS NOT NULL
BEGIN
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '  And UPPER(LTrim(RTrim(DA.TEXT_VALUE))) = ''' +  UPPER(LTrim(RTrim(@Area)))+ ''''
END

IF @Completo IS NOT NULL
BEGIN
	IF  UPPER(LTrim(RTrim(@Completo))) = 'S'
	BEGIN
		SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '  And TK.TASK_PCT_COMP = 100'
	END
	ELSE
	BEGIN
		SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '  And TK.TASK_PCT_COMP <> 100'
	END
END

IF @DtInicio IS NOT NULL
BEGIN
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' And TK.TASK_START_DATE <= '''  + CONVERT(VARCHAR(8),@dtAtual,112) + ' 23:59:59'''
END


SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' Order By TK.PROJ_ID, TK.TASK_ID '


EXECUTE (@strSQL)

GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

CREATE PROCEDURE SP_INCLUIR_REL_TAREFAS_DETALHE_TB_TEMP
(
@Usuario varchar(10),
@Frente varchar(255),
@Equipe varchar(255) = Null
)
AS
DECLARE @strSQL VARCHAR(2000)
DECLARE @dtAtual as datetime
SET @dtAtual = GETDATE()


delete from TabelaTemp Where Upper(RTrim(LTrim(Usuario))) = Upper(RTrim(LTrim(@Usuario))) AND Upper(RTrim(LTrim(Tipo))) = 'C'


SET @strSQL = 'Insert into TabelaTemp SELECT TK.TASK_OUTLINE_NUM, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.TASK_NAME, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.TASK_OUTLINE_LEVEL, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.TASK_BASE_START, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.TASK_BASE_FINISH, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.TASK_START_DATE, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.TASK_FINISH_DATE, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.TASK_PCT_COMP, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.TASK_IS_SUMMARY, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.TASK_IS_EXTERNAL, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.PROJ_ID, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'PJ.PROJ_NAME, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.TASK_UID, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.TASK_ID, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.TASK_DUR, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'UPPER(DC.TEXT_VALUE) as CLI, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'UPPER(DF.TEXT_VALUE) as Frente, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'UPPER(DE.TEXT_VALUE) as Equipe, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'UPPER(DG.TEXT_VALUE) as Governanca, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'UPPER(RI.TEXT_VALUE) as RespInt, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'UPPER(DA.TEXT_VALUE) as  Area,'
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'UPPER(DU.TEXT_VALUE) as  Unidade, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'UPPER(DL.TEXT_VALUE) as  Login, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'UPPER(DV.TEXT_VALUE) as  Arquivo, '

SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '''' +  @Usuario + ''' as Usuario, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '''C'' as Tipo '

SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'FROM MSP_PROJECTS PJ, MSP_TASKS TK left outer join DadosCLI DC on '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.PROJ_ID = DC.PROJ_ID and TK.TASK_UID = DC.TEXT_REF_UID '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'left outer join DadosRespInt RI on '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.PROJ_ID = RI.PROJ_ID and TK.TASK_UID = RI.TEXT_REF_UID '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'left outer join DadosFrete DF on '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.PROJ_ID = DF.PROJ_ID and TK.TASK_UID = DF.TEXT_REF_UID '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'left outer join DadosEquipe DE on '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.PROJ_ID = DE.PROJ_ID and TK.TASK_UID = DE.TEXT_REF_UID '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'left outer join DadosGovernanca DG on '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.PROJ_ID = DG.PROJ_ID and TK.TASK_UID = DG.TEXT_REF_UID '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'left outer join DadosArea DA on '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.PROJ_ID = DA.PROJ_ID and TK.TASK_UID = DA.TEXT_REF_UID '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'left outer join DadosUnidade DU on '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.PROJ_ID = DU.PROJ_ID and TK.TASK_UID = DU.TEXT_REF_UID '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'left outer join DadosLogin DL on '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.PROJ_ID = DL.PROJ_ID and TK.TASK_UID = DL.TEXT_REF_UID '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'left outer join DadosArquivo DV on '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.PROJ_ID = DV.PROJ_ID and TK.TASK_UID = DV.TEXT_REF_UID '

SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'WHERE PJ.PROJ_ID = TK.PROJ_ID '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' And (TK.TASK_ID IS NOT NULL)  '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' And (TK.TASK_ID <> 0) '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' And (TK.TASK_IS_SUMMARY <> 1) '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' And TK.TASK_PCT_COMP < 100'
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' And CONVERT(VARCHAR(8),TK.TASK_START_DATE,112) <= '''  + CONVERT(VARCHAR(8),GETDATE(),112) + ''''
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' And TK.PROJ_ID = 1 '

IF @Equipe IS NOT NULL
BEGIN
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '  And UPPER(LTrim(RTrim(DE.TEXT_VALUE))) = ''' +  UPPER(LTrim(RTrim(@Equipe)))+ ''''
END
ELSE
BEGIN

	IF @Frente = 'TI' OR @Frente = 'INT'
	BEGIN
		SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '  And UPPER(LTrim(RTrim(DE.TEXT_VALUE))) = ''' +  UPPER(LTrim(RTrim(@Frente)))+ ''''
	END
	ELSE
	BEGIN
		SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '  And UPPER(LTrim(RTrim(DE.TEXT_VALUE))) IN ' + CASE Upper(RTrim(LTrim(@Frente)))
        	 WHEN 'GM' THEN '(''GM-AO'', ''GM-CO'', ''GM-MO'', ''GM-SUP'', ''GM-TR'')'
	         WHEN 'FI' THEN '(''INT-ECCS'', ''INT-FT'', ''INT-FI'')'
        	 WHEN 'SD+P&C' THEN '(''INT-SD'', ''INT-PC'')'
	         WHEN 'PX+MES' THEN '(''INT-LT'', ''INT-MES'', ''INT-CBS'', ''INT-PM'', ''INT-PO'', ''INT-PP'' , ''INT-PS'', ''INT-QM'')'
		END
	END


END

SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' Order By TK.PROJ_ID, TK.TASK_ID '

EXECUTE (@strSQL)
/*print @strSQL*/
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS OFF 
GO




CREATE PROCEDURE SP_INCLUIR_RISKS
@Project_Code			varchar(25),	
@Sr_No			numeric(9),
@ID				varchar(10),	
@Name				text,
@Access			varchar(20),
@Area_Impacto			varchar(50),	
@Categoria			varchar(50),	
@Complexidade			varchar(20),	
@Aberto_em			datetime,
@Data_Limite			datetime,	
@Data_Fechamento		datetime,	
@Descricao			text,
@Estimativa_Horas		numeric(9),
@Fase_Gestao_Risco		varchar(	50),	
@Impacto			varchar(20),
@Mitigation			varchar(50),	
@Mitigation_Completed		varchar(1),	
@Modulo			varchar(50),
@Overall_Status		varchar(20),	
@Percent_Complete		float,
@Priority			varchar(20),	
@Probabilidade			varchar(20),	
@Ranking			varchar(20),	
@Equipe			varchar(50),
@Usuario			varchar(50),
@Workflow			varchar(50),	
@Identificador			varchar(50),
@Designador			varchar(50),
@Responsavel			varchar(50),
@Integrador			varchar(50),
@Revisor			varchar(50),
@Aprovador			varchar(50),
@Comments			text,	
@Traceability			text,	
@Activity_Log			text,	
@Comite			varchar(50),
@Tipo_Risco			varchar(50),
@Dashboard			varchar(50)

AS

declare @Nu_Access int
declare @Nu_Equipe int
declare @Nu_Comite int
declare @Nu_Usuario int

/* Access*/

select @Nu_Access = Cod_Access from Access where LTrim(RTrim(Desc_Access)) = LTrim(RTrim(@Access))

if @Nu_Access = 0 or @Nu_Access is Null  
Begin
	Insert into Access (Desc_Access) Values (LTrim(RTrim(@Access)))
	set @Nu_Access = @@IDENTITY
End

/* Equipes*/
select @Nu_Equipe = Cod_Eqp from Equipes where LTrim(RTrim(Desc_Eqp)) = LTrim(RTrim(@Equipe))

if @Nu_Equipe = 0  or @Nu_Equipe is Null  
Begin
	Insert into Equipes (Desc_Eqp) Values (LTrim(RTrim(@Equipe)))
	set @Nu_Equipe = @@IDENTITY
End

/* Comites*/
select @Nu_Comite = Cod_Comite from Comites where LTrim(RTrim(Desc_Comite)) = LTrim(RTrim(@Comite))

if @Nu_Comite = 0  or @Nu_Comite is Null  
Begin
	Insert into Comites (Desc_Comite) Values (LTrim(RTrim(@Comite)))
	set @Nu_Comite = @@IDENTITY
End


/* Usuario*/
select @Nu_Usuario = Cod_Funcionario from Funcionarios where Upper(LTrim(RTrim(Nome_Funcionario))) = Upper(LTrim(RTrim(@Usuario)))

if @Nu_Usuario = 0  or @Nu_Usuario is Null  
Begin
	Insert into Funcionarios (Nome_Funcionario) Values (Upper(LTrim(RTrim(@Usuario))))
	set @Nu_Usuario = @@IDENTITY
End


Insert into RISKS (Project_Code, Sr_No, ID, Name, Access, Area_Impacto, Categoria, Complexidade, Aberto_em, Data_Limite, Data_Fechamento, Descricao, Estimativa_Horas, Fase_Gestao_Risco, Impacto, Mitigation, Mitigation_Completed, Modulo, Overall_Status, Percent_Complete, Priority, Probabilidade, Ranking, Equipe, Usuario, Workflow, Identificador, Designador, Responsavel, Integrador, Revisor, Aprovador, Comments, Traceability, Activity_Log, Comite, Tipo_Risco, Dashboard)
Values (@Project_Code, @Sr_No, @ID, @Name, @Nu_Access, @Area_Impacto, @Categoria, @Complexidade, @Aberto_em, @Data_Limite, @Data_Fechamento, @Descricao, @Estimativa_Horas, @Fase_Gestao_Risco, @Impacto, @Mitigation, @Mitigation_Completed, @Modulo, @Overall_Status, @Percent_Complete, @Priority, @Probabilidade, @Ranking, @Nu_Equipe, @Nu_Usuario, @Workflow, @Identificador, @Designador, @Responsavel, @Integrador, @Revisor, @Aprovador, @Comments, @Traceability, @Activity_Log, @Nu_Comite, @Tipo_Risco, @Dashboard)



GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS OFF 
GO



CREATE PROCEDURE SP_INCLUIR_TASKS
@Project_Code		varchar(25),
@ID			varchar(	10),
@Name			text,
@Planned_Finish	datetime,
@Overall_Status	varchar(20)
AS
DECLARE @NU_Access int
select @NU_Access = Cod_Access from Access Where UPPER(LTrim(RTrim(Desc_Access))) = 'PUBLIC'
Insert into TASKS (Project_Code,  ID, Name, Data_Limite, Overall_Status, Access)
Values (@Project_Code, @ID, @Name, @Planned_Finish, @Overall_Status, @NU_Access)


GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF

SET ANSI_NULLS OFF

CREATE PROCEDURE SP_Incluir_Historico_Issue_KPI AS

Begin

declare @Seq as varchar

select 
	@SEQ = CASE 
		WHEN MAX(c.Seq) is null THEN 0 
		ELSE MAX(c.Seq) END + 1 
from 
	scorecard b, Issue_Historico_KPI c
group by 	
	c.data 
having
	CONVERT(VARCHAR, MAX(b.Data_Score), 112) = c.data

IF @Seq is null

	begin
	
		Insert into Issue_Historico_KPI
		select 
			a.Project_Code, 
			a.ID, 
			CONVERT(VARCHAR, MAX(b.Data_Score), 112) as Data,
			1 as SEQ,
			a.Aberto_em, 
			a.Data_Limite, 
			a.Identificador, 
			a.Designador, 
			a.Responsavel, 
			a.Revisor,
			a.Aprovador ,
			a.Workflow
		from 
			ISSUES a, scorecard b
		where 
			a.Overall_Status <> 'Closed'
		group by 	
			a.Project_Code, 
			a.ID, 
			a.Aberto_em, 
			a.Data_Limite, 
			a.Identificador, 
			a.Designador, 
			a.Responsavel, 
			a.Revisor,
			a.Aprovador ,
			a.Workflow
	
	END

Else

	begin
	
		Insert into Issue_Historico_KPI
		select 
			a.Project_Code, 
			a.ID, 
			CONVERT(VARCHAR, MAX(b.Data_Score), 112) as Data,
			SEQ = CASE 
				WHEN MAX(c.Seq) is null THEN 0 
				ELSE MAX(c.Seq) END + 1,
			a.Aberto_em, 
			a.Data_Limite, 
			a.Identificador, 
			a.Designador, 
			a.Responsavel, 
			a.Revisor,
			a.Aprovador ,
			a.Workflow
		from 
			ISSUES a, scorecard b, Issue_Historico_KPI c
		where 
			a.Project_Code *= c.Project_Code and 
			a.ID *= c.ID and 
			a.Overall_Status <> 'Closed'
		group by 	
			a.Project_Code, 
			a.ID, 
			a.Aberto_em, 
			a.Data_Limite, 
			a.Identificador, 
			a.Designador, 
			a.Responsavel, 
			a.Revisor,
			a.Aprovador ,
			a.Workflow,
			c.data 
		having

			CONVERT(VARCHAR, MAX(b.Data_Score), 112) = c.data
	
	END

END


GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS OFF 
GO




CREATE PROCEDURE SP_Incluir_Issue_KPI AS

insert into Issue_KPI
Select 
	A.PROJECT_CODE, 
	A.ID, 
	GETDATE() AS DATA, 
	Adiamento = CASE 
		WHEN A.DATA_LIMITE > B.DATA_LIMITE THEN 1 + CASE WHEN C.Adiamentos IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE C.Adiamentos END
		ELSE 0 END ,
	0 AS Rejeicao,
	Identificador1 = CASE A.WORKFLOW
		WHEN 'Identificador' THEN 1 + CASE WHEN C.Identificador IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE C.Identificador END
		ELSE C.Identificador END ,
	Designador1 = CASE A.WORKFLOW
		WHEN 'Designador' THEN 1 + CASE WHEN C.Designador IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE C.Designador END
		ELSE C.Designador END ,
	Responsavel1 = CASE A.WORKFLOW
		WHEN 'Responsável' THEN 1 + CASE WHEN C.Responsavel IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE C.Responsavel END
		ELSE C.Responsavel END ,
	Revisor1 = CASE A.WORKFLOW
		WHEN 'Revisor' THEN 1 + CASE WHEN C.Revisor IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE C.Revisor END
		ELSE C.Revisor END ,
	Aprovador1 = CASE A.WORKFLOW
		WHEN 'Aprovador' THEN 1 + CASE WHEN C.Aprovador IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE C.Aprovador END
		ELSE C.Aprovador END,
	DATEDIFF(day, A.ABERTO_EM, A.DATA_LIMITE) AS TEMPO_RESOLUCAO, 
	DATEDIFF(day, A.DATA_LIMITE, GETDATE()) AS TEMPO_ANTIGUIDADE
	
FROM
	(
	select *
	from Issue_historico_KPI
	where seq = (select max(seq) from Issue_historico_KPI where convert(varchar,data,112) = convert(varchar,getdate(),112)) and 
	convert(varchar,data,112) = convert(varchar,getdate(),112)
	) A,

	(
	select *
	from Issue_historico_KPI
	where seq = (select max(seq) from Issue_historico_KPI where convert(varchar,data,112) < convert(varchar,getdate(),112)) and 
	convert(varchar,data,112) = (select convert(varchar,max(data),112) from Issue_historico_KPI where convert(varchar,data,112) < convert(varchar,getdate(),112))
	) B,

	(
	select *
	from Issue_KPI
	where data = (select max(data) from Issue_KPI where convert(varchar,data,112) < convert(varchar,getdate(),112))
	) C


WHERE
A.PROJECT_CODE = B.PROJECT_CODE AND
A.ID = B.ID AND
A.PROJECT_CODE *= C.PROJECT_CODE AND
A.ID *= C.ID


GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS OFF 
GO





CREATE PROCEDURE SP_LISTAR_AREAS
(@Cod_Area NUMERIC(9) = NULL)
AS
DECLARE @strSQL VARCHAR(100)
SET @strSQL = 'SELECT * FROM AREAS'
IF @Cod_Area IS NOT NULL
BEGIN
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' WHERE Cod_Area = ' +  str(@Cod_Area) + ' '
END
EXECUTE (@strSQL)




GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

CREATE PROCEDURE SP_LISTAR_AREAS_GVI
AS

SELECT TEXT_VALUE as Desc_Area
	FROM DadosArea
WHERE PROJ_ID = 8 or PROJ_ID = 7
GROUP BY TEXT_VALUE
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO


CREATE PROCEDURE SP_LISTAR_ATUALIZA_DATAS_PERC_PMO_EP
(
@Codigo_Projeto_Org numeric(9) = Null,
@UID_Tarefa_Org numeric(9)  = Null,
@Codigo_Projeto_Des numeric(9)  = Null,
@UID_Tarefa_Des numeric(9)  = Null
)

AS

DECLARE @strSQL VARCHAR(1000)

SET @strSQL = 'Select AP.Cod_Proj_Orig, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'MPO.PROJ_NAME as Proj_Org, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'AP.Cod_UID_Orig, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'MTO.TASK_NAME as Tar_Org, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'AP.Cod_Proj_Dest, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'MPD.PROJ_NAME as Proj_Des, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'AP.Cod_UID_Dest, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'MTD.TASK_NAME as Tar_Des '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'From PMO.dbo.Atualiza_Datas_Perc_SMR_EP AP, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'PMO.dbo.MSP_PROJECTS MPO, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'PMO.dbo.MSP_TASKS MTO, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'EP.dbo.MSP_PROJECTS MPD, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'EP.dbo.MSP_TASKS MTD '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'Where AP.Cod_Proj_Orig = MPO.PROJ_ID And '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'AP.Cod_Proj_Orig = MTO.PROJ_ID And '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'AP.Cod_UID_Orig = MTO.TASK_UID And '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'AP.Cod_Proj_Dest = MPD.PROJ_ID And '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'AP.Cod_Proj_Dest = MTD.PROJ_ID And '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'AP.Cod_UID_Dest = MTD.TASK_UID '

IF @Codigo_Projeto_Org IS NOT NULL And @UID_Tarefa_Org IS NOT NULL And @Codigo_Projeto_Des IS NOT NULL And @UID_Tarefa_Des IS NOT NULL
BEGIN
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'And AP.Cod_Proj_Orig = ' +  str(@Codigo_Projeto_Org) + ' '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'And AP.Cod_UID_Orig = ' +  str(@UID_Tarefa_Org) + ' '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'And AP.Cod_Proj_Dest = ' +  str(@Codigo_Projeto_Des) + ' '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'And AP.Cod_UID_Dest = ' +  str(@UID_Tarefa_Des) + ' '

CREATE PROCEDURE SP_LISTAR_ATUALIZA_PERC_SMR_SMRTRAB
AS

SELECT TK.TASK_NAME,
	PJ.PROJ_NAME,
	TK.TASK_PCT_COMP, 
	TK.PROJ_ID, 
	TK.TASK_UID, 
	TK.TASK_ID
FROM MSP_TASKS TK, MSP_PROJECTS PJ
WHERE PJ.PROJ_ID = TK.PROJ_ID
	And TK.TASK_ID IS NOT NULL
	And TK.TASK_ID <> 0
	And TK.TASK_IS_SUMMARY <> 1
Order By TK.PROJ_ID, TK.TASK_ID
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'AP.Cod_UID_Orig = MTO.TASK_UID And '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'AP.Cod_Proj_Dest = MPD.PROJ_ID And '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'AP.Cod_Proj_Dest = MTD.PROJ_ID And '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'AP.Cod_UID_Dest = MTD.TASK_UID '

IF @Codigo_Projeto_Org IS NOT NULL And @UID_Tarefa_Org IS NOT NULL And @Codigo_Projeto_Des IS NOT NULL And @UID_Tarefa_Des IS NOT NULL
BEGIN
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'And AP.Cod_Proj_Orig = ' +  str(@Codigo_Projeto_Org) + ' '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'And AP.Cod_UID_Orig = ' +  str(@UID_Tarefa_Org) + ' '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'And AP.Cod_Proj_Dest = ' +  str(@Codigo_Projeto_Des) + ' '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'And AP.Cod_UID_Dest = ' +  str(@UID_Tarefa_Des) + ' '

END

EXECUTE (@strSQL)




GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

CREATE PROCEDURE SP_LISTAR_CHANG_REQUEST_ALT
(
@Usuario varchar(10)
)
AS

DECLARE @dtInicio as datetime
DECLARE @dtFim as datetime

SET @dtFim = GETDATE()
WHILE  DATEPART(dw, @dtFim) <> 3 
BEGIN
	SET @dtFim = DATEADD(d,1,@dtFim)
END

SET @dtInicio = (@dtFim - 7)

SET NOCOUNT ON

Select CA.Login,
	CA.Data,
	CA.PROJ_ID,
	CA.UID,
	CA.Acao,
	CA.Dt_Inicio_Nov,
	CA.Dt_Final_Nov,
	CA.Perc_Comp_Nov,
	MP.PROJ_NAME,
	TK.TASK_NAME as Nome
From Chang_Requests_Alt CA, MSP_PROJECTS MP, MSP_TASKS TK
Where CA.PROJ_ID = MP.PROJ_ID AND
	CA.UID = TK.TASK_UID AND
	UPPER(LTRIM(RTRIM(CA.Login))) = UPPER(LTRIM(RTRIM(@Usuario))) AND
	CONVERT(VARCHAR(8),CA.Data, 112) >= CONVERT(VARCHAR(8), @dtInicio, 112) AND
	CONVERT(VARCHAR(8),CA.Data, 112) <= CONVERT(VARCHAR(8), @dtFim, 112)
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

CREATE PROCEDURE SP_LISTAR_CHANG_REQUEST_DETALHE 
(
@Usuario varchar(10),
@Tipo varchar(1)
)
AS
DECLARE @strSQL VARCHAR(2000)

SET @strSQL = 'SELECT TK.TASK_OUTLINE_NUM, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.TASK_NAME, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.TASK_OUTLINE_LEVEL, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'CONVERT(varchar(10),TK.TASK_BASE_START,103) as TASK_BASE_START, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'CONVERT(varchar(10),TK.TASK_BASE_FINISH,103) as TASK_BASE_FINISH, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'CONVERT(varchar(10),TK.TASK_START_DATE,103) as TASK_START_DATE, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'CONVERT(varchar(10),TK.TASK_FINISH_DATE,103) as TASK_FINISH_DATE, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.TASK_PCT_COMP, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.TASK_IS_SUMMARY, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.TASK_IS_EXTERNAL, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.PROJ_ID, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.PROJ_NAME, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.TASK_UID, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.TASK_ID, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '(TK.TASK_DUR / 4800) as DURACAO, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.CLI, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.Frente, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.Equipe, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.Login, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.Arquivo, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'CONVERT(varchar(10),DT.DATE_VALUE,103) as Data_Limite, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.Governanca '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'FROM TabelaTemp TK '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'left outer join DadosDtLimite DT on '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TK.PROJ_ID = DT.PROJ_ID and TK.TASK_UID = DT.DATE_REF_UID '

SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' Where UPPER(LTrim(RTrim(TK.Usuario))) = ''' +  UPPER(LTrim(RTrim(@Usuario)))+ ''''
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' AND  UPPER(LTrim(RTrim(TK.Tipo))) = ''' +  UPPER(LTrim(RTrim(@Tipo)))+ ''''

SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' Order By TK.PROJ_ID, TK.TASK_ID '

EXECUTE (@strSQL)
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

CREATE PROCEDURE SP_LISTAR_CHANG_REQUEST_DETALHE_INC 
AS

SELECT TK.TASK_OUTLINE_NUM, 
	TK.TASK_NAME, 
	TK.TASK_OUTLINE_LEVEL, 
	CONVERT(varchar(10),TASK_BASE_START,103) as TASK_BASE_START, 
	CONVERT(varchar(10),TASK_BASE_FINISH,103) as TASK_BASE_FINISH, 
	CONVERT(varchar(10),TASK_START_DATE,103) as TASK_START_DATE, 
	CONVERT(varchar(10),TASK_FINISH_DATE,103) as TASK_FINISH_DATE, 
	TK.TASK_PCT_COMP, 
	TK.TASK_IS_SUMMARY, 
	TK.TASK_IS_EXTERNAL, 
	TK.PROJ_ID, 
	PJ.PROJ_NAME, 
	TK.TASK_UID, 
	TK.TASK_ID, 
	(TASK_DUR / 4800) as DURACAO
FROM MSP_PROJECTS PJ, MSP_TASKS TK 
WHERE PJ.PROJ_ID = TK.PROJ_ID 
	And (TK.TASK_ID IS NOT NULL)  
	And (TK.TASK_ID <> 0) 
	And (TK.PROJ_ID = 1)
ORDER BY TK.PROJ_ID, TK.TASK_ID
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

CREATE PROCEDURE SP_LISTAR_CHANG_REQUEST_SUMARIA
(
@PROJ NUMERIC(9),
@WBS VARCHAR(255)
)
as

SET NOCOUNT ON

SET @WBS = REVERSE(@WBS)

SET @WBS = SUBSTRING(@WBS, PATINDEX('%.%', @WBS) + 1, LEN(@WBS) - PATINDEX('%.%', @WBS))

SET @WBS = REVERSE(@WBS)

Select * 
From MSP_TASKS
Where LTRIM(RTRIM(TASK_OUTLINE_NUM)) = @WBS
	AND PROJ_ID = @PROJ
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS OFF 
GO



CREATE PROCEDURE SP_LISTAR_COMITE 

@Cod_Eqp Numeric(9) = NULL

AS

DECLARE @strSQL VARCHAR(2000)
SET @strSQL = 'select A.comite, B.desc_comite from issues A, comites B '
IF @Cod_Eqp IS NOT NULL
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' WHERE B.cod_comite = A.comite and Equipe = ' +  Str(@Cod_Eqp) + ' group by a.Equipe, comite, B.desc_comite  '
ELSE
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' where B.cod_comite = A.comite group by comite, B.desc_comite  ORDER BY B.desc_comite '

EXECUTE (@strSQL)

GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS OFF 
GO





CREATE PROCEDURE SP_LISTAR_COMITES
@Cod_Comite NUMERIC(9) = NULL
AS
DECLARE @strSQL VARCHAR(100)
SET @strSQL = 'SELECT * FROM Comites'
IF @Cod_Comite IS NOT NULL
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' WHERE Cod_Comite = ' +  CAST(@Cod_Comite AS VARCHAR)
ELSE
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' ORDER BY Desc_Comite'
EXECUTE (@strSQL)




GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

GO

GO

CREATE PROCEDURE SP_LISTAR_CONFIRMACAO_MEDICAO
(
@Usuario varchar(10)
)
AS

SELECT Log_Tarefas.Cod_Usuario, 
	Usuarios.Nome_Usuario AS Nome, 
	Usuarios.Flag_CLI,
	Log_Tarefas.Data_Atualizacao, 
	CONVERT(varchar(10),Log_Tarefas.Data_Atualizacao,103) as Data,
	MSP_PROJECTS.PROJ_NAME, 
	Log_Tarefas.TASK_UID,
	MSP_TASKS.TASK_NAME,
	CONVERT(varchar(10),MSP_TASKS.TASK_START_DATE,103) as TASK_START_DATE, 
	CONVERT(varchar(10),MSP_TASKS.TASK_FINISH_DATE,103) as TASK_FINISH_DATE, 
	Log_Tarefas.TASK_PCT_COMP
FROM Log_Tarefas INNER JOIN Usuarios 
	ON Log_Tarefas.Cod_Usuario = Usuarios.Login COLLATE Latin1_General_CI_AS 
	INNER JOIN MSP_PROJECTS ON Log_Tarefas.PROJ_ID = MSP_PROJECTS.PROJ_ID 
	INNER JOIN MSP_TASKS ON Log_Tarefas.TASK_UID = MSP_TASKS.TASK_UID AND Log_Tarefas.PROJ_ID = MSP_TASKS.PROJ_ID 
Where CONVERT(VARCHAR(8),Data_Atualizacao,112) = CONVERT(VARCHAR(8),getdate(),112) AND 
	UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(Log_Tarefas.Cod_Usuario))) = UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(@Usuario)))
Order By MSP_PROJECTS.PROJ_NAME, Log_Tarefas.Data_Atualizacao desc

GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS OFF 
GO


CREATE PROCEDURE SP_LISTAR_CRITICIDADE_ALTO
(
@Data Datetime,
@Cod_Eqp Numeric(9) = Null
)
AS
IF @Cod_Eqp IS NOT NULL
BEGIN
	Select Data_Score,
		Criticidade,
		Sum(Andamento) as Andamento,
		Sum(Atrasadas10) as Atrasadas10,
		Sum(AtrasadasMais10) as AtrasadasMais10
	From Scorecard
	Where Cod_Equipe = @Cod_Eqp And
		UPPER(LTrim(RTrim(Criticidade))) = 'ALTO' And
		CONVERT(VARCHAR(8),Data_Score,112) = CONVERT(VARCHAR(8),@Data,112)
	Group By Data_Score, Criticidade
END
ELSE
BEGIN
	Select Data_Score,
		Criticidade,
		Sum(Andamento) as Andamento,
		Sum(Atrasadas10) as Atrasadas10,
		Sum(AtrasadasMais10) as AtrasadasMais10
	From Scorecard
	Where 	UPPER(LTrim(RTrim(Criticidade))) = 'ALTO' And
		CONVERT(VARCHAR(8),Data_Score,112) =  CONVERT(VARCHAR(8),@Data,112)
	Group By Data_Score, Criticidade

END

GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

CREATE PROCEDURE SP_LISTAR_CRITICIDADE_NAO_DEFINIDO_RISKS

	Select Data_Score,
		Criticidade,
		Sum(Andamento) as Andamento,
		Sum(Atrasadas10) as Atrasadas10,
		Sum(AtrasadasMais10) as AtrasadasMais10
	From Scorecard_Risks
	Where Cod_Equipe = @Cod_Eqp And
		UPPER(LTrim(RTrim(Criticidade))) = '--NAO DEFINIDO--' And
		CONVERT(VARCHAR(8),Data_Score,112) = CONVERT(VARCHAR(8),@Data,112)
	Group By Data_Score, Criticidade
END
ELSE
BEGIN
	Select Data_Score,
		Criticidade,
		Sum(Andamento) as Andamento,
		Sum(Atrasadas10) as Atrasadas10,
		Sum(AtrasadasMais10) as AtrasadasMais10
	From Scorecard_Risks
	Where 	UPPER(LTrim(RTrim(Criticidade))) = '--NAO DEFINIDO--' And
		CONVERT(VARCHAR(8),Data_Score,112) = CONVERT(VARCHAR(8),@Data,112)
	Group By Data_Score, Criticidade

END


GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS OFF 
GO





CREATE PROCEDURE SP_LISTAR_DATAS_CRITICIDADE
(
@Todas varchar(1) = Null
)
AS

IF @Todas is not Null
BEGIN

	Select Distinct CONVERT(varchar(10), Data_Score,103) as Data_Score , Data_Score as Data_Score1
	from Scorecard 
	Group By Data_Score
	Order By Data_Score1 desc


END
ELSE
BEGIN

	Select Distinct CONVERT(varchar(10), max(Data_Score),103) as Data_Score
	from Scorecard 
	Order By Data_Score desc

END


GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO


CREATE PROCEDURE SP_LISTAR_DATAS_CRITICIDADE_RISKS
(
@Todas varchar(1) = Null
)
AS

IF @Todas is not Null
BEGIN

	Select Distinct CONVERT(varchar(10), Data_Score,103) as Data_Score , Data_Score as Data_Score1
	from Scorecard_Risks
	Group By Data_Score
	Order By Data_Score1 desc


END
ELSE
BEGIN

	Select Distinct CONVERT(varchar(10), max(Data_Score),103) as Data_Score
	from Scorecard_Risks
	Order By Data_Score desc

END



GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF

CREATE PROCEDURE SP_LISTAR_DESENVOLVIMENTO_TESTE_CARGA
(
@Cod_Desenv VARCHAR(50),
@Cod_Ciclo  VARCHAR(50)
)
AS

SELECT TK.*
FROM MSP_TASKS TK left outer join DadosCodDesenvolvimento DC on 
	TK.PROJ_ID = DC.PROJ_ID and TK.TASK_UID = DC.TEXT_REF_UID 
	left outer join DadosCiclo DF on 
	TK.PROJ_ID = DF.PROJ_ID and TK.TASK_UID = DF.TEXT_REF_UID 
WHERE (TK.TASK_ID IS NOT NULL)
	And (TK.TASK_ID <> 0) 
	AND TK.PROJ_ID = 1
	AND UPPER(LTRIM(RTRIM(DC.TEXT_VALUE))) = UPPER(LTRIM(RTRIM(@Cod_Desenv)))
	AND UPPER(LTRIM(RTRIM(DF.TEXT_VALUE))) = UPPER(LTRIM(RTRIM(@Cod_Ciclo)))
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS OFF 
GO

CREATE PROCEDURE SP_LISTAR_ISSUES_DETALHE 
(
@Data DateTime,
@Tipo varchar(20),
@Cod_Eqp Numeric(9) = NULL,
@Criticidade varchar(20) = NULL
)
AS

DECLARE @strSQL VARCHAR(2000)

SET @strSQL = 'Select I.*, desc_comite,  E.Desc_Eqp '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'From Issues I, Equipes E , Comites C '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'Where I.comite = C.Cod_Comite AND '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'I.Equipe = E.Cod_Eqp  '

IF UPPER(LTRIM(RTRIM(@Tipo))) = 'A'
BEGIN
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' AND I.Data_Limite >= ''' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(8), @Data ,112) + ''' AND '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '(UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(I.Overall_Status))) = ''OPEN'' OR UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(I.Overall_Status))) = ''PAST DUE'') '
END

IF UPPER(LTRIM(RTRIM(@Tipo))) = 'AT10'
BEGIN
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' AND I.Data_Limite >= ''' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(8),(DATEADD(d, -10, @Data)),112) + ''' AND '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'I.Data_Limite < ''' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(8), @Data ,112) + ''' AND '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '(UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(I.Overall_Status))) = ''OPEN'' OR UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(I.Overall_Status))) = ''PAST DUE'') '
END

IF UPPER(LTRIM(RTRIM(@Tipo))) = 'ATM10'
BEGIN
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' AND I.Data_Limite < ''' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(8),(DATEADD(d, -10, @Data)),112) + ''' AND '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '(UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(I.Overall_Status))) = ''OPEN'' OR UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(I.Overall_Status))) = ''PAST DUE'') '
END

IF UPPER(LTRIM(RTRIM(@Tipo))) = 'TODOS'
BEGIN
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' AND (UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(I.Overall_Status))) = ''OPEN'' OR UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(I.Overall_Status))) = ''PAST DUE'') '
END


IF @Cod_Eqp IS NOT NULL
BEGIN
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '  And  E.Cod_Eqp = ' + STR(@Cod_Eqp) + ' '
END

IF @Criticidade IS NOT NULL
BEGIN
	IF RTrim(LTrim(@Criticidade)) = '--Nao Definido--'
	BEGIN
		SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' And (UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(I.Criticidade_Impacto))) = ''--Nao Definido--'' OR UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(I.Criticidade_Impacto))) = ''--Não Definido--'')' 
	END
	ELSE
	BEGIN
		SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' And UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(I.Criticidade_Impacto))) = ''' + UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(@Criticidade))) + '''' 
	END
END

IF UPPER(LTRIM(RTRIM(@Tipo))) <> 'TODOS'
BEGIN

	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' Order By I.Project_Code, I.ID '
END

EXECUTE (@strSQL)
/*print @strSQL*/
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS OFF 
GO



CREATE PROCEDURE SP_LISTAR_ISSUES_DETALHE_CARTEIRA 
(
@Data DateTime,
@TpCart varchar(20),
@Cod_Eqp Numeric(9) = NULL,
@Comite varchar(20) = NULL
)
AS

DECLARE @strSQL VARCHAR(2000)


IF UPPER(LTRIM(RTRIM(@TpCart))) = 'C3'
BEGIN
	SET @strSQL = 'Select I.*, desc_comite, E.Desc_Eqp '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'From Issues I, Equipes E , Comites C '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'Where I.Equipe = E.Cod_Eqp AND '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'I.comite = C.Cod_Comite AND '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'I.Data_Limite >= ''' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(8), @Data ,112) + ''' AND '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '(UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(I.Overall_Status))) = ''OPEN'' OR UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(I.Overall_Status))) = ''PAST DUE'') AND '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '(UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(I.Criticidade_Impacto))) = ''Baixo'' OR UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(I.Criticidade_Impacto))) = ''Moderado'') '
	
	IF @Cod_Eqp IS NOT NULL
	BEGIN
		SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '  And  E.Cod_Eqp  = ' + Str(@Cod_Eqp) + ''
	END

	IF @Comite IS NOT NULL
	BEGIN
		SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '  And  UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(I.Comite))) = ''' + UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(@Comite))) + ''''
	END

	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' Union All Select I.*, desc_comite, E.Desc_Eqp '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'From Issues I, Equipes E , Comites C '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'Where I.Equipe = E.Cod_Eqp AND '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'I.comite = C.Cod_Comite AND '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'I.Data_Limite >= ''' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(8),(DATEADD(d, -10, @Data)),112) + ''' AND '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'I.Data_Limite < ''' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(8), @Data ,112) + ''' AND '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '(UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(I.Overall_Status))) = ''OPEN'' OR UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(I.Overall_Status))) = ''PAST DUE'') AND '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '(UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(I.Criticidade_Impacto))) = ''Baixo'') '

	IF @Cod_Eqp IS NOT NULL
	BEGIN
		SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '  And  E.Cod_Eqp  = ' + Str(@Cod_Eqp) + ''
	END

	IF @Comite IS NOT NULL
	BEGIN
		SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '  And  UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(I.Comite))) = ''' + UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(@Comite))) + ''''
	END


END

IF UPPER(LTRIM(RTRIM(@TpCart))) = 'C2'
BEGIN

	SET @strSQL = 'Select I.*, desc_comite, E.Desc_Eqp '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'From Issues I, Equipes E , Comites C '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'Where I.Equipe = E.Cod_Eqp AND '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'I.comite = C.Cod_Comite AND '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'I.Data_Limite < ''' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(8),(DATEADD(d, -10, @Data)),112) + ''' AND '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '(UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(I.Overall_Status))) = ''OPEN'' OR UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(I.Overall_Status))) = ''PAST DUE'') AND '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '(UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(I.Criticidade_Impacto))) = ''Baixo'' OR UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(I.Criticidade_Impacto))) = ''Moderado'') '

	IF @Cod_Eqp IS NOT NULL
	BEGIN
		SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '  And  E.Cod_Eqp  = ' + Str(@Cod_Eqp) + ''
	END

	IF @Comite IS NOT NULL
	BEGIN
		SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '  And  UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(I.Comite))) = ''' + UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(@Comite))) + ''''
	END

	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' Union All Select I.*, desc_comite, E.Desc_Eqp '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'From Issues I, Equipes E , Comites C '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'Where I.Equipe = E.Cod_Eqp AND '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'I.comite = C.Cod_Comite AND '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'I.Data_Limite >= ''' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(8),(DATEADD(d, -10, @Data)),112) + ''' AND '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'I.Data_Limite < ''' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(8), @Data ,112) + ''' AND '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '(UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(I.Overall_Status))) = ''OPEN'' OR UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(I.Overall_Status))) = ''PAST DUE'') AND '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '(UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(I.Criticidade_Impacto))) = ''Moderado'' OR UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(I.Criticidade_Impacto))) = ''Alto'') '

	IF @Cod_Eqp IS NOT NULL
	BEGIN
		SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '  And  E.Cod_Eqp  = ' + Str(@Cod_Eqp) + ''
	END

	IF @Comite IS NOT NULL
	BEGIN
		SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '  And  UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(I.Comite))) = ''' + UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(@Comite))) + ''''
	END

	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' Union All Select I.*,  desc_comite, E.Desc_Eqp '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'From Issues I, Equipes E , Comites C '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'Where I.Equipe = E.Cod_Eqp AND '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'I.comite = C.Cod_Comite AND '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'I.Data_Limite >= ''' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(8), @Data ,112) + ''' AND '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '(UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(I.Overall_Status))) = ''OPEN'' OR UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(I.Overall_Status))) = ''PAST DUE'') AND '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '(UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(I.Criticidade_Impacto))) = ''Alto'') '

	IF @Cod_Eqp IS NOT NULL
	BEGIN
		SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '  And  E.Cod_Eqp  = ' + Str(@Cod_Eqp) + ''
	END

	IF @Comite IS NOT NULL
	BEGIN
		SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '  And  UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(I.Comite))) = ''' + UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(@Comite))) + ''''
	END

END

IF UPPER(LTRIM(RTRIM(@TpCart))) = 'C1'
BEGIN
	SET @strSQL = 'Select I.*, desc_comite, E.Desc_Eqp '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'From Issues I, Equipes E , Comites C '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'Where I.Equipe = E.Cod_Eqp AND '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'I.comite = C.Cod_Comite AND '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '(UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(I.Overall_Status))) = ''OPEN'' OR UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(I.Overall_Status))) = ''PAST DUE'') AND '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '(UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(I.Criticidade_Impacto))) = ''Critico'') '

	IF @Cod_Eqp IS NOT NULL
	BEGIN
		SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '  And  E.Cod_Eqp  = ' + Str(@Cod_Eqp) + ''
	END

	IF @Comite IS NOT NULL
	BEGIN
		SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '  And  UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(I.Comite))) = ''' + UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(@Comite))) + ''''
	END

	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' Union All Select I.*, desc_comite, E.Desc_Eqp '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'From Issues I, Equipes E , Comites C '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'Where I.Equipe = E.Cod_Eqp AND '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'I.comite = C.Cod_Comite AND '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'I.Data_Limite < ''' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(8),(DATEADD(d, -10, @Data)),112) + ''' AND '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '(UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(I.Overall_Status))) = ''OPEN'' OR UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(I.Overall_Status))) = ''PAST DUE'') AND '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '(UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(I.Criticidade_Impacto))) = ''Alto'') '

	IF @Cod_Eqp IS NOT NULL
	BEGIN
		SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '  And  E.Cod_Eqp  = ' + Str(@Cod_Eqp) + ''
	END

	IF @Comite IS NOT NULL
	BEGIN
		SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '  And  UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(I.Comite))) = ''' + UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(@Comite))) + ''''
	END

END

IF UPPER(LTRIM(RTRIM(@TpCart))) = 'ND'
BEGIN
	SET @strSQL = 'Select I.*, desc_comite, desc_comite, E.Desc_Eqp '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'From Issues I, Equipes E , Comites C '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'Where I.Equipe = E.Cod_Eqp AND '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'I.comite = C.Cod_Comite AND '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '(UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(I.Overall_Status))) = ''OPEN'' OR UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(I.Overall_Status))) = ''PAST DUE'') AND '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '(UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(Criticidade_Impacto))) = ''--Nao Definido--'' OR UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(Criticidade_Impacto))) = ''--Não Definido--'') '

	IF @Cod_Eqp IS NOT NULL
	BEGIN
		SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '  And  E.Cod_Eqp  = ' + Str(@Cod_Eqp) + ''
	END

	IF @Comite IS NOT NULL
	BEGIN
		SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '  And  UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(I.Comite))) = ''' + UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(@Comite))) + ''''
	END

END

SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' Order By I.Project_Code, I.ID '


EXECUTE (@strSQL)
/*print @strSQL*/

GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS OFF

CREATE PROCEDURE SP_LISTAR_ISSUES_DETALHE_USUARIO
(
@Data DateTime,
@Tipo varchar(20),
@Usuario varchar(50) = NULL
)
AS

DECLARE @strSQL VARCHAR(2000)


SET @strSQL = 'Select I.*, desc_comite, E.Desc_Eqp, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'dbo.RetornaUsuario(I.Workflow, F.Nome_Funcionario, I.Identificador, I.Designador, I.Responsavel, I.Revisor, I.Aprovador) as Ret_Usuario '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'from Issues I, Funcionarios F, Equipes E  , Comites C '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'Where I.Equipe = E.Cod_Eqp AND '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'I.comite = C.Cod_Comite AND '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'I.Usuario = F.Cod_Funcionario And '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'I.Overall_Status <> ''Closed'' And '

IF UPPER(LTRIM(RTRIM(@Tipo))) = 'A'
BEGIN
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'I.Data_Limite >= ''' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(8), @Data ,112) + ''' '
END

IF UPPER(LTRIM(RTRIM(@Tipo))) = 'AT10'
BEGIN
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'I.Data_Limite >= ''' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(8),(DATEADD(d, -10, @Data)),112) + ''' AND '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'I.Data_Limite < ''' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(8), @Data ,112) + ''' '
END

IF UPPER(LTRIM(RTRIM(@Tipo))) = 'ATM10'
BEGIN
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'I.Data_Limite < ''' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(8),(DATEADD(d, -10, @Data)),112) + ''' '
END

IF @Usuario IS NOT NULL
BEGIN
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '  And  dbo.RetornaUsuario(I.Workflow, F.Nome_Funcionario, I.Identificador, I.Designador, I.Responsavel, I.Revisor, I.Aprovador) = ''' + UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(@Usuario))) + ''''
END

SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' Order By I.Project_Code, I.ID '

EXECUTE (@strSQL)
/*print @strSQL*/

GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS OFF 
GO




CREATE PROCEDURE SP_LISTAR_ISSUES_GVI
(
@Cod_Proj NUMERIC(9),
@Cod_UID NUMERIC(9),
@Cod_Issue NUMERIC(9) = Null
)
AS

IF @Cod_Issue IS NOT NULL
Begin

	SELECT I.* 
	FROM ISSUES_GVI I 
	WHERE I.PROJ_ID = @Cod_Proj
		AND I.UID = @Cod_UID
		AND I.Fechamento = 0
		AND I.Cod_Issue = @Cod_Issue

End
Else
Begin

	SELECT I.* 
	FROM ISSUES_GVI I 
	WHERE I.PROJ_ID = @Cod_Proj
		AND I.UID = @Cod_UID
		AND I.Fechamento = 0

End


GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS OFF 
GO





CREATE PROCEDURE SP_LISTAR_ISSUES_KPI 
AS

DECLARE @strSQL VARCHAR(2000)

SET @strSQL = 'Select I.*, P.Desc_Projeto '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'From Issue_KPI I,  Projetos_PMO P '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'Where I.Project_Code = P.Project_Code and data = (SELECT max(data) FROM Issue_KPI)'

SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' Order By I.Project_Code, I.ID '

EXECUTE (@strSQL)
/*print @strSQL*/


GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS OFF 
GO





CREATE PROCEDURE SP_LISTAR_MEDICAO_DETALHE 
(
@Usuario varchar(10),
@Tipo varchar(1)
)
AS
DECLARE @strSQL VARCHAR(2000)

SET @strSQL = 'SELECT TASK_OUTLINE_NUM, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TASK_NAME, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TASK_OUTLINE_LEVEL, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'CONVERT(varchar(10),TASK_BASE_START,103) as TASK_BASE_START, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'CONVERT(varchar(10),TASK_BASE_FINISH,103) as TASK_BASE_FINISH, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'CONVERT(varchar(10),TASK_START_DATE,103) as TASK_START_DATE, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'CONVERT(varchar(10),TASK_FINISH_DATE,103) as TASK_FINISH_DATE, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TASK_PCT_COMP, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TASK_IS_SUMMARY, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TASK_IS_EXTERNAL, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'PROJ_ID, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'PROJ_NAME, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TASK_UID, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TASK_ID, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '(TASK_DUR / 4800) as DURACAO, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'CLI, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'Frente, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'Equipe, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'Login, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'Arquivo, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'Governanca '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'FROM TabelaTemp '

SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' Where UPPER(LTrim(RTrim(Usuario))) = ''' +  UPPER(LTrim(RTrim(@Usuario)))+ ''''
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' AND  UPPER(LTrim(RTrim(Tipo))) = ''' +  UPPER(LTrim(RTrim(@Tipo)))+ ''''

SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' Order By PROJ_ID, TASK_ID '

EXECUTE (@strSQL)

GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS OFF 
GO

CREATE PROCEDURE SP_LISTAR_PAINEL_SINOPTICO_DATAS
(
@Cod_UID NUMERIC(9)
)
AS

FROM MSP_TASKS TK 
WHERE TK.PROJ_ID = 7 AND TK.TASK_UID = @Cod_UID


GO

CREATE PROCEDURE SP_LISTAR_PAINEL_SINOPTICO_PREVISTO
(
@Cod_UID NUMERIC(9)
)
AS

SELECT TK.TASK_UID, 
	ROUND(DC.NUM_VALUE,2) as VALOR
FROM MSP_TASKS TK left outer join DadosPainelSinopticoPrevisto DC on 
	TK.PROJ_ID = DC.PROJ_ID and TK.TASK_UID = DC.NUM_REF_UID 
WHERE TK.PROJ_ID = 7 AND TK.TASK_UID = @Cod_UID
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

CREATE PROCEDURE SP_LISTAR_PAN2005_DETALHE 
AS

SELECT TK.TASK_OUTLINE_NUM, 
	TK.TASK_OUTLINE_LEVEL, 
	TK.TASK_NAME, 
	CONVERT(varchar(10),TK.TASK_START_DATE,103) as TASK_START_DATE, 
	CONVERT(varchar(10),TK.TASK_FINISH_DATE,103) as TASK_FINISH_DATE, 
	TK.TASK_PCT_COMP, 
	TK.TASK_IS_SUMMARY, 
	TK.TASK_UID, 
	TK.TASK_ID, 
	(TK.TASK_DUR / 4800) as DURACAO, 
	DC.TEXT_VALUE as CRITICIDADE, 
	ROUND(DP.NUM_VALUE,2) as Planejado
FROM MSP_TASKS TK left outer join Criticidade DC on 
	TK.PROJ_ID = DC.PROJ_ID and TK.TASK_UID = DC.TEXT_REF_UID 
	left outer join DadosPlan DP on 
	TK.PROJ_ID = DP.PROJ_ID and TK.TASK_UID = DP.NUM_REF_UID 
WHERE 	TK.TASK_UID <> 0 AND
	TK.TASK_PCT_COMP <> 100 AND
	TK.PROJ_ID = 10
ORDER BY TK.TASK_ID
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS OFF 
GO



CREATE PROCEDURE SP_LISTAR_PROJETOS 
@Cod_Proj VARCHAR(25) = NULL,
@Rel VARCHAR(1) = NULL
AS
DECLARE @strSQL VARCHAR(100)
SET @strSQL = 'SELECT * FROM Projetos_PMO'

IF @Cod_Proj IS NOT NULL
BEGIN
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' WHERE Project_Code = ''' +  @Cod_Proj + ''''
END
ELSE
BEGIN
	IF @Rel IS NOT NULL
	BEGIN	
		SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' WHERE Project_Code <>''kcin001813BR_PMO'''
	END

	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' ORDER BY Prioridade_Ordenacao'

END

EXECUTE (@strSQL)
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS OFF 
GO

CREATE PROCEDURE SP_LISTAR_QUANTIDADE_TESTE_CARGA
(
@Cod_Proj NUMERIC(9),
@Cod_UID NUMERIC(9),
@Quantidade NUMERIC(9) = NULL OUT
)
AS
DECLARE @strWBS VARCHAR(50)

	set nocount on

	Select @strWBS = TASK_OUTLINE_NUM + '.'
	From MSP_TASKS
	Where PROJ_ID = @Cod_Proj AND
		TASK_UID = @Cod_UID

	Select @Quantidade = COUNT(*)
	From MSP_TASKS
	Where Upper(RTrim(LTrim(SUBSTRING(TASK_OUTLINE_NUM,1,LEN(@strWBS))))) = @strWBS
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS OFF 
GO

CREATE PROCEDURE SP_LISTAR_RELATORIO_TAREFAS_DETALHE 
(
@Usuario varchar(10),
@Tipo varchar(1)
)
AS
DECLARE @strSQL VARCHAR(2000)

SET @strSQL = 'SELECT TT.TASK_OUTLINE_NUM, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TT.TASK_NAME, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TT.TASK_OUTLINE_LEVEL, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'CONVERT(varchar(10),TT.TASK_BASE_START,103) as TASK_BASE_START, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'CONVERT(varchar(10),TT.TASK_BASE_FINISH,103) as TASK_BASE_FINISH, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'CONVERT(varchar(10),TT.TASK_START_DATE,103) as TASK_START_DATE, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'CONVERT(varchar(10),TT.TASK_FINISH_DATE,103) as TASK_FINISH_DATE, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TT.TASK_PCT_COMP, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TT.TASK_IS_SUMMARY, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TT.TASK_IS_EXTERNAL, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TT.PROJ_ID, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TT.PROJ_NAME, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TT.TASK_UID, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TT.TASK_ID, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '(TT.TASK_DUR / 4800) as DURACAO, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TT.RespInt, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TT.CLI, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TT.Frente, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TT.Equipe, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TT.Governanca, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TT.Area,'
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TT.Arquivo, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TT.Unidade, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TT.Login, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'DP.NUM_VALUE as Perc_Plan, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'DR.NUM_VALUE as Perc_Real, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'SP.NUM_VALUE as SPI, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'SV.NUM_VALUE as Perc_SV, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '(SD.DUR_VALUE / 4800) as SV_DIAS '

SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'FROM TabelaTemp TT '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'left outer join DadosPlan DP on '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TT.PROJ_ID = DP.PROJ_ID and TT.TASK_UID = DP.NUM_REF_UID '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'left outer join DadosReal DR on '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TT.PROJ_ID = DR.PROJ_ID and TT.TASK_UID = DR.NUM_REF_UID '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'left outer join DadosSPI SP on '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TT.PROJ_ID = SP.PROJ_ID and TT.TASK_UID = SP.NUM_REF_UID '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'left outer join DadosSV SV on '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TT.PROJ_ID = SV.PROJ_ID and TT.TASK_UID = SV.NUM_REF_UID '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'left outer join DadosSVDias SD on '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'TT.PROJ_ID = SD.PROJ_ID and TT.TASK_UID = SD.DUR_REF_UID '

SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' Where UPPER(LTrim(RTrim(TT.Usuario))) = ''' +  UPPER(LTrim(RTrim(@Usuario)))+ ''''
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' AND  UPPER(LTrim(RTrim(TT.Tipo))) = ''' +  UPPER(LTrim(RTrim(@Tipo)))+ ''''


SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' Order By TT.PROJ_ID, TT.TASK_ID '


EXECUTE (@strSQL)
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO


CREATE PROCEDURE SP_LISTAR_RISKS_DETALHE 
(
@Data DateTime,
@Tipo varchar(20),
@Cod_Eqp Numeric(9) = NULL,
@Criticidade varchar(20) = NULL
)
AS

DECLARE @strSQL VARCHAR(2000)

SET @strSQL = 'Select R.*, desc_comite,  E.Desc_Eqp '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'From Risks R, Equipes E , Comites C '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'Where R.comite = C.Cod_Comite AND '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'R.Equipe = E.Cod_Eqp AND '

IF UPPER(LTRIM(RTRIM(@Tipo))) = 'A'
BEGIN
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'R.Data_Limite >= ''' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(8), @Data ,112) + ''' AND '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '(UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(R.Overall_Status))) = ''OPEN'' OR UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(R.Overall_Status))) = ''PAST DUE'') '
END

IF UPPER(LTRIM(RTRIM(@Tipo))) = 'AT10'
BEGIN
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'R.Data_Limite >= ''' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(8),(DATEADD(d, -10, @Data)),112) + ''' AND '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'R.Data_Limite < ''' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(8), @Data ,112) + ''' AND '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '(UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(R.Overall_Status))) = ''OPEN'' OR UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(R.Overall_Status))) = ''PAST DUE'') '
END

IF UPPER(LTRIM(RTRIM(@Tipo))) = 'ATM10'
BEGIN
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'R.Data_Limite < ''' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(8),(DATEADD(d, -10, @Data)),112) + ''' AND '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '(UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(R.Overall_Status))) = ''OPEN'' OR UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(R.Overall_Status))) = ''PAST DUE'') '
END

IF @Cod_Eqp IS NOT NULL
BEGIN
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '  And  E.Cod_Eqp = ' + STR(@Cod_Eqp) + ' '
END

IF @Criticidade IS NOT NULL
BEGIN
	IF RTrim(LTrim(@Criticidade)) = '--Nao Definido--'
	BEGIN
		SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' And (UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(R.Impacto))) = ''--Nao Definido--'' OR UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(R.Impacto))) = ''--Não Definido--'')' 
	END
	ELSE
	BEGIN
		SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' And UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(R.Impacto))) = ''' + UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(@Criticidade))) + '''' 
	END
END

SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' Order By R.Project_Code, R.ID '

EXECUTE (@strSQL)
/*print @strSQL*/

GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO


CREATE PROCEDURE SP_LISTAR_RISKS_DETALHE_CARTEIRA
(
@Data DateTime,
@TpCart varchar(20),
@Cod_Eqp Numeric(9) = NULL,
@Comite varchar(20) = NULL
)
AS

DECLARE @strSQL VARCHAR(2000)


IF UPPER(LTRIM(RTRIM(@TpCart))) = 'C3'
BEGIN
	SET @strSQL = 'Select R.*, desc_comite, E.Desc_Eqp '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'From Risks R, Equipes E , Comites C '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'Where R.Equipe = E.Cod_Eqp AND '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'R.comite = C.Cod_Comite AND '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'R.Data_Limite >= ''' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(8), @Data ,112) + ''' AND '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '(UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(R.Overall_Status))) = ''OPEN'' OR UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(R.Overall_Status))) = ''PAST DUE'') AND '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '(UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(R.Impacto))) = ''Baixo'' OR UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(R.Impacto))) = ''Medio'') '
	
	IF @Cod_Eqp IS NOT NULL
	BEGIN
		SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '  And  E.Cod_Eqp  = ' + Str(@Cod_Eqp) + ''
	END

	IF @Comite IS NOT NULL
	BEGIN
		SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '  And  UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(R.Comite))) = ''' + UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(@Comite))) + ''''
	END

	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' Union All Select R.*, desc_comite, E.Desc_Eqp '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'From Risks R, Equipes E , Comites C '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'Where R.Equipe = E.Cod_Eqp AND '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'R.comite = C.Cod_Comite AND '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'R.Data_Limite >= ''' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(8),(DATEADD(d, -10, @Data)),112) + ''' AND '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'R.Data_Limite < ''' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(8), @Data ,112) + ''' AND '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '(UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(R.Overall_Status))) = ''OPEN'' OR UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(R.Overall_Status))) = ''PAST DUE'') AND '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '(UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(R.Impacto))) = ''Baixo'') '

	IF @Cod_Eqp IS NOT NULL
	BEGIN
		SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '  And  E.Cod_Eqp  = ' + Str(@Cod_Eqp) + ''
	END

	IF @Comite IS NOT NULL
	BEGIN
		SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '  And  UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(R.Comite))) = ''' + UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(@Comite))) + ''''
	END


END

IF UPPER(LTRIM(RTRIM(@TpCart))) = 'C2'
BEGIN

	SET @strSQL = 'Select R.*, desc_comite, E.Desc_Eqp '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'From Risks R, Equipes E , Comites C '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'Where R.Equipe = E.Cod_Eqp AND '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'R.comite = C.Cod_Comite AND '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'R.Data_Limite < ''' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(8),(DATEADD(d, -10, @Data)),112) + ''' AND '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '(UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(R.Overall_Status))) = ''OPEN'' OR UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(R.Overall_Status))) = ''PAST DUE'') AND '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '(UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(R.Impacto))) = ''Baixo'' OR UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(R.Impacto))) = ''Medio'') '

	IF @Cod_Eqp IS NOT NULL
	BEGIN
		SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '  And  E.Cod_Eqp  = ' + Str(@Cod_Eqp) + ''
	END

	IF @Comite IS NOT NULL
	BEGIN
		SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '  And  UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(R.Comite))) = ''' + UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(@Comite))) + ''''
	END

	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' Union All Select R.*, desc_comite, E.Desc_Eqp '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'From Risks R, Equipes E , Comites C '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'Where R.Equipe = E.Cod_Eqp AND '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'R.comite = C.Cod_Comite AND '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'R.Data_Limite >= ''' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(8),(DATEADD(d, -10, @Data)),112) + ''' AND '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'R.Data_Limite < ''' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(8), @Data ,112) + ''' AND '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '(UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(R.Overall_Status))) = ''OPEN'' OR UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(R.Overall_Status))) = ''PAST DUE'') AND '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '(UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(R.Impacto))) = ''Medio'' OR UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(R.Impacto))) = ''Alto'') '

	IF @Cod_Eqp IS NOT NULL
	BEGIN
		SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '  And  E.Cod_Eqp  = ' + Str(@Cod_Eqp) + ''
	END

	IF @Comite IS NOT NULL
	BEGIN
		SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '  And  UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(R.Comite))) = ''' + UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(@Comite))) + ''''
	END

	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' Union All Select R.*,  desc_comite, E.Desc_Eqp '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'From Risks R, Equipes E , Comites C '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'Where R.Equipe = E.Cod_Eqp AND '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'R.comite = C.Cod_Comite AND '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'R.Data_Limite >= ''' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(8), @Data ,112) + ''' AND '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '(UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(R.Overall_Status))) = ''OPEN'' OR UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(R.Overall_Status))) = ''PAST DUE'') AND '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '(UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(R.Impacto))) = ''Alto'') '

	IF @Cod_Eqp IS NOT NULL
	BEGIN
		SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '  And  E.Cod_Eqp  = ' + Str(@Cod_Eqp) + ''
	END

	IF @Comite IS NOT NULL
	BEGIN
		SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '  And  UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(R.Comite))) = ''' + UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(@Comite))) + ''''
	END

END

IF UPPER(LTRIM(RTRIM(@TpCart))) = 'C1'
BEGIN
	SET @strSQL = 'Select R.*, desc_comite, E.Desc_Eqp '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'From Risks R, Equipes E , Comites C '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'Where R.Equipe = E.Cod_Eqp AND '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'R.comite = C.Cod_Comite AND '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '(UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(R.Overall_Status))) = ''OPEN'' OR UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(R.Overall_Status))) = ''PAST DUE'') AND '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '(UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(R.Impacto))) = ''Critico'') '

	IF @Cod_Eqp IS NOT NULL
	BEGIN
		SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '  And  E.Cod_Eqp  = ' + Str(@Cod_Eqp) + ''
	END

	IF @Comite IS NOT NULL
	BEGIN
		SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '  And  UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(R.Comite))) = ''' + UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(@Comite))) + ''''
	END

	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' Union All Select R.*, desc_comite, E.Desc_Eqp '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'From Risks R, Equipes E , Comites C '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'Where R.Equipe = E.Cod_Eqp AND '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'R.comite = C.Cod_Comite AND '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'R.Data_Limite < ''' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(8),(DATEADD(d, -10, @Data)),112) + ''' AND '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '(UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(R.Overall_Status))) = ''OPEN'' OR UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(R.Overall_Status))) = ''PAST DUE'') AND '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '(UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(R.Impacto))) = ''Alto'') '

	IF @Cod_Eqp IS NOT NULL
	BEGIN
		SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '  And  E.Cod_Eqp  = ' + Str(@Cod_Eqp) + ''
	END

	IF @Comite IS NOT NULL
	BEGIN
		SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '  And  UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(R.Comite))) = ''' + UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(@Comite))) + ''''
	END

END

IF UPPER(LTRIM(RTRIM(@TpCart))) = 'ND'
BEGIN
	SET @strSQL = 'Select R.*, desc_comite, desc_comite, E.Desc_Eqp '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'From Risks R, Equipes E , Comites C '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'Where R.Equipe = E.Cod_Eqp AND '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'R.comite = C.Cod_Comite AND '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '(UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(R.Overall_Status))) = ''OPEN'' OR UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(R.Overall_Status))) = ''PAST DUE'') AND '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '(UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(Impacto))) = ''--Nao Definido--'' OR UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(Impacto))) = ''--Não Definido--'') '

	IF @Cod_Eqp IS NOT NULL
	BEGIN
		SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '  And  E.Cod_Eqp  = ' + Str(@Cod_Eqp) + ''
	END

	IF @Comite IS NOT NULL
	BEGIN
		SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '  And  UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(R.Comite))) = ''' + UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(@Comite))) + ''''
	END

END

SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' Order By R.Project_Code, R.ID '


EXECUTE (@strSQL)
/*print @strSQL*/


GO
