CREATE PROCEDURE SP_LISTAR_RISKS_DETALHE_USUARIO
(
@Data DateTime,
@Tipo varchar(20),
@Usuario varchar(50) = NULL
)
AS

DECLARE @strSQL VARCHAR(2000)


SET @strSQL = 'Select R.*, desc_comite, E.Desc_Eqp, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'dbo.RetornaUsuario(R.Workflow, F.Nome_Funcionario, R.Identificador, R.Designador, R.Responsavel, R.Revisor, R.Aprovador) as Ret_Usuario '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'from Risks R, Funcionarios F, Equipes E  , Comites C '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'Where R.Equipe = E.Cod_Eqp AND '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'R.comite = C.Cod_Comite AND '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'R.Usuario = F.Cod_Funcionario And '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'R.Overall_Status <> ''Closed'' And '

IF UPPER(LTRIM(RTRIM(@Tipo))) = 'A'
BEGIN
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'R.Data_Limite >= ''' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(8), @Data ,112) + ''' '
END

IF UPPER(LTRIM(RTRIM(@Tipo))) = 'AT10'
BEGIN
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'R.Data_Limite >= ''' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(8),(DATEADD(d, -10, @Data)),112) + ''' AND '
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'R.Data_Limite < ''' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(8), @Data ,112) + ''' '
END

IF UPPER(LTRIM(RTRIM(@Tipo))) = 'ATM10'
BEGIN
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'R.Data_Limite < ''' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(8),(DATEADD(d, -10, @Data)),112) + ''' '
END

IF @Usuario IS NOT NULL
BEGIN
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + '  And  dbo.RetornaUsuario(R.Workflow, F.Nome_Funcionario, R.Identificador, R.Designador, R.Responsavel, R.Revisor, R.Aprovador) = ''' + UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(@Usuario))) + ''''
END

SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' Order By R.Project_Code, R.ID '

EXECUTE (@strSQL)
/*print @strSQL*/


GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS OFF 
GO





CREATE PROCEDURE SP_LISTAR_SCORECARD
(
@Data DateTime = Null
)
AS

IF @Data is not Null
BEGIN
	Select CONVERT(varchar(10), S.Data_Score,103) as Data_Score, Data_Score as Data_Score1, 
		S.Cod_Equipe, 
		E.Desc_Eqp,
		SUM(S.Andamento) as Andamento,
		SUM(S.Atrasadas10) as Atrasadas10,
		SUM(S.AtrasadasMais10) as AtrasadasMais10
	From Scorecard S, Equipes E
	Where S.Cod_Equipe = E.Cod_Eqp AND
		CONVERT(VARCHAR(8),S.Data_Score,112)  =  CONVERT(VARCHAR(8),@Data,112) 
	Group By S.Data_Score, S.Cod_Equipe, E.Desc_Eqp
	Order By Data_Score1 desc
END
ELSE
BEGIN
	Select CONVERT(varchar(10), S.Data_Score,103) as Data_Score, Data_Score as Data_Score1, 
		S.Cod_Equipe, 
		E.Desc_Eqp,
		SUM(S.Andamento) as Andamento,
		SUM(S.Atrasadas10) as Atrasadas10,
		SUM(S.AtrasadasMais10) as AtrasadasMais10
	From Scorecard S, Equipes E
	Where S.Cod_Equipe = E.Cod_Eqp
	Group By S.Data_Score, S.Cod_Equipe, E.Desc_Eqp
	Order By Data_Score1 desc
END


GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO


CREATE PROCEDURE SP_LISTAR_SCORECARD_RISKS
(
@Data DateTime = Null
)
AS

IF @Data is not Null
BEGIN
	Select CONVERT(varchar(10), S.Data_Score,103) as Data_Score, Data_Score as Data_Score1, 
		S.Cod_Equipe, 
		E.Desc_Eqp,
		SUM(S.Andamento) as Andamento,
		SUM(S.Atrasadas10) as Atrasadas10,
		SUM(S.AtrasadasMais10) as AtrasadasMais10
	From Scorecard_Risks S, Equipes E
	Where S.Cod_Equipe = E.Cod_Eqp AND
		CONVERT(VARCHAR(8),S.Data_Score,112)  =  CONVERT(VARCHAR(8),@Data,112) 
	Group By S.Data_Score, S.Cod_Equipe, E.Desc_Eqp
	Order By Data_Score1 desc
END
ELSE
BEGIN
	Select CONVERT(varchar(10), S.Data_Score,103) as Data_Score, Data_Score as Data_Score1, 
		S.Cod_Equipe, 
		E.Desc_Eqp,
		SUM(S.Andamento) as Andamento,
		SUM(S.Atrasadas10) as Atrasadas10,
		SUM(S.AtrasadasMais10) as AtrasadasMais10
	From Scorecard_Risks S, Equipes E
	Where S.Cod_Equipe = E.Cod_Eqp
	Group By S.Data_Score, S.Cod_Equipe, E.Desc_Eqp
	Order By Data_Score1 desc
END



GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS OFF 
GO




CREATE PROCEDURE SP_LISTAR_SCORECARD_USUARIO
AS

Select dbo.RetornaUsuario(I.Workflow, F.Nome_Funcionario, I.Identificador, I.Designador, I.Responsavel, I.Revisor, I.Aprovador) as Ret_Usuario,
	sum(dbo.ScoreCardAndamentoUsuario(I.Data_Limite)) As NoPrazo,
	sum(dbo.ScoreCardAtrasadas10Usuario(I.Data_Limite)) As Atras10,
	sum(dbo.ScoreCardAtrasadasMais10Usuario(I.Data_Limite)) As AtrasM10
From Issues I, Funcionarios F
Where I.Usuario = F.Cod_Funcionario And
	I.Overall_Status <> 'Closed'
Group by dbo.RetornaUsuario(I.Workflow, F.Nome_Funcionario, I.Identificador, I.Designador, I.Responsavel, I.Revisor, I.Aprovador)
Order by AtrasM10 desc



GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS OFF 
GO




CREATE PROCEDURE SP_LISTAR_SUMARIAS 
(
@Usuario   VARCHAR(10) = Null,
@Tipo        VARCHAR(1) = Null
)
AS

IF @Usuario IS NOT NULL
BEGIN
	SELECT TK.TASK_OUTLINE_NUM, 
		TK.TASK_NAME, 
		TK.TASK_OUTLINE_LEVEL, 
		TK.TASK_BASE_START, 
		TK.TASK_BASE_FINISH, 
		TK.TASK_START_DATE, 
		TK.TASK_FINISH_DATE, 
		TK.TASK_PCT_COMP, 
		TK.TASK_IS_SUMMARY, 
		TK.TASK_IS_EXTERNAL, 
		TK.PROJ_ID, 
		PJ.PROJ_NAME, 
		TK.TASK_UID, 
		TK.TASK_ID, 
		TK.TASK_DUR, 
		UPPER(DC.TEXT_VALUE) as CLI, 
		UPPER(DF.TEXT_VALUE) as Frente, 
		UPPER(DE.TEXT_VALUE) as Equipe, 
		UPPER(DG.TEXT_VALUE) as Governanca, 
		UPPER(RI.TEXT_VALUE) as RespInt, 
		UPPER(DA.TEXT_VALUE) as  Area,
		UPPER(DU.TEXT_VALUE) as  Unidade 
	FROM MSP_PROJECTS PJ, MSP_TASKS TK left outer join DadosCLI DC on 
		TK.PROJ_ID = DC.PROJ_ID and TK.TASK_UID = DC.TEXT_REF_UID 
		left outer join DadosRespInt RI on 
		TK.PROJ_ID = RI.PROJ_ID and TK.TASK_UID = RI.TEXT_REF_UID 
		left outer join DadosFrete DF on 
		TK.PROJ_ID = DF.PROJ_ID and TK.TASK_UID = DF.TEXT_REF_UID 
		left outer join DadosEquipe DE on 
		TK.PROJ_ID = DE.PROJ_ID and TK.TASK_UID = DE.TEXT_REF_UID 
		left outer join DadosGovernanca DG on 
		TK.PROJ_ID = DG.PROJ_ID and TK.TASK_UID = DG.TEXT_REF_UID 
		left outer join DadosArea DA on 
		TK.PROJ_ID = DA.PROJ_ID and TK.TASK_UID = DA.TEXT_REF_UID 
		left outer join DadosUnidade DU on 
		TK.PROJ_ID = DU.PROJ_ID and TK.TASK_UID = DU.TEXT_REF_UID 
	WHERE PJ.PROJ_ID = TK.PROJ_ID 
 		And (TK.TASK_ID IS NOT NULL)  
		And (TK.TASK_ID <> 0) 
		And (TK.TASK_IS_SUMMARY = 1) 
		And (TK.PROJ_ID in (Select Distinct PROJ_ID from TabelaTemp Where UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(Usuario))) = UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(@Usuario))) And UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(Tipo))) = UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(@Tipo)))))
	Order By TK.PROJ_ID, TK.TASK_OUTLINE_NUM

END
ELSE
BEGIN

	SELECT TK.TASK_OUTLINE_NUM, 
		TK.TASK_NAME, 
		TK.TASK_OUTLINE_LEVEL, 
		TK.TASK_BASE_START, 
		TK.TASK_BASE_FINISH, 
		TK.TASK_START_DATE, 
		TK.TASK_FINISH_DATE, 
		TK.TASK_PCT_COMP, 
		TK.TASK_IS_SUMMARY, 
		TK.TASK_IS_EXTERNAL, 
		TK.PROJ_ID, 
		PJ.PROJ_NAME, 
		TK.TASK_UID, 
		TK.TASK_ID, 
		TK.TASK_DUR, 
		UPPER(DC.TEXT_VALUE) as CLI, 
		UPPER(DF.TEXT_VALUE) as Frente, 
		UPPER(DE.TEXT_VALUE) as Equipe, 
		UPPER(DG.TEXT_VALUE) as Governanca, 
		UPPER(RI.TEXT_VALUE) as RespInt, 
		UPPER(DA.TEXT_VALUE) as  Area,
		UPPER(DU.TEXT_VALUE) as  Unidade 
	FROM MSP_PROJECTS PJ, MSP_TASKS TK left outer join DadosCLI DC on 
		TK.PROJ_ID = DC.PROJ_ID and TK.TASK_UID = DC.TEXT_REF_UID 
		left outer join DadosRespInt RI on 
		TK.PROJ_ID = RI.PROJ_ID and TK.TASK_UID = RI.TEXT_REF_UID 
		left outer join DadosFrete DF on 
		TK.PROJ_ID = DF.PROJ_ID and TK.TASK_UID = DF.TEXT_REF_UID 
		left outer join DadosEquipe DE on 
		TK.PROJ_ID = DE.PROJ_ID and TK.TASK_UID = DE.TEXT_REF_UID 
		left outer join DadosGovernanca DG on 
		TK.PROJ_ID = DG.PROJ_ID and TK.TASK_UID = DG.TEXT_REF_UID 
		left outer join DadosArea DA on 
		TK.PROJ_ID = DA.PROJ_ID and TK.TASK_UID = DA.TEXT_REF_UID 
		left outer join DadosUnidade DU on 
		TK.PROJ_ID = DU.PROJ_ID and TK.TASK_UID = DU.TEXT_REF_UID 
	WHERE PJ.PROJ_ID = TK.PROJ_ID 
 		And (TK.TASK_ID IS NOT NULL)  
		And (TK.TASK_ID <> 0) 
		And (TK.TASK_IS_SUMMARY = 1) 
	Order By TK.PROJ_ID, TK.TASK_OUTLINE_NUM

END

GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS OFF 
GO


CREATE PROCEDURE SP_LISTAR_SUMARIAS 
(
@Usuario   VARCHAR(10) = Null,
@Tipo        VARCHAR(1) = Null
)
AS

IF @Usuario IS NOT NULL
BEGIN
	SELECT TK.TASK_OUTLINE_NUM, 
		TK.TASK_NAME, 
		TK.TASK_OUTLINE_LEVEL, 
		TK.TASK_BASE_START, 
		TK.TASK_BASE_FINISH, 
		TK.TASK_START_DATE, 
		TK.TASK_FINISH_DATE, 
		TK.TASK_PCT_COMP, 
		TK.TASK_IS_SUMMARY, 
		TK.TASK_IS_EXTERNAL, 
		TK.PROJ_ID, 
		PJ.PROJ_NAME, 
		TK.TASK_UID, 
		TK.TASK_ID, 
		TK.TASK_DUR, 
		UPPER(DC.TEXT_VALUE) as CLI, 
		UPPER(DF.TEXT_VALUE) as Frente, 
		UPPER(DE.TEXT_VALUE) as Equipe, 
		UPPER(DG.TEXT_VALUE) as Governanca, 
		UPPER(RI.TEXT_VALUE) as RespInt, 
		UPPER(DA.TEXT_VALUE) as  Area,
		UPPER(DU.TEXT_VALUE) as  Unidade 
	FROM MSP_PROJECTS PJ, MSP_TASKS TK left outer join DadosCLI DC on 
		TK.PROJ_ID = DC.PROJ_ID and TK.TASK_UID = DC.TEXT_REF_UID 
		left outer join DadosRespInt RI on 
		TK.PROJ_ID = RI.PROJ_ID and TK.TASK_UID = RI.TEXT_REF_UID 
		left outer join DadosFrete DF on 
		TK.PROJ_ID = DF.PROJ_ID and TK.TASK_UID = DF.TEXT_REF_UID 
		left outer join DadosEquipe DE on 
		TK.PROJ_ID = DE.PROJ_ID and TK.TASK_UID = DE.TEXT_REF_UID 
		left outer join DadosGovernanca DG on 
		TK.PROJ_ID = DG.PROJ_ID and TK.TASK_UID = DG.TEXT_REF_UID 
		left outer join DadosArea DA on 
		TK.PROJ_ID = DA.PROJ_ID and TK.TASK_UID = DA.TEXT_REF_UID 
		left outer join DadosUnidade DU on 
		TK.PROJ_ID = DU.PROJ_ID and TK.TASK_UID = DU.TEXT_REF_UID 
	WHERE PJ.PROJ_ID = TK.PROJ_ID 
 		And (TK.TASK_ID IS NOT NULL)  
		And (TK.TASK_ID <> 0) 
		And (TK.TASK_IS_SUMMARY = 1) 
		And (TK.PROJ_ID in (Select Distinct PROJ_ID from TabelaTemp Where UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(Usuario))) = UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(@Usuario))) And UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(Tipo))) = UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(@Tipo)))))
	Order By TK.PROJ_ID, TK.TASK_OUTLINE_NUM

END
ELSE
BEGIN

	SELECT TK.TASK_OUTLINE_NUM, 
		TK.TASK_NAME, 
		TK.TASK_OUTLINE_LEVEL, 
		TK.TASK_BASE_START, 
		TK.TASK_BASE_FINISH, 
		TK.TASK_START_DATE, 
		TK.TASK_FINISH_DATE, 
		TK.TASK_PCT_COMP, 
		TK.TASK_IS_SUMMARY, 
		TK.TASK_IS_EXTERNAL, 
		TK.PROJ_ID, 
		PJ.PROJ_NAME, 
		TK.TASK_UID, 
		TK.TASK_ID, 
		TK.TASK_DUR, 
		UPPER(DC.TEXT_VALUE) as CLI, 
		UPPER(DF.TEXT_VALUE) as Frente, 
		UPPER(DE.TEXT_VALUE) as Equipe, 
		UPPER(DG.TEXT_VALUE) as Governanca, 
		UPPER(RI.TEXT_VALUE) as RespInt, 
		UPPER(DA.TEXT_VALUE) as  Area,
		UPPER(DU.TEXT_VALUE) as  Unidade 
	FROM MSP_PROJECTS PJ, MSP_TASKS TK left outer join DadosCLI DC on 
		TK.PROJ_ID = DC.PROJ_ID and TK.TASK_UID = DC.TEXT_REF_UID 
		left outer join DadosRespInt RI on 
		TK.PROJ_ID = RI.PROJ_ID and TK.TASK_UID = RI.TEXT_REF_UID 
		left outer join DadosFrete DF on 
		TK.PROJ_ID = DF.PROJ_ID and TK.TASK_UID = DF.TEXT_REF_UID 
		left outer join DadosEquipe DE on 
		TK.PROJ_ID = DE.PROJ_ID and TK.TASK_UID = DE.TEXT_REF_UID 
		left outer join DadosGovernanca DG on 
		TK.PROJ_ID = DG.PROJ_ID and TK.TASK_UID = DG.TEXT_REF_UID 
		left outer join DadosArea DA on 
		TK.PROJ_ID = DA.PROJ_ID and TK.TASK_UID = DA.TEXT_REF_UID 
		left outer join DadosUnidade DU on 
		TK.PROJ_ID = DU.PROJ_ID and TK.TASK_UID = DU.TEXT_REF_UID 
	WHERE PJ.PROJ_ID = TK.PROJ_ID 
 		And (TK.TASK_ID IS NOT NULL)  
		And (TK.TASK_ID <> 0) 
		And (TK.TASK_IS_SUMMARY = 1) 
	Order By TK.PROJ_ID, TK.TASK_OUTLINE_NUM

END
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS OFF 
GO




CREATE PROCEDURE SP_LISTAR_TAREFAS
(
@Cod_Proj NUMERIC(9) = NULL,
@Cod_UID NUMERIC(9) = NULL
)
AS

DECLARE @strSQL VARCHAR(1000)

SET @strSQL = 'SELECT MT.*, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'CONVERT(varchar(10),MT.TASK_START_DATE,103) as DATA_INICIO, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'CONVERT(varchar(10),MT.TASK_FINISH_DATE,103) as DATA_FIM, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'MP.PROJ_NAME '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'FROM MSP_TASKS MT, MSP_PROJECTS MP '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'WHERE MT.TASK_UID > 0 AND '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'MT.PROJ_ID = MP.PROJ_ID '

IF @Cod_Proj IS NOT NULL
Begin
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' AND MT.PROJ_ID = ' +  CAST(@Cod_Proj AS VARCHAR)
	
	IF @Cod_UID IS NOT NULL
	Begin
		SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' AND MT.TASK_UID = ' +  CAST(@Cod_UID AS VARCHAR)
	End
End
ELSE
Begin
	IF @Cod_UID IS NOT NULL
	Begin
		SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' AND MT.TASK_UID = ' +  CAST(@Cod_UID AS VARCHAR)
	End

End

SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' ORDER BY MT.TASK_UID'

EXECUTE (@strSQL)
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS OFF 
GO


CREATE PROCEDURE SP_LISTAR_TAREFAS_DESENV_SIN_TIN
(
@Cod_Proj NUMERIC(9) = NULL,
@Cod_UID NUMERIC(9) = NULL
)
AS

DECLARE @strSQL VARCHAR(1000)

SET @strSQL = 'SELECT MT.*, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'MP.PROJ_NAME, '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'DA.DATE_VALUE as Finish1 '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'FROM MSP_PROJECTS MP, MSP_TASKS MT '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'left outer join DadosDtAtrazo DA on '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'MT.PROJ_ID = DA.PROJ_ID and MT.TASK_UID = DA.DATE_REF_UID '

SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'WHERE MT.TASK_UID > 0 AND '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'MT.PROJ_ID = MP.PROJ_ID '

IF @Cod_Proj IS NOT NULL
Begin
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' AND MT.PROJ_ID = ' +  CAST(@Cod_Proj AS VARCHAR)
	
	IF @Cod_UID IS NOT NULL
	Begin
		SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' AND MT.TASK_UID = ' +  CAST(@Cod_UID AS VARCHAR)
	End
End
ELSE
Begin
	IF @Cod_UID IS NOT NULL
	Begin
		SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' AND MT.TASK_UID = ' +  CAST(@Cod_UID AS VARCHAR)
	End

End

SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' ORDER BY MT.TASK_UID'

EXECUTE (@strSQL)

GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS OFF 
GO



CREATE PROCEDURE SP_LISTAR_TAREFAS_ISSUES_GVI
(
@Cod_Proj NUMERIC(9) = Null,
@Cod_UID NUMERIC(9) = Null
)

AS

IF @Cod_Proj IS NOT NULL AND @Cod_UID IS NOT NULL
BEGIN

	Select T.PROJ_ID,
		P.PROJ_NAME,
		T.TASK_UID, 
		T.TASK_NAME,
		CONVERT(varchar(10),T.TASK_START_DATE,103) as TASK_START_DATE, 
		CONVERT(varchar(10),T.TASK_FINISH_DATE,103) as TASK_FINISH_DATE, 
		T.TASK_PCT_COMP,
		UPPER(RTrim(LTrim(A.TEXT_VALUE))) as INTEGRADOR,
		UPPER(RTrim(LTrim(C.TEXT_VALUE))) as CLI
	From ISSUES_GVI I,
		MSP_PROJECTS P,
		MSP_TASKS T
		left outer join DadosIntegrador A on 
		T.PROJ_ID = A.PROJ_ID and T.TASK_UID = A.TEXT_REF_UID 
		left outer join DadosCLI C on 
		T.PROJ_ID = C.PROJ_ID and T.TASK_UID = C.TEXT_REF_UID 
	Where T.PROJ_ID = P.PROJ_ID AND
		T.PROJ_ID = I.PROJ_ID AND
		T.TASK_UID = I.UID AND
		T.PROJ_ID = @Cod_Proj AND
		T.TASK_UID =@Cod_UID AND
		I.Fechamento = 0
	Group By T.PROJ_ID, P.PROJ_NAME, T.TASK_UID, T.TASK_NAME, T.TASK_START_DATE, T.TASK_FINISH_DATE, T.TASK_PCT_COMP, A.TEXT_VALUE, C.TEXT_VALUE
	Order By T.PROJ_ID, A.TEXT_VALUE

--	Order By T.PROJ_ID, P.PROJ_NAME, T.TASK_UID, T.TASK_NAME, T.TASK_START_DATE, T.TASK_FINISH_DATE, T.TASK_PCT_COMP, A.TEXT_VALUE, C.TEXT_VALUE


END
ELSE
BEGIN

	Select T.PROJ_ID,
		P.PROJ_NAME,
		T.TASK_UID, 
		T.TASK_NAME,
		CONVERT(varchar(10),T.TASK_START_DATE,103) as TASK_START_DATE, 
		CONVERT(varchar(10),T.TASK_FINISH_DATE,103) as TASK_FINISH_DATE, 
		T.TASK_PCT_COMP,
		UPPER(RTrim(LTrim(A.TEXT_VALUE))) as INTEGRADOR,
		UPPER(RTrim(LTrim(C.TEXT_VALUE))) as CLI
	From ISSUES_GVI I,
		MSP_PROJECTS P,
		MSP_TASKS T
		left outer join DadosIntegrador A on 
		T.PROJ_ID = A.PROJ_ID and T.TASK_UID = A.TEXT_REF_UID 
		left outer join DadosCLI C on 
		T.PROJ_ID = C.PROJ_ID and T.TASK_UID = C.TEXT_REF_UID 
	Where T.PROJ_ID = P.PROJ_ID AND
		T.PROJ_ID = I.PROJ_ID AND
		T.TASK_UID = I.UID AND
		I.Fechamento = 0
	Group By T.PROJ_ID, P.PROJ_NAME, T.TASK_UID, T.TASK_NAME, T.TASK_START_DATE, T.TASK_FINISH_DATE, T.TASK_PCT_COMP, A.TEXT_VALUE, C.TEXT_VALUE
	Order By T.PROJ_ID, A.TEXT_VALUE

END


GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS OFF 
GO




CREATE PROCEDURE SP_LISTAR_TB_TEMP 
(
@Usuario Varchar (255),
@Tipo Varchar(1)
)
AS
Select * 
from TabelaTemp
Where Upper(RTrim(LTrim(Usuario))) = Upper(RTrim(LTrim(@Usuario))) AND  Upper(RTrim(LTrim(Tipo))) = @Tipo
Order By PROJ_ID, TASK_OUTLINE_NUM



GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS OFF 
GO





CREATE PROCEDURE SP_LISTAR_UNIDADE
@Unidade Numeric(9) = NULL
AS
IF @Unidade IS NULL
BEGIN
	SELECT * FROM Unidades
	ORDER BY Desc_Unidade
END
ELSE
BEGIN
	SELECT * FROM Unidades
	WHERE Cod_Unidade = @Unidade
END




GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

CREATE PROCEDURE SP_LISTAR_UNIDADE_GVI
AS

SELECT TEXT_VALUE as Desc_Unidade
	FROM DadosUnidade
WHERE PROJ_ID = 8 or PROJ_ID = 7
GROUP BY TEXT_VALUE
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS OFF 
GO





CREATE PROCEDURE SP_LISTAR_USUARIO 
@Login varchar(10) = NULL,
@Flag_CLI BIT = Null
AS

IF @Flag_CLI IS NULL
BEGIN

	IF @Login IS NULL
	BEGIN
		SELECT * FROM Usuarios
		ORDER BY Nome_Usuario
	END
	ELSE
	BEGIN
		SELECT * FROM Usuarios
		WHERE Login = LTrim(RTrim(@Login))
	END

END
ELSE
BEGIN

	IF @Login IS NULL
	BEGIN
		SELECT * FROM Usuarios
		WHERE FLAG_CLI = @Flag_CLI
		ORDER BY Nome_Usuario
	END
	ELSE
	BEGIN
		SELECT * FROM Usuarios
		WHERE Login = LTrim(RTrim(@Login)) AND
			FLAG_CLI = @Flag_CLI
	END

END

GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS OFF 
GO




CREATE PROCEDURE SP_RELATORIOS

(
@Projeto 		VARCHAR(800),
@Usuario 		VARCHAR(500),
@Tipo 			VARCHAR(500),
@Status 		VARCHAR(10),
@Prioridade 		VARCHAR(300),
@Comite 		VARCHAR(300),
@Equipe 		VARCHAR(300),
@RDO			VARCHAR(3),
@Acesso		VARCHAR(50),
@PesqAvancada	VARCHAR(5), 
@OpLogico1		VARCHAR(5),
@CampoAPesquisar	VARCHAR(20),
@InfoASerPesquisada	VARCHAR(200)
)

AS

DECLARE @Cod_Access	NUMERIC(9)
DECLARE @strCamposSQL 	VARCHAR(500)
DECLARE @strFromSQL 	VARCHAR(1000)
DECLARE @strWhereSQL 	VARCHAR(2500)
DECLARE @strOrderBySQL 	VARCHAR(100)
DECLARE @strSQL 		VARCHAR(8000)
DECLARE @DataInicial		DATETIME
DECLARE @DataFinal		DATETIME
DECLARE @Posicao1 		INT
DECLARE @Posicao2 		INT
DECLARE @Posicao3 		INT
DECLARE @Posicao4 		INT
DECLARE @Posicao5 		INT
DECLARE @Posicao6 		INT
DECLARE @NomeTabela1 	VARCHAR(20)
DECLARE @NomeTabela2 	VARCHAR(20)
DECLARE @NomeTabela3 	VARCHAR(20)
DECLARE @NomeTabela4 	VARCHAR(20)
DECLARE @NomeTabela5 	VARCHAR(20)
DECLARE @NomeTabela6 	VARCHAR(20)

Set @NomeTabela1 = ''
Set @NomeTabela2  = ''
Set @NomeTabela3  = ''
Set @NomeTabela4  = ''
Set @NomeTabela5  = ''
Set @NomeTabela6  = ''


/***************************************************
Lista de campos a serem trazidos pelo SQL
***************************************************/
SET @strCamposSQL = 'T.Project_Code, P.Desc_Projeto, F.Nome_Funcionario, T.Aberto_em, T.ID, T.Workflow, T.Data_Limite, T.Name, T.Priority, T.Data_Fechamento, E.Desc_Eqp, C.Desc_Comite, T.Overall_Status, T.Descricao, T.Comments, SUBSTRING(T.ID,1,3) AS TipoID, P.Prioridade_Ordenacao'

/**********************************************************************************************************
Apontando para a tabela correta, de acordo com o que foi selecionado na página de filtro
***********************************************************************************************************/
SET @strFromSQL = 'Funcionarios as F, Equipes as E, Comites as C, Projetos_PMO as P, '

/***********************************
Montando a clausula Order By
***********************************/
SET @strOrderBySQL = 'TipoID, P.Prioridade_Ordenacao, T.ID'

/*************************************************
Verificando quais tipos foram selecionados
*************************************************/
IF LTRIM(RTRIM(@Tipo)) <> ''
	BEGIN

	SELECT @Posicao1 = CHARINDEX(',', @Tipo)

	IF @Posicao1 > 0 
		BEGIN
		SET @NomeTabela1 = SUBSTRING(@Tipo, 2, (@Posicao1 - 3)) + ' as T '
		SET @Posicao2 = CHARINDEX(',', @Tipo, (@Posicao1 + 1))

		IF @Posicao2 > 0 
			BEGIN
			SET @NomeTabela2 = SUBSTRING(@Tipo, (@Posicao1 + 2), ((@Posicao2 - @Posicao1) - 3)) + ' as T '
			SET @Posicao3 = CHARINDEX(',', @Tipo, (@Posicao2 + 1))

			IF @Posicao3 > 0 
				BEGIN
				SET @NomeTabela3 = SUBSTRING(@Tipo, (@Posicao2 + 2), ((@Posicao3 - @Posicao2) - 3)) + ' as T '
				SET @Posicao4 = CHARINDEX(',', @Tipo, (@Posicao3 + 1))

				IF @Posicao4 > 0 
					BEGIN
					SET @NomeTabela4 = SUBSTRING(@Tipo, (@Posicao3 + 2), ((@Posicao4 - @Posicao3) - 3)) + ' as T '
					SET @Posicao5 = CHARINDEX(',', @Tipo, (@Posicao4 + 1))

					IF @Posicao5 > 0 
						BEGIN
						SET @NomeTabela5 = SUBSTRING(@Tipo, (@Posicao4 + 2), ((@Posicao5 - @Posicao4) - 3)) + ' as T '
						SET @Posicao6 = CHARINDEX(',', @Tipo, (@Posicao5 + 1))

						IF @Posicao6 > 0 
							SET @NomeTabela6 = SUBSTRING(@Tipo, (@Posicao5 + 2), ((@Posicao6 - @Posicao5) - 3)) + ' as T '
						ELSE
							SET @NomeTabela6 = SUBSTRING(@Tipo, (@Posicao5 + 2), (LEN(@Tipo) - (@Posicao5 + 2))) + ' as T '
						END

					ELSE
						SET @NomeTabela5 = SUBSTRING(@Tipo, (@Posicao4 + 2), (LEN(@Tipo) - (@Posicao4 + 2))) + ' as T '
					END
				ELSE
					SET @NomeTabela4 = SUBSTRING(@Tipo, (@Posicao3 + 2), (LEN(@Tipo) - (@Posicao3 + 2))) + ' as T '
				END
			ELSE
				SET @NomeTabela3 = SUBSTRING(@Tipo, (@Posicao2 + 2), (LEN(@Tipo) - (@Posicao2 + 2))) + ' as T '
			END
		ELSE
			SET @NomeTabela2 = SUBSTRING(@Tipo, (@Posicao1 + 2), (LEN(@Tipo) - (@Posicao1 + 2))) + ' as T '
		END
	ELSE
		SET @NomeTabela1 = SUBSTRING(@Tipo, 2, (LEN(@Tipo) - 2)) + ' as T '
	END

/*******************************************
Montando a cláusula where do SQL
********************************************/
SET @strWhereSQL = 'T.Usuario = F.Cod_Funcionario AND T.Equipe = E.Cod_Eqp AND T.Comite = C.Cod_Comite AND T.Project_Code = P.Project_Code'

IF UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(@Acesso))) <> ''
	BEGIN
	SELECT @Cod_Access = Cod_Access FROM Access WHERE UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(Desc_Access))) = UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(@Acesso)))
	IF @@ROWCOUNT > 0
		SET @strWhereSQL = @strWhereSQL + ' AND T.Access = ' + CAST(@Cod_Access AS VARCHAR)
	ELSE
		BEGIN
		SELECT @Cod_Access = Cod_Access FROM Access WHERE UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(Desc_Access))) = 'PUBLIC'
		SET @strWhereSQL = @strWhereSQL + ' AND T.Access = ' + CAST(@Cod_Access AS VARCHAR)
		END
	END

IF UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(@Projeto))) <> 'TRUE'
	BEGIN
	-- Removendo as ' da string
	SET @Projeto = REPLACE(@Projeto, "'",'')
	-- Removendo as " da string
	SET @Projeto = REPLACE(@Projeto, '"', "'")
	SET @strWhereSQL = @strWhereSQL + ' AND T.Project_Code IN (' + @Projeto + ')'
	END

IF RTRIM(LTRIM(@Usuario)) <> ''
	SET @strWhereSQL = @strWhereSQL + ' AND T.Usuario IN (' + @Usuario + ')'

IF RTRIM(LTRIM(@Prioridade)) <> ''
	BEGIN
	-- Removendo as ' da string
	SET @Prioridade = REPLACE(@Prioridade, "'",'')
	-- Removendo as " da string
	SET @Prioridade = REPLACE(@Prioridade, '"', "'")
	SET @strWhereSQL = @strWhereSQL + ' AND T.Priority IN (' + @Prioridade + ')'
	END

IF RTRIM(LTRIM(@Comite)) <> ''
	SET @strWhereSQL = @strWhereSQL + ' AND T.Comite IN (' + @Comite + ')'

IF RTRIM(LTRIM(@Equipe)) <> ''
	SET @strWhereSQL = @strWhereSQL + ' AND T.Equipe IN (' + @Equipe + ')'

IF RTRIM(LTRIM(@RDO)) <> ''
	SET @strWhereSQL = @strWhereSQL + ' AND UPPER(LTRIM(RTRIM(T.Modulo))) = ''' + UPPER(LTRIM(RTRIM(@RDO))) + ''''

-- SEMANA ATUAL
IF UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(@Status))) = 'SA'
	BEGIN
	-- Procurando em que data caiu a segunda-feira na semana atual
	SET @DataInicial = GETDATE()
	IF DATEPART(dw, @DataInicial) > 1 -- SUNDAY
		BEGIN
		WHILE  DATEPART(dw, @DataInicial) <> 2 -- MONDAY
			BEGIN
			SET @DataInicial = DATEADD(d,-1,@DataInicial)
			END
		END
	ELSE
		BEGIN
		SET @DataInicial = DATEADD(d,1,@DataInicial)
		END
	-- Procurando em que data cairá a próxima sexta-feira na mesma semana da data atual
	SET @DataFinal = @DataInicial
	WHILE  DATEPART(dw, @DataFinal) <> 6 -- FRIDAY
		BEGIN
		SET @DataFinal = DATEADD(d,1,@DataFinal)
		END
	SET @strWhereSQL = @strWhereSQL + ' AND T.Data_Limite BETWEEN ''' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(8),@DataInicial,112) + ''' AND ''' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(8),@DataFinal,112) + ''''
	SET @strWhereSQL = @strWhereSQL + ' AND (UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(T.Overall_Status))) = ''OPEN'' OR UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(T.Overall_Status))) = ''PAST DUE'')'
	END

-- PRÓXIMA SEMANA	
IF UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(@Status))) = 'PS'
	BEGIN
	-- Procurando em que data cairá a segunda-feira na próxima semana
	SET @DataInicial = GETDATE()
	SET @DataInicial = DATEADD(wk, 1, @DataInicial)
	IF DATEPART(dw, @DataInicial) > 1 -- SUNDAY
		BEGIN
		WHILE  DATEPART(dw, @DataInicial) <> 2 -- MONDAY
			BEGIN
			SET @DataInicial = DATEADD(d,-1,@DataInicial)
			END
		END
	ELSE
		BEGIN
		SET @DataInicial = DATEADD(d,1,@DataInicial)
		END
	-- Procurando em que data cairá a sexta-feira na próxima semana
	SET @DataFinal = @DataInicial
	WHILE  DATEPART(dw, @DataFinal) <> 6 -- FRIDAY
		BEGIN
		SET @DataFinal = DATEADD(d,1,@DataFinal)
		END
	SET @strWhereSQL = @strWhereSQL + ' AND T.Data_Limite BETWEEN ''' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(8),@DataInicial,112) + ''' AND ''' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(8),@DataFinal,112) + ''''
	SET @strWhereSQL = @strWhereSQL + ' AND (UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(T.Overall_Status))) = ''OPEN'' OR UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(T.Overall_Status))) = ''PAST DUE'')'
	END

-- TODOS ABERTOS
IF LTRIM(RTRIM(@Status)) = 'TA'
	SET @strWhereSQL = @strWhereSQL + ' AND (UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(T.Overall_Status))) = ''OPEN'' OR UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(T.Overall_Status))) = ''PAST DUE'')'

/*-- PRÓXIMA QUINZENA
IF UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(@Status))) = 'PQ'
	BEGIN
	-- Procurando em que data cairá a segunda-feira na próxima quinzena
	SET @DataInicial = GETDATE()
	SET @DataInicial = DATEADD(wk, 2, @DataInicial)
	IF DATEPART(dw, @DataInicial) > 1 -- SUNDAY
		BEGIN
		WHILE  DATEPART(dw, @DataInicial) <> 2 -- MONDAY
			BEGIN
			SET @DataInicial = DATEADD(d,-1,@DataInicial)
			END
		END
	ELSE
		BEGIN
		SET @DataInicial = DATEADD(d,1,@DataInicial)
		END
	-- Procurando em que data cairá a sexta-feira na próxima quizena
	SET @DataFinal = @DataInicial
	WHILE  DATEPART(dw, @DataFinal) <> 6 --FRIDAY
		BEGIN
		SET @DataFinal = DATEADD(d,1,@DataFinal)
		END
	SET @strWhereSQL = @strWhereSQL + ' AND T.Data_Limite BETWEEN ''' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(8),@DataInicial,112) + ''' AND ''' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(8),@DataFinal,112) + ''''
	END*/

-- NO PRAZO
IF UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(@Status))) = 'NP'
	BEGIN
		SET @strWhereSQL = @strWhereSQL + 'AND T.Data_Limite >= ''' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(8), GETDATE() ,112) + '''  '
		SET @strWhereSQL = @strWhereSQL + 'AND (UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(T.Overall_Status))) = ''OPEN'' OR UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(T.Overall_Status))) = ''PAST DUE'') '
	END

-- ATÉ 10 DIAS ATRASADOS
IF UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(@Status))) = 'PD-10'
	BEGIN
		SET @strWhereSQL = @strWhereSQL + 'AND T.Data_Limite >= ''' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(8),(DATEADD(d, -10, GETDATE())),112) + ''' '
		SET @strWhereSQL = @strWhereSQL + 'AND T.Data_Limite < ''' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(8), GETDATE() ,112) + ''' '
		SET @strWhereSQL = @strWhereSQL + 'AND (UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(T.Overall_Status))) = ''OPEN'' OR UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(T.Overall_Status))) = ''PAST DUE'') '
	END
	
-- MAIS DE 10 DIAS ATRASADOS
IF UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(@Status))) = 'PD+10'
	BEGIN
		SET @strWhereSQL = @strWhereSQL + 'AND T.Data_Limite < ''' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(8),(DATEADD(d, -10, GETDATE())),112) + ''' '
		SET @strWhereSQL = @strWhereSQL + 'AND (UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(T.Overall_Status))) = ''OPEN'' OR UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(T.Overall_Status))) = ''PAST DUE'') '
	END


-- PAST DUE
IF UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(@Status))) = 'PD'
	BEGIN
	-- Procurar até a data atual
	SET @strWhereSQL = @strWhereSQL + ' AND T.Data_Limite <= ''' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(8),GETDATE(),112)  + ''''
	SET @strWhereSQL = @strWhereSQL + ' AND UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(T.Overall_Status))) = ''PAST DUE'''
	END
-- FECHADOS
IF UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(@Status))) = 'CLOSED'
	BEGIN
	-- Procurar fechados
	SET @strWhereSQL = @strWhereSQL + ' AND UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(T.Overall_Status))) = ''CLOSED'''
	END

IF UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(@PesqAvancada))) = 'TRUE'
	BEGIN
	-- Monta busca avançada
	SET @strWhereSQL = @strWhereSQL + ' ' + RTRIM(LTRIM(@OpLogico1)) + ' ' + RTRIM(LTRIM(@CampoAPesquisar)) + ' LIKE ''%' + RTRIM(LTRIM(@InfoASerPesquisada)) + '%'''
	END
	
/*******************
Montando SQL
********************/
IF (LTRIM(RTRIM(@NomeTabela1)) <> '') AND (LTRIM(RTRIM(@NomeTabela2)) = '') AND (LTRIM(RTRIM(@NomeTabela3)) = '') AND 
    (LTRIM(RTRIM(@NomeTabela4)) = '') AND (LTRIM(RTRIM(@NomeTabela5)) = '') AND (LTRIM(RTRIM(@NomeTabela6)) = '')
BEGIN

	IF (LTRIM(RTRIM(@NomeTabela1)) <> 'Risks as T')
	BEGIN
		SET @strSQL = 'SELECT ' + @strCamposSQL + ' FROM ' + @strFromSQL + @NomeTabela1 + ' WHERE ' + @strWhereSQL + ' ORDER BY ' + @strOrderBySQL
	END
	ELSE
	BEGIN
		
		SET @strCamposSQL = @strCamposSQL + ', T.Modulo, T.Tipo_Risco, T.Dashboard '

		SET @strSQL = 'SELECT ' + @strCamposSQL + ' FROM ' + @strFromSQL + @NomeTabela1 + ' WHERE ' + @strWhereSQL + ' ORDER BY ' + @strOrderBySQL

	END
END	
ELSE

	BEGIN


	SET @strSQL = 'SELECT ' + @strCamposSQL + ' FROM ' + @strFromSQL + @NomeTabela1 + ' WHERE ' + @strWhereSQL
	

	IF (LTRIM(RTRIM(@NomeTabela2)) <> '') 
		BEGIN
		SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' UNION ALL '
		SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'SELECT ' + @strCamposSQL + ' FROM ' + @strFromSQL + @NomeTabela2 + ' WHERE ' + @strWhereSQL
		END
	
	IF (LTRIM(RTRIM(@NomeTabela3)) <> '') 
		BEGIN
		SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' UNION ALL '
		SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'SELECT ' + @strCamposSQL + ' FROM ' + @strFromSQL + @NomeTabela3 + ' WHERE ' + @strWhereSQL
		END

	IF (LTRIM(RTRIM(@NomeTabela4)) <> '') 
		BEGIN
		SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' UNION ALL '
		SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'SELECT ' + @strCamposSQL + ' FROM ' + @strFromSQL + @NomeTabela4 + ' WHERE ' + @strWhereSQL
		END

	IF (LTRIM(RTRIM(@NomeTabela5)) <> '') 
		BEGIN
		SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' UNION ALL '
		SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'SELECT ' + @strCamposSQL + ' FROM ' + @strFromSQL + @NomeTabela5 + ' WHERE ' + @strWhereSQL
		END

	IF (LTRIM(RTRIM(@NomeTabela6)) <> '') 
		BEGIN
		SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' UNION ALL '
		SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'SELECT ' + @strCamposSQL + ' FROM ' + @strFromSQL + @NomeTabela6 + ' WHERE ' + @strWhereSQL
		END

	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' ORDER BY ' + @strOrderBySQL

	END

/**************************************
Executa o SQL que foi montado
***************************************/
EXECUTE (@strSQL)


GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS OFF 
GO




CREATE PROCEDURE SP_RELATORIOS_COM_TIPO
(@Projeto 	VARCHAR(25),
@Usuario 	NUMERIC(9),
@Tipo 		CHAR(1),
@Status 	VARCHAR(10),
@Prioridade 	VARCHAR(10),
@Comite 	NUMERIC(9),
@QtdeDias 	VARCHAR(2),
@Acesso	VARCHAR(50))
AS
DECLARE @Cod_Access	NUMERIC(9)
DECLARE @strCamposSQL 	VARCHAR(500)
DECLARE @strFromSQL 	VARCHAR(500)
DECLARE @strWhereSQL 	VARCHAR(500)
DECLARE @strOrderBySQL 	VARCHAR(100)
DECLARE @strSQL 		VARCHAR(5000)
DECLARE @DataInicial		DATETIME
DECLARE @DataFinal		DATETIME
/***************************************************
Lista de campos a serem trazidos pelo SQL
***************************************************/
SET @strCamposSQL = 'T.Project_Code, P.Desc_Projeto, F.Nome_Funcionario, T.Aberto_em, T.ID, T.Workflow, T.Data_Limite, T.Name, T.Priority, T.Data_Fechamento, E.Desc_Eqp, C.Desc_Comite, T.Overall_Status, T.Descricao, T.Comments, SUBSTRING(T.ID,1,3) AS TipoID, P.Prioridade_Ordenacao'
/**********************************************************************************************************
Apontando para a tabela correta, de acordo com o que foi selecionado na página de filtro
***********************************************************************************************************/
SET @strFromSQL = 'Funcionarios as F, Equipes as E, Comites as C, Projetos_PMO as P, '
IF @Tipo = 'I'
	BEGIN
	--Tipo = 'Issues'
	SET @strFromSQL = @strFromSQL + 'Issues as T'
	END
ELSE
	IF @Tipo = 'R'
		BEGIN
		--Tipo = 'Riscos'
		SET SET @strFromSQL = @strFromSQL + 'Risks as T'
		END
	ELSE
		IF @Tipo = 'A'
			BEGIN
			--Tipo = 'Ações'
			SET SET @strFromSQL = @strFromSQL + 'Actions as T'
			END
		ELSE
			IF @Tipo = 'C'
				BEGIN
				--Tipo = 'Change Request'
				SET SET @strFromSQL = @strFromSQL + 'Chang_Requests as T'
				END
/*******************************************
Montando a cláusula where do SQL
********************************************/
SET @strWhereSQL = 'T.Usuario = F.Cod_Funcionario AND T.Equipe = E.Cod_Eqp AND T.Comite = C.Cod_Comite AND T.Project_Code = P.Project_Code'
IF UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(@Acesso))) <> ''
	BEGIN
	SELECT @Cod_Access = Cod_Access FROM Access WHERE UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(Desc_Access))) = UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(@Acesso)))
	IF @@ROWCOUNT > 0
		BEGIN
		IF LTRIM(RTRIM(@strWhereSQL)) <> ''
			SET @strWhereSQL = @strWhereSQL + ' AND T.Access = ' + CAST(@Cod_Access AS VARCHAR)
		END
	ELSE
		BEGIN
		SELECT @Cod_Access = Cod_Access FROM Access WHERE UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(Desc_Access))) = 'PUBLIC'
		SET @strWhereSQL = @strWhereSQL + ' AND T.Access = ' + CAST(@Cod_Access AS VARCHAR)
		END
	END
IF UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(@Projeto))) <> 'T'
	BEGIN
	IF LTRIM(RTRIM(@strWhereSQL)) <> ''
		SET @strWhereSQL = @strWhereSQL + ' AND T.Project_Code = ''' + @Projeto + ''''
	END
IF @Usuario <> 0
	BEGIN
	IF LTRIM(RTRIM(@strWhereSQL)) <> ''
		SET @strWhereSQL = @strWhereSQL + ' AND T.Usuario = ' + CAST(@Usuario AS VARCHAR)
	END
IF UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(@Status))) <> 'T'
	BEGIN
	IF LTRIM(RTRIM(@Status)) = 'TA'
		SET @strWhereSQL = @strWhereSQL + ' AND (UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(T.Overall_Status))) = ''OPEN'' OR UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(T.Overall_Status))) = ''PAST DUE'')'
	ELSE
		IF LTRIM(RTRIM(@strWhereSQL)) <> ''
			SET @strWhereSQL = @strWhereSQL + ' AND T.Overall_Status = ''' + @Status + ''''
	END
IF UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(@Prioridade))) <> 'T'
	BEGIN
	IF LTRIM(RTRIM(@strWhereSQL)) <> ''
		SET @strWhereSQL = @strWhereSQL + ' AND T.Priority = ''' + @Prioridade + ''''
	END
IF @Comite <> 0
	BEGIN
	IF LTRIM(RTRIM(@strWhereSQL)) <> ''
		SET @strWhereSQL = @strWhereSQL + ' AND T.Comite = ' + CAST(@Comite AS VARCHAR)
	END
IF UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(@QtdeDias))) <> 'T'
	BEGIN
	--SEMANA ATUAL
	IF UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(@QtdeDias))) = 'SA'
		BEGIN
		--Procurando em que data caiu a segunda-feira na semana atual
		SET @DataInicial = GETDATE()
		IF DATEPART(dw, @DataInicial) > 1 --SUNDAY
			BEGIN
			WHILE  DATEPART(dw, @DataInicial) <> 2 --MONDAY
				BEGIN
				SET @DataInicial = DATEADD(d,-1,@DataInicial)
				END
			END
		ELSE
			BEGIN
			SET @DataInicial = DATEADD(d,1,@DataInicial)
			END
		--Procurando em que data cairá a próxima sexta-feira na mesma semana da data atual
		SET @DataFinal = @DataInicial
		WHILE  DATEPART(dw, @DataFinal) <> 6 --FRIDAY
			BEGIN
			SET @DataFinal = DATEADD(d,1,@DataFinal)
			END
		IF LTRIM(RTRIM(@strWhereSQL)) <> ''
			SET @strWhereSQL = @strWhereSQL + ' AND T.Data_Limite BETWEEN ''' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(8),@DataInicial,112) + ''' AND ''' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(8),@DataFinal,112) + ''''
		END
	--PRÓXIMA SEMANA	
	IF UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(@QtdeDias))) = 'PS'
		BEGIN
		--Procurando em que data cairá a segunda-feira na próxima semana
		SET @DataInicial = GETDATE()
		SET @DataInicial = DATEADD(wk, 1, @DataInicial)
		IF DATEPART(dw, @DataInicial) > 1 --SUNDAY
			BEGIN
			WHILE  DATEPART(dw, @DataInicial) <> 2 --MONDAY
				BEGIN
				SET @DataInicial = DATEADD(d,-1,@DataInicial)
				END
			END
		ELSE
			BEGIN
			SET @DataInicial = DATEADD(d,1,@DataInicial)
			END
		--Procurando em que data cairá a sexta-feira na próxima semana
		SET @DataFinal = @DataInicial
		WHILE  DATEPART(dw, @DataFinal) <> 6 --FRIDAY
			BEGIN
			SET @DataFinal = DATEADD(d,1,@DataFinal)
			END
		IF LTRIM(RTRIM(@strWhereSQL)) <> ''
			SET @strWhereSQL = @strWhereSQL + ' AND T.Data_Limite BETWEEN ''' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(8),@DataInicial,112) + ''' AND ''' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(8),@DataFinal,112) + ''''
		END
	--PRÓXIMA QUINZENA
	IF UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(@QtdeDias))) = 'PQ'
		BEGIN
		--Procurando em que data cairá a segunda-feira na próxima quinzena
		SET @DataInicial = GETDATE()
		SET @DataInicial = DATEADD(wk, 2, @DataInicial)
		IF DATEPART(dw, @DataInicial) > 1 --SUNDAY
			BEGIN
			WHILE  DATEPART(dw, @DataInicial) <> 2 --MONDAY
				BEGIN
				SET @DataInicial = DATEADD(d,-1,@DataInicial)
				END
			END
		ELSE
			BEGIN
			SET @DataInicial = DATEADD(d,1,@DataInicial)
			END
		--Procurando em que data cairá a sexta-feira na próxima quizena
		SET @DataFinal = @DataInicial
		WHILE  DATEPART(dw, @DataFinal) <> 6 --FRIDAY
			BEGIN
			SET @DataFinal = DATEADD(d,1,@DataFinal)
			END
		IF LTRIM(RTRIM(@strWhereSQL)) <> ''
			SET @strWhereSQL = @strWhereSQL + ' AND T.Data_Limite BETWEEN ''' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(8),@DataInicial,112) + ''' AND ''' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(8),@DataFinal,112) + ''''
		END
	--PAST DUE
	IF UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(@QtdeDias))) = 'PD'
		BEGIN
		--Procurar até a data atual
		IF LTRIM(RTRIM(@strWhereSQL)) <> ''
			SET @strWhereSQL = @strWhereSQL + ' AND T.Data_Limite <= ''' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(8),GETDATE(),112) + ''''
		END
	END
/***********************************
Montando a clausula Order By
***********************************/
SET @strOrderBySQL = 'TipoID, P.Prioridade_Ordenacao, T.ID'
/*******************
Montando SQL
********************/
SET @strSQL = 'SELECT ' + @strCamposSQL + ' FROM ' + @strFromSQL + ' WHERE ' + @strWhereSQL + ' ORDER BY ' + @strOrderBySQL
/**************************************
Executa o SQL que foi montado
***************************************/
EXECUTE (@strSQL)



GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS OFF 
GO




CREATE PROCEDURE SP_RELATORIOS_SEM_TIPO
(@Projeto 	VARCHAR(25),
@Usuario 	NUMERIC(9),
@Status 	VARCHAR(10),
@Prioridade 	VARCHAR(10),
@Comite 	NUMERIC(9),
@QtdeDias 	VARCHAR(2),
@Acesso	VARCHAR(50))
AS
DECLARE @Cod_Access	NUMERIC(9)
DECLARE @strCamposSQL 	VARCHAR(500)
DECLARE @strFromSQL 	VARCHAR(500)
DECLARE @strWhereSQL 	VARCHAR(500)
DECLARE @strOrderBySQL 	VARCHAR(100)
DECLARE @strSQL 		VARCHAR(5000)
DECLARE @DataInicial 		DATETIME
DECLARE @DataFinal 		DATETIME
SET NOCOUNT ON
/***************************************************
Lista de campos a serem trazidos pelo SQL
****************************************************/
SET @strCamposSQL = 'T.Project_Code, P.Desc_Projeto, F.Nome_Funcionario, T.Aberto_em, T.ID, T.Workflow, T.Data_Limite, T.Name, T.Priority, T.Data_Fechamento, E.Desc_Eqp, C.Desc_Comite, T.Overall_Status, T.Descricao, T.Comments, SUBSTRING(T.ID,1,3) AS TipoID, P.Prioridade_Ordenacao'
/****************
Fazendo join 
*****************/
SET @strFromSQL = 'Funcionarios as F, Equipes as E, Comites as C, Projetos_PMO as P, '
/******************************************
Montando a cláusula where do SQL
******************************************/
SET @strWhereSQL = 'T.Usuario = F.Cod_Funcionario AND T.Equipe = E.Cod_Eqp AND T.Comite = C.Cod_Comite AND T.Project_Code = P.Project_Code'
IF UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(@Acesso))) <> ''
	BEGIN
	SELECT @Cod_Access = Cod_Access FROM Access WHERE UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(Desc_Access))) = + '''' + UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(@Acesso))) + ''''
	IF @@ROWCOUNT > 0
		BEGIN
		IF LTRIM(RTRIM(@strWhereSQL)) <> ''
			SET @strWhereSQL = @strWhereSQL + ' AND T.Access = ' + CAST(@Cod_Access AS VARCHAR)
		END
	ELSE
		BEGIN
		SELECT @Cod_Access = Cod_Access FROM Access WHERE UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(Desc_Access))) = 'PUBLIC'
		SET @strWhereSQL = @strWhereSQL + ' AND T.Access = ' + CAST(@Cod_Access AS VARCHAR)
		END
	END
IF UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(@Projeto))) <> 'T'
	BEGIN
	IF LTRIM(RTRIM(@strWhereSQL)) <> ''
		SET @strWhereSQL = @strWhereSQL + ' AND T.Project_Code = ''' + @Projeto + ''''
	END
IF @Usuario <> 0
	BEGIN
	IF LTRIM(RTRIM(@strWhereSQL)) <> ''
		SET @strWhereSQL = @strWhereSQL + ' AND T.Usuario = ' + CAST(@Usuario AS VARCHAR)
	END
IF UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(@Status))) <> 'T'
	BEGIN
	IF LTRIM(RTRIM(@Status)) = 'TA'
		SET @strWhereSQL = @strWhereSQL + ' AND (UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(T.Overall_Status))) = ''OPEN'' OR UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(T.Overall_Status))) = ''PAST DUE'')'
	ELSE
		IF LTRIM(RTRIM(@strWhereSQL)) <> ''
			SET @strWhereSQL = @strWhereSQL + ' AND T.Overall_Status = ''' + @Status + ''''
	END
IF UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(@Prioridade))) <> 'T'
	BEGIN
	IF LTRIM(RTRIM(@strWhereSQL)) <> ''
		SET @strWhereSQL = @strWhereSQL + ' AND T.Priority = ''' + @Prioridade + ''''
	END
IF @Comite <> 0
	BEGIN
	IF LTRIM(RTRIM(@strWhereSQL)) <> ''
		SET @strWhereSQL = @strWhereSQL + ' AND T.Comite = ' + CAST(@Comite AS VARCHAR)
	END
IF UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(@QtdeDias))) <> 'T'
	BEGIN
	--SEMANA ATUAL
	IF UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(@QtdeDias))) = 'SA'
		BEGIN
		--Procurando em que data caiu a segunda-feira na semana atual
		SET @DataInicial = GETDATE()
		IF DATEPART(dw, @DataInicial) > 1 --SUNDAY
			BEGIN
			WHILE  DATEPART(dw, @DataInicial) <> 2 --MONDAY
				BEGIN
				SET @DataInicial = DATEADD(d,-1,@DataInicial)
				END
			END
		ELSE
			BEGIN
			SET @DataInicial = DATEADD(d,1,@DataInicial)
			END
		--Procurando em que data cairá a próxima sexta-feira na mesma semana da data atual
		SET @DataFinal = @DataInicial
		WHILE  DATEPART(dw, @DataFinal) <> 6 --FRIDAY
			BEGIN
			SET @DataFinal = DATEADD(d,1,@DataFinal)
			END
		IF LTRIM(RTRIM(@strWhereSQL)) <> ''
			SET @strWhereSQL = @strWhereSQL + ' AND T.Data_Limite BETWEEN ''' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(8),@DataInicial,112) + ''' AND ''' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(8),@DataFinal,112) + ''''
		END
	--PRÓXIMA SEMANA	
	IF UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(@QtdeDias))) = 'PS'
		BEGIN
		--Procurando em que data cairá a segunda-feira na próxima semana
		SET @DataInicial = GETDATE()
		SET @DataInicial = DATEADD(wk, 1, @DataInicial)
		IF DATEPART(dw, @DataInicial) > 1 --SUNDAY
			BEGIN
			WHILE  DATEPART(dw, @DataInicial) <> 2 --MONDAY
				BEGIN
				SET @DataInicial = DATEADD(d,-1,@DataInicial)
				END
			END
		ELSE
			BEGIN
			SET @DataInicial = DATEADD(d,1,@DataInicial)
			END
		--Procurando em que data cairá a sexta-feira na próxima semana
		SET @DataFinal = @DataInicial
		WHILE  DATEPART(dw, @DataFinal) <> 6 --FRIDAY
			BEGIN
			SET @DataFinal = DATEADD(d,1,@DataFinal)
			END
		IF LTRIM(RTRIM(@strWhereSQL)) <> ''
			SET @strWhereSQL = @strWhereSQL + ' AND T.Data_Limite BETWEEN ''' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(8),@DataInicial,112) + ''' AND ''' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(8),@DataFinal,112) + ''''
		END
	--PRÓXIMA QUINZENA
	IF UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(@QtdeDias))) = 'PQ'
		BEGIN
		--Procurando em que data cairá a segunda-feira na próxima quinzena
		SET @DataInicial = GETDATE()
		SET @DataInicial = DATEADD(wk, 2, @DataInicial)
		IF DATEPART(dw, @DataInicial) > 1 --SUNDAY
			BEGIN
			WHILE  DATEPART(dw, @DataInicial) <> 2 --MONDAY
				BEGIN
				SET @DataInicial = DATEADD(d,-1,@DataInicial)
				END
			END
		ELSE
			BEGIN
			SET @DataInicial = DATEADD(d,1,@DataInicial)
			END
		--Procurando em que data cairá a sexta-feira na próxima quizena
		SET @DataFinal = @DataInicial
		WHILE  DATEPART(dw, @DataFinal) <> 6 --FRIDAY
			BEGIN
			SET @DataFinal = DATEADD(d,1,@DataFinal)
			END
		IF LTRIM(RTRIM(@strWhereSQL)) <> ''
			SET @strWhereSQL = @strWhereSQL + ' AND T.Data_Limite BETWEEN ''' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(8),@DataInicial,112) + ''' AND ''' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(8),@DataFinal,112) + ''''
		END
	--PAST DUE
	IF UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(@QtdeDias))) = 'PD'
		BEGIN
		--Procurar até a data atual
		IF LTRIM(RTRIM(@strWhereSQL)) <> ''
			SET @strWhereSQL = @strWhereSQL + ' AND T.Data_Limite <= ''' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(8),GETDATE(),112) + ''''
		END
	END
/***********************************
Montando a clausula Order By
***********************************/
SET @strOrderBySQL = 'TipoID, P.Prioridade_Ordenacao, T.ID'
/*******************
Montando SQL
********************/
SET @strSQL = 'SELECT ' + @strCamposSQL + ' FROM ' + @strFromSQL + 'Issues as T WHERE ' + @strWhereSQL
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' UNION ALL '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'SELECT ' + @strCamposSQL + ' FROM ' + @strFromSQL + 'Risks as T WHERE ' + @strWhereSQL
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' UNION ALL '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'SELECT ' + @strCamposSQL + ' FROM ' + @strFromSQL + 'Actions as T WHERE ' + @strWhereSQL
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' UNION ALL '
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + 'SELECT ' + @strCamposSQL + ' FROM ' + @strFromSQL + 'Chang_Requests as T WHERE ' + @strWhereSQL
SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' ORDER BY ' + @strOrderBySQL
/**************************************
Executa o SQL que foi montado
**************************************/
EXECUTE (@strSQL)



GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS OFF 
GO




CREATE PROCEDURE SP_RELATORIOS_TRACEABILITY 
(
@Codigo_Projeto 	VARCHAR(25),
@ID 			VARCHAR(10),
@Acesso		VARCHAR(50)
)
AS
DECLARE @strSQL 		VARCHAR(1000)
DECLARE @Proj_Destino 	VARCHAR(25)
DECLARE @ID_Destino 	VARCHAR(10)
DECLARE @Cod_Access	NUMERIC(9)
/******************************************************************************************
Desabilitando envio de mensagens após a execução de cada comando SQL
******************************************************************************************/
SET NOCOUNT ON
/****************************************************************
Criando uma tabela temporária onde serão armazenados os registros
que serão retornados por esta Stored Procedure
****************************************************************/
CREATE TABLE #TabelaTemp (Desc_Projeto VARCHAR(50), ID VARCHAR(10), Nome TEXT, Status VARCHAR(20), Data_Limite DATETIME, Nome_Funcionario VARCHAR(50))
/************************************************************************
Montando um cursor para buscar informações de Traceability
************************************************************************/
DECLARE Traceability_Cursor CURSOR FOR
SELECT 
	Cod_Proj_Destino,
	Id_Destino
FROM
	Traceability
WHERE
	Cod_Proj_Origem = @Codigo_Projeto AND
	Id_Origem = @ID
/*****************
Abrindo cursor
*****************/
OPEN Traceability_Cursor
FETCH NEXT FROM Traceability_Cursor
INTO @Proj_Destino, @ID_Destino
WHILE @@FETCH_STATUS = 0
BEGIN
	/*************************************************************************
	Procurando o código de acesso referente a descrição passada
	*************************************************************************/
	IF UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(@Acesso))) <> ''
	BEGIN
		SELECT @Cod_Access = Cod_Access FROM Access WHERE UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(Desc_Access))) = UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(@Acesso)))
		IF @@ROWCOUNT = 0
		BEGIN
			SELECT @Cod_Access = Cod_Access FROM Access WHERE UPPER(RTRIM(LTRIM(Desc_Access))) = 'PUBLIC'
		END
	END
	/***************************************************************************
	Selecionando dados que serão mostrados na lista de Traceability
	***************************************************************************/
	SET @strSQL = 'SELECT B.Desc_Projeto, A.ID, A.Name AS Nome, A.Overall_Status as Status, A.Data_Limite, C.Nome_Funcionario'
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' FROM ' + dbo.DESCRICAO_TIPO(SUBSTRING(@ID_Destino,1,3)) + ' as A,'
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' Projetos_PMO as B,'
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' Funcionarios as C'
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' WHERE A.Project_Code = B.Project_Code'
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' AND A.Usuario = C.Cod_Funcionario'
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' AND A.Access = ' + CAST(@Cod_Access AS VARCHAR)
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' AND A.Project_Code = ''' + @Proj_Destino + ''''
	SET @strSQL = @strSQL + ' AND A.ID = ''' + @ID_Destino + ''''
	/****************************************************
	Verificando se algum registro foi selecionado
	****************************************************/
	EXECUTE(@strSQL)
	IF @@ROWCOUNT > 0
	BEGIN
		/*********************************************************
		Transferindo os dados recuperados pelo SQL acima para uma
		tabela temporária
		*********************************************************/
		EXECUTE ('INSERT INTO #TabelaTemp ' + @strSQL)
	END
	
    	FETCH NEXT FROM Traceability_Cursor
	INTO @Proj_Destino, @ID_Destino
END
/*******************
Fechando cursor
*******************/
CLOSE Traceability_Cursor
DEALLOCATE Traceability_Cursor
/*********************************************************
Selecionando todos os dados incluídos na tabela temporária
*********************************************************/
SELECT * FROM #TabelaTemp



GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS OFF 
GO





CREATE PROCEDURE SP_SELECIONAR_STATUS_REPORT

(
@Comite	VARCHAR(50),
@Prioridade	VARCHAR(20),
@NomeTabela	VARCHAR(20),
@NomeProjeto	VARCHAR(50) = NULL
)

AS

DECLARE @strSQL VARCHAR(2000)

/******************************************************************************************
Desabilitando envio de mensagens após a execução de cada comando SQL
******************************************************************************************/
SET NOCOUNT ON

/****************************************************************************
Selecionando Registros de acordo com as informações passadas
****************************************************************************/
SET @strSQL = 'SELECT '
SET @strSQL =  @strSQL + '	T.Project_Code, P.Desc_Projeto, F.Nome_Funcionario, T.Aberto_em, T.ID, T.Workflow, T.Data_Limite, T.Name, T.Priority, T.Data_Fechamento, E.Desc_Eqp, C.Desc_Comite, T.Overall_Status, T.Descricao, T.Comments, P.Prioridade_Ordenacao'
SET @strSQL =  @strSQL + ' FROM '
SET @strSQL =  @strSQL + '	Funcionarios as F, Equipes as E, Comites as C, Projetos_PMO as P, ' + UPPER(LTRIM(RTRIM(@NomeTabela))) + ' as T'
SET @strSQL =  @strSQL + ' WHERE '
SET @strSQL =  @strSQL + '	T.Usuario = F.Cod_Funcionario AND '
SET @strSQL =  @strSQL + '	T.Equipe = E.Cod_Eqp AND '
SET @strSQL =  @strSQL + '	T.Comite = C.Cod_Comite AND '
SET @strSQL =  @strSQL + '	T.Project_Code = P.Project_Code '
IF LTRIM(RTRIM(@Prioridade)) <> ''
	SET @strSQL =  @strSQL + '	AND UPPER(LTRIM(RTRIM(T.Priority))) = ''' + UPPER(LTRIM(RTRIM(@Prioridade))) + ''''
ELSE
	SET @strSQL =  @strSQL + '	AND UPPER(LTRIM(RTRIM(T.Priority))) <> ''CRITICAL'''
SET @strSQL =  @strSQL + '	AND UPPER(LTRIM(RTRIM(C.Desc_Comite))) = ''' + UPPER(LTRIM(RTRIM(@Comite))) + ''''
SET @strSQL =  @strSQL + '	AND UPPER(LTRIM(RTRIM(T.Overall_Status))) <> ''CLOSED'''
IF @NomeProjeto IS NOT NULL
	SET @strSQL =  @strSQL + '	AND UPPER(LTRIM(RTRIM(P.Desc_Projeto))) = ''' + UPPER(LTRIM(RTRIM(@NomeProjeto))) + ''''
ELSE
	SET @strSQL =  @strSQL + '	AND UPPER(LTRIM(RTRIM(P.Desc_Projeto))) <> ''SEA'' AND UPPER(LTRIM(RTRIM(P.Desc_Projeto))) <> ''RH'''
SET @strSQL =  @strSQL + ' ORDER BY'
IF @NomeProjeto IS NULL
	SET @strSQL =  @strSQL + '	P.Prioridade_Ordenacao, T.ID'
ELSE
	SET @strSQL =  @strSQL + '	T.ID'

EXECUTE (@strSQL)




GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS OFF 
GO





CREATE PROCEDURE SP_VERIFICAR_ORDENACAO_PROJETO_EXISTE
(
@Ordenacao		SMALLINT
)

AS

SELECT
	Project_Code
FROM
	Projetos_PMO
WHERE
	Prioridade_Ordenacao = @Ordenacao




GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER OFF 
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON 
GO
